    "W": (POWER, _F(1), True), "hp": (POWER, _F("745.69987158227022"), False),
    # volume / speed / concentration
    "L": (VOLUME, _F("0.001"), True), "gal": (VOLUME, _F("0.003785411784"), False),
    "mph": (SPEED, _F("0.44704"), False),
    "M": (CONCENTRATION, _F(1000), True),
    # angle & ratios (dimensionless, but with a scale)
    "rad": (DIMENSIONLESS, _F(1), True), "deg": (DIMENSIONLESS, Fraction(math.pi) / 180, False),
    "%": (DIMENSIONLESS, _F("0.01"), False),
}
_PREFIXES: dict[str, Fraction] = {
    "T": _F(10) ** 12, "G": _F(10) ** 9, "M": _F(10) ** 6, "k": _F(10) ** 3, "h": _F(10) ** 2,
    "d": _F(1, 10), "c": _F(1, 100), "m": _F(1, 1000), "µ": _F(1, 10 ** 6), "μ": _F(1, 10 ** 6),
    "u": _F(1, 10 ** 6), "n": _F(1, 10 ** 9), "p": _F(1, 10 ** 12), "f": _F(1, 10 ** 15),
}
_PREFIX_WORDS = {"tera": "T", "giga": "G", "mega": "M", "kilo": "k", "hecto": "h", "deci": "d", "centi": "c",
                 "milli": "m", "micro": "µ", "nano": "n", "pico": "p", "femto": "f"}
_AFFINE = {
    "°C": Unit(TEMPERATURE, _F(1), _F("273.15"), "°C"),
    "°F": Unit(TEMPERATURE, _F(5, 9), _F("459.67") * 5 / 9, "°F"),
}
# words and legacy spellings (matched case-insensitively)
_WORDS: dict[str, str] = {
    "meter": "m", "meters": "m", "metre": "m", "metres": "m", "gram": "g", "grams": "g",
    "second": "s", "seconds": "s", "sec": "s", "secs": "s", "minute": "min", "minutes": "min",
    "hour": "h", "hours": "h", "day": "d", "days": "d", "ampere": "A", "amperes": "A", "amp": "A", "amps": "A",
    "kelvin": "K", "mole": "mol", "moles": "mol", "newton": "N", "newtons": "N", "pascal": "Pa", "pascals": "Pa",
    "joule": "J", "joules": "J", "watt": "W", "watts": "W", "volt": "V", "volts": "V", "ohms": "ohm",
    "hertz": "Hz", "liter": "L", "liters": "L", "litre": "L", "litres": "L", "radian": "rad", "radians": "rad",
    "degree": "deg", "degrees": "deg", "inch": "in", "inches": "in", "foot": "ft", "feet": "ft", "mile": "mi",
    "miles": "mi", "pound": "lb", "pounds": "lb", "lbs": "lb", "ounce": "oz", "ounces": "oz", "molar": "M",
    "celsius": "°C", "fahrenheit": "°F", "percent": "%", "kohm": "kΩ", "kohms": "kΩ", "knot": "kn_", "knots": "kn_",
    "ω": "Ω", "c": "°C", "f": "°F",
}


def _scaled(sym: str, prefix: str = "") -> Unit:
    dim, factor, _ = _BASE[sym]
    if prefix:
        factor = factor * _PREFIXES[prefix]
    return Unit(dim, factor, _F(0), prefix + sym)


def _all_exact() -> dict[str, Unit]:
    table: dict[str, Unit] = {"K": Unit(TEMPERATURE, _F(1), _F(0), "K"), **_AFFINE}
    for sym, (dim, _f, prefixable) in _BASE.items():
        table[sym] = _scaled(sym)
        if prefixable:
            for p in _PREFIXES:
                if p + sym not in _BASE:
                    table.setdefault(p + sym, _scaled(sym, p))
    for p in ("m", "µ", "μ", "u", "n", "p", "f", "c", "d", "k", "M", "G", "T", "h"):  # prefixed kelvin e.g. mK
        table.setdefault(p + "K", Unit(TEMPERATURE, _PREFIXES[p], _F(0), p + "K"))
    table["knot"] = Unit(SPEED, _F(1852, 3600), _F(0), "kn")
    return table


_EXACT = _all_exact()
_EXACT_BY_LOWER: dict[str, set[Unit]] = {}
for _k, _u in _EXACT.items():
    _EXACT_BY_LOWER.setdefault(_k.lower(), set()).add(_u)
_PREFIXABLE_CI: dict[str, str] = {k.lower(): k for k, v in _BASE.items() if v[2]}
_PREFIXABLE_CI["k"] = "K"


def _ci_unit(token: str) -> tuple[Unit, str] | None:
    """Case-insensitive reading of a token; returns (unit, note) or None. Raises on ambiguity."""
    low = token.lower()
    cands = {u for u in _EXACT_BY_LOWER.get(low, set()) if not u.affine}
    if len(cands) > 1:                      # "mv" -> mV or MV?  keep the prefix case the user typed
        same_prefix = {u for u in cands if u.symbol[:1] == token[:1]}
        if len(same_prefix) >= 1 and len({(u.dim, u.factor) for u in same_prefix}) == 1:
            cands = same_prefix
    if len({(u.dim, u.factor) for u in cands}) == 1:
        u = next(iter(cands))
        return Unit(u.dim, u.factor, u.offset, u.symbol), f"unit '{token}' read as '{u.symbol}'"
    if len({(u.dim, u.factor) for u in cands}) > 1:
        raise UnitError(f"Ambiguous unit '{token}': could be " + " or ".join(sorted(u.symbol for u in cands)))
    # prefix is case-sensitive ("m" milli / "M" mega), the unit symbol after it is not ("mv" -> mV)
    for p in sorted(_PREFIXES, key=len, reverse=True):
        if token.startswith(p) and len(token) > len(p):
            rest = token[len(p):]
            base = _PREFIXABLE_CI.get(rest.lower())
            if base == "K":
                return Unit(TEMPERATURE, _PREFIXES[p], _F(0), p + "K"), f"unit '{token}' read as '{p}K'"
            if base:
                return _scaled(base, p), f"unit '{token}' read as '{p}{base}'"
    if token.startswith("K") and len(token) > 1:           # "Kg", "KM" -> kilo
        base = _PREFIXABLE_CI.get(token[1:].lower())
        if base and base != "K":
            return _scaled(base, "k"), f"unit '{token}' read as 'k{base}'"
    return None


def lookup_atom(token: str, notes: list[str] | None = None, expected: Dimension | None = None) -> Unit:
    """Resolve one unit atom such as ``km``, ``mV`` or ``°C``."""
    if token in _EXACT:
        return _EXACT[token]
    if expected == TEMPERATURE and token in ("C", "F"):          # "25 C" where a temperature is required
        return _AFFINE["°" + token]
    low = token.lower()
    if low in _WORDS:
        target = _WORDS[low]
        if target == "kn_":
            return _EXACT["knot"]
        if target in _EXACT:
            return _EXACT[target]
    if token == "°":
        return _EXACT["deg"]
    ci = _ci_unit(token)
    if ci:
        if notes is not None:
            notes.append(ci[1])
        return ci[0]
    hint = difflib.get_close_matches(token, list(_EXACT) + list(_WORDS), n=3, cutoff=0.6)
    raise UnitError(f"Unsupported unit '{token}'" + (f" (did you mean {', '.join(repr(h) for h in hint)}?)" if hint else ""))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Compound unit parser:  m/s^2   kg*m/s²   J/(kg·K)   mol m-3   km per hour
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
_UNIT_TOKEN = re.compile(
    r"""\s*(?:
        (?P<atom>°[CFK]?|[^\W\d_]+)(?P<iexp>[+-]?\d+)?
      | (?P<num>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)
      | (?P<op>\*\*|[*/^()\-])
    )""", re.X)


def _prepare_unit_text(text: str) -> str:
    s = convert_superscripts(str(text), "caret") if re.search("[⁰¹²³⁴⁵⁶⁷⁸⁹⁺⁻]", str(text)) else str(text)
    s = re.sub(r"[​﻿]", "", s)
    s = s.replace("℃", "°C").replace("℉", "°F").replace("º", "°")
    s = re.sub(r"(?i)\bdeg(?:rees?)?\s*([CFK])\b", r"°\1", s)
    s = re.sub(r"\s+per\s+", "/", s, flags=re.I)
    s = re.sub(r"[·⋅∙×•]", "*", s).replace("−", "-").replace("÷", "/")
    s = s.replace("°K", "K")
    return s.strip()


class _UnitParser:
    def __init__(self, text: str, notes: list[str], expected: Dimension | None):
        self.text, self.notes, self.expected = _prepare_unit_text(text), notes, expected
        self.tokens: list[tuple[str, str, str | None]] = []
        pos = 0
        while pos < len(self.text):
            m = _UNIT_TOKEN.match(self.text, pos)
            if not m or m.end() == pos:
                raise UnitError(f"Could not parse unit '{text}'")
            if m.group("atom"):
                self.tokens.append(("atom", m.group("atom"), m.group("iexp")))
            elif m.group("num"):
                self.tokens.append(("num", m.group("num"), None))
            else:
                self.tokens.append(("op", m.group("op"), None))
            pos = m.end()
        self.i = 0
        self.simple_atom: Unit | None = None
        self.atom_count = 0

    def peek(self):
        return self.tokens[self.i] if self.i < len(self.tokens) else None

    def parse(self) -> tuple[Dimension, Fraction, Fraction]:
        if not self.tokens:
            return DIMENSIONLESS, _F(1), _F(0)
        d, f = self.expr()
        if self.i != len(self.tokens):
            raise UnitError(f"Could not parse unit '{self.text}'")
        if self.atom_count == 1 and self.simple_atom is not None and self.simple_atom.affine and self._plain:
            return self.simple_atom.dim, self.simple_atom.factor, self.simple_atom.offset
        return d, f, _F(0)

    _plain = True

    def expr(self):
        d, f = self.term()
        while True:
            t = self.peek()
            if t is None or (t[0] == "op" and t[1] == ")"):
                return d, f
            if t == ("op", "*", None) or t == ("op", "-", None):
                self.i += 1; d2, f2 = self.term(); d, f = d * d2, f * f2; self._plain = False
            elif t == ("op", "/", None):
                self.i += 1; d2, f2 = self.term(); d, f = d / d2, f / f2; self._plain = False
            elif t[0] in ("atom", "num") or t == ("op", "(", None):
                d2, f2 = self.term(); d, f = d * d2, f * f2; self._plain = False   # implicit: "kg m"
            else:
                raise UnitError(f"Could not parse unit '{self.text}'")

    def term(self):
        t = self.peek()
        if t is None:
            raise UnitError(f"Could not parse unit '{self.text}'")
        iexp: Fraction | None = None
        if t[0] == "atom":
            self.i += 1
            u = lookup_atom(t[1], self.notes, self.expected)
            self.atom_count += 1; self.simple_atom = u
            d, f = u.dim, u.factor
            if t[2] is not None:
                iexp = Fraction(int(t[2]))
        elif t[0] == "num":
            self.i += 1
            if t[1] != "1":
                raise UnitError(f"Unexpected number '{t[1]}' in unit '{self.text}'")
            d, f = DIMENSIONLESS, _F(1)
        elif t == ("op", "(", None):
            self.i += 1; d, f = self.expr()
            if self.peek() != ("op", ")", None):
                raise UnitError(f"Unbalanced parentheses in unit '{self.text}'")
            self.i += 1; self._plain = False
        else:
            raise UnitError(f"Could not parse unit '{self.text}'")
        nxt = self.peek()
        if nxt and nxt[0] == "op" and nxt[1] in ("^", "**"):
            self.i += 1; iexp = self.exponent()
        if iexp is not None:
            d = d ** iexp
            f = f ** int(iexp) if iexp.denominator == 1 else Fraction(float(f) ** float(iexp))
            if iexp != 1:
                self._plain = False
        return d, f

    def exponent(self) -> Fraction:
        paren = self.peek() == ("op", "(", None)
        if paren:
            self.i += 1
        sign = 1
        t = self.peek()
        if t and t[0] == "op" and t[1] in "-":
            sign = -1; self.i += 1; t = self.peek()
        if not t or t[0] != "num":
            raise UnitError(f"Bad exponent in unit '{self.text}'")
        self.i += 1
        value = Fraction(t[1])
        if paren and self.peek() == ("op", "/", None):
            self.i += 1; t2 = self.peek()
            if not t2 or t2[0] != "num":
                raise UnitError(f"Bad exponent in unit '{self.text}'")
            self.i += 1; value /= Fraction(t2[1])
        if paren:
            if self.peek() != ("op", ")", None):
                raise UnitError(f"Bad exponent in unit '{self.text}'")
            self.i += 1
        return sign * value


def parse_unit(text: str, *, expected: Dimension | None = None, notes: list[str] | None = None) -> Unit:
    """Parse a (possibly compound) unit string.

    A bare ``°C``/``°F`` keeps its offset (absolute temperature).  Inside a compound
    (``J/(kg·°C)``) the degree is a temperature *interval*, so only its size is used.
    """
    local: list[str] = [] if notes is None else notes
    dim, factor, offset = _UnitParser(text, local, expected).parse()
    return Unit(dim, factor, offset, _prepare_unit_text(text) or "1")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Quantities
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Quantity:
    value: float
    unit: Unit

    @property
    def dimension(self) -> Dimension:
        return self.unit.dim

    def _si_exact(self, delta: bool) -> Fraction:
        return Fraction(self.value) * self.unit.factor + (0 if delta else self.unit.offset)

    def to_si(self, *, delta: bool = False) -> float:
        return float(self._si_exact(delta))

    def to(self, target: "Unit | str", *, delta: bool = False) -> float:
        tu = parse_unit(target) if isinstance(target, str) else target
        if tu.dim != self.unit.dim:
            raise DimensionError(
                f"Incompatible units: {self.unit.symbol or 'dimensionless'} ({self.unit.dim.name()}) "
                f"and {tu.symbol or 'dimensionless'} ({tu.dim.name()})")
        out = float((self._si_exact(delta) - (0 if delta else tu.offset)) / tu.factor)
        if not math.isfinite(out):
            raise UnitError("Conversion result is non-finite")
        return out


def parse_quantity(text: str, *, expected: Dimension | None = None, notes: list[str] | None = None) -> Quantity:
    """Parse ``"500 g"``, ``"2 m/s²"``, ``"25 °C"``, ``"1.5×10^-3 kg"``.  A bare number is dimensionless."""
    try:
        value, rest = split_number_prefix(text)
    except NotationError as exc:
        raise UnitError(str(exc)) from exc
    unit = parse_unit(rest, expected=expected, notes=notes) if rest else Unit(DIMENSIONLESS, _F(1), _F(0), "")
    return Quantity(value, unit)


def convert(value: float, from_unit: str, to_unit: str, *, delta: bool = False,
            notes: list[str] | None = None) -> float:
    fu = parse_unit(from_unit, notes=notes)
    tu = parse_unit(to_unit, expected=fu.dim, notes=notes)
    return Quantity(float(value), fu).to(tu, delta=delta)


def dimension_of(unit_text: str, notes: list[str] | None = None) -> Dimension:
    return parse_unit(unit_text, notes=notes).dim


def supported_unit_symbols() -> Iterable[str]:
    return sorted(_EXACT) + sorted(_WORDS)


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/inputs/dimensions.py ---

"""Dimensional consistency checks for expressions and equations.

The analysis walks the *syntax tree* of the (already normalised) expression, not the
simplified SymPy result, so ``m + d - d`` is still reported as mixing a mass with a
distance even though SymPy would cancel the ``d`` terms.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import math
from fractions import Fraction
from typing import Mapping

from app.inputs.notation import CONSTANT_NAMES, FUNCTION_ARITY, normalize_expression, split_equation
from app.inputs.units import DIMENSIONLESS, Dimension, DimensionError, UnitError

# functions whose argument must be dimensionless and which return a dimensionless value
_DIMENSIONLESS_FUNCS = {
    "sin", "cos", "tan", "sec", "csc", "cot", "asin", "acos", "atan", "sinh", "cosh", "tanh",
    "asinh", "acosh", "atanh", "exp", "log", "ln", "log10", "log2", "factorial",
}
_SAME_DIM_FUNCS = {"abs", "floor", "ceiling"}


class MissingUnitError(UnitError):
    """A variable used in the expression has no declared unit."""

    def __init__(self, names: list[str]):
        self.names = names
        super().__init__("No unit declared for variable(s): " + ", ".join(names))


def _const_value(node: ast.AST) -> float | None:
    """Numeric value of an AST made only of constants, or None if it has variables."""
    if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and isinstance(node.value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(node.value, bool):
        return float(node.value)
    if isinstance(node, ast.Name) and node.id in ("pi", "E", "e"):
        return math.pi if node.id == "pi" else math.e
    if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(node.op, (ast.USub, ast.UAdd)):
        v = _const_value(node.operand)
        return None if v is None else (-v if isinstance(node.op, ast.USub) else v)
    if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
        a, b = _const_value(node.left), _const_value(node.right)
        if a is None or b is None:
            return None
        try:
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Add): return a + b
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Sub): return a - b
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Mult): return a * b
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Div): return a / b
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow): return a ** b
        except (ZeroDivisionError, OverflowError, ValueError):
            return None
    return None


def _is_zero(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
    return _const_value(node) == 0


def _show(node: ast.AST) -> str:
    try:
        return ast.unparse(node)
    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - defensive
        return "<expression>"


def infer_dimension(source: str, symbol_dims: Mapping[str, Dimension],
                    variables: list[str] | None = None) -> Dimension:
    """Return the dimension of ``source`` or raise :class:`DimensionError`.

    Every variable must be present in ``symbol_dims``; otherwise :class:`MissingUnitError`.
    A literal ``0`` is dimension-agnostic (``x - 0`` is fine for any ``x``).
    """
    text = normalize_expression(source, variables if variables is not None else list(symbol_dims))
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(text, mode="eval")
    except SyntaxError as exc:
        raise UnitError(f"Expression could not be parsed: {source!r}") from exc

    missing: list[str] = []

    def visit(node: ast.AST) -> Dimension | None:
        if isinstance(node, ast.Expression):
            return visit(node.body)
        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
            return None if node.value == 0 else DIMENSIONLESS
        if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
            if node.id in symbol_dims:
                return symbol_dims[node.id]
            if node.id in CONSTANT_NAMES:
                return DIMENSIONLESS
            if node.id not in missing:
                missing.append(node.id)
            return DIMENSIONLESS
        if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp):
            return visit(node.operand)
        if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
            left, right = visit(node.left), visit(node.right)
            if isinstance(node.op, (ast.Add, ast.Sub, ast.Mod)):
                if left is None: return right
                if right is None: return left
                if left != right:
                    verb = "add" if isinstance(node.op, ast.Add) else "subtract" if isinstance(node.op, ast.Sub) else "combine"
                    raise DimensionError(
                        f"Dimensional mismatch: cannot {verb} {left} and {right} in '{_show(node)}'")
                return left
            left = DIMENSIONLESS if left is None else left
            right = DIMENSIONLESS if right is None else right
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Mult): return left * right
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Div): return left / right
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow):
                if not right.dimensionless:
                    raise DimensionError(f"Dimensional mismatch: exponent in '{_show(node)}' must be dimensionless, got {right}")
                exp = _const_value(node.right)
                if exp is None:
                    if left.dimensionless:
                        return DIMENSIONLESS
                    raise DimensionError(
                        f"Dimensional mismatch: exponent in '{_show(node)}' must be a numeric constant "
                        f"because the base has dimension {left}")
                return left ** Fraction(exp).limit_denominator(1000)
            raise DimensionError(f"Unsupported operator in '{_show(node)}'")
        if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Name):
            name = node.func.id
            if name not in FUNCTION_ARITY:
                raise UnitError(f"Unsupported function '{name}'")
            args = [visit(a) for a in node.args]
            args = [DIMENSIONLESS if a is None else a for a in args]
            if name in _DIMENSIONLESS_FUNCS:
                for a in args:
                    if not a.dimensionless:
                        raise DimensionError(f"Dimensional mismatch: {name}() needs a dimensionless argument, got {a} in '{_show(node)}'")
                return DIMENSIONLESS
            if name in _SAME_DIM_FUNCS:
                return args[0]
            if name == "sqrt": return args[0] ** Fraction(1, 2)
            if name == "cbrt": return args[0] ** Fraction(1, 3)
            if name == "atan2":
                if args[0] != args[1]:
                    raise DimensionError(f"Dimensional mismatch: atan2 arguments must share a dimension in '{_show(node)}'")
                return DIMENSIONLESS
            return DIMENSIONLESS
        raise UnitError(f"Unsupported expression element: {type(node).__name__}")

    result = visit(tree)
    if missing:
        raise MissingUnitError(missing)
    return DIMENSIONLESS if result is None else result


def check_equation_dimensions(equation: str, symbol_dims: Mapping[str, Dimension]) -> Dimension:
    """Both sides of ``lhs = rhs`` must have the same dimension; returns that dimension."""
    lhs, rhs = split_equation(equation, list(symbol_dims))
    dl, dr = infer_dimension(lhs, symbol_dims), infer_dimension(rhs, symbol_dims)
    if rhs.strip() == "0":                   # "expr = 0" carries no information about rhs
        return dl
    if lhs.strip() == "0":
        return dr
    if dl != dr:
        raise DimensionError(f"Dimensional mismatch: left side is {dl} but right side is {dr} in '{equation}'")
    return dl


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/inputs/notation.py ---

"""Shared notation normalizer.

One place that turns the many ways people write maths, numbers and chemistry
into the single canonical form the solvers understand.  Nothing in here evaluates
user input: the output is plain text (or plain data) that is parsed later by the
restricted AST parser in ``core_math.safe_expr``.

Design rules
------------
* Function names (``sin``, ``log10`` ...), variable names (``x1``, ``theta``),
  constants and chemical formulas are never rewritten character-by-character;
  the text is tokenised first, so ``log10(x)`` stays ``log10(x)`` and ``H2O`` is
  never turned into ``H*2*O``.
* ``normalize_expression`` is idempotent: normalising twice equals normalising once.
* Equation boundaries (``=``, ``==``, ``＝``, chemical arrows) are located on tokens,
  never inside numbers or names.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field


class NotationError(ValueError):
    """The text cannot be interpreted safely as the requested notation."""


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Vocabulary shared with the solvers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# canonical function name -> (min_args, max_args)
FUNCTION_ARITY: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {
    "sin": (1, 1), "cos": (1, 1), "tan": (1, 1), "sec": (1, 1), "csc": (1, 1), "cot": (1, 1),
    "asin": (1, 1), "acos": (1, 1), "atan": (1, 1), "atan2": (2, 2),
    "sinh": (1, 1), "cosh": (1, 1), "tanh": (1, 1), "asinh": (1, 1), "acosh": (1, 1), "atanh": (1, 1),
    "exp": (1, 1), "log": (1, 2), "ln": (1, 1), "log10": (1, 1), "log2": (1, 1),
    "sqrt": (1, 1), "cbrt": (1, 1), "abs": (1, 1), "floor": (1, 1), "ceiling": (1, 1),
    "factorial": (1, 1),
}
FUNCTION_ALIASES = {
    "arcsin": "asin", "arccos": "acos", "arctan": "atan", "arctan2": "atan2",
    "arcsinh": "asinh", "arccosh": "acosh", "arctanh": "atanh",
    "ceil": "ceiling", "fabs": "abs", "lg": "log10",
}
CONSTANT_NAMES = frozenset({"pi", "E", "e"})

_SUPERSCRIPT_CHARS = "⁰¹²³⁴⁵⁶⁷⁸⁹⁺⁻"
_SUPERSCRIPT_TABLE = str.maketrans("⁰¹²³⁴⁵⁶⁷⁸⁹⁺⁻", "0123456789+-")
_SUBSCRIPT_TABLE = str.maketrans("₀₁₂₃₄₅₆₇₈₉₊₋", "0123456789+-")
_SUPERSCRIPT_RUN = re.compile(f"[{_SUPERSCRIPT_CHARS}]+")
_INVISIBLE = re.compile("[​‌‍⁠﻿­]")

_CHAR_MAP = {
    # minus / dash variants
    "−": "-", "–": "-", "—": "-", "‐": "-", "‑": "-", "‒": "-",
    "﹣": "-", "－": "-",
    # multiplication
    "×": "*", "·": "*", "⋅": "*", "∙": "*", "✕": "*", "∗": "*", "•": "*", "×": "*",
    # division
    "÷": "/", "∕": "/", "⁄": "/", "⁄": "/",
    # equality
    "＝": "=", "≔": "=",
    # misc punctuation
    "，": ",", "＋": "+",
}


@dataclass
class NormalizationReport:
    """Normalised text plus a human-readable list of what was rewritten."""
    text: str
    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Low-level cleaning
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def convert_superscripts(text: str, style: str = "pow") -> str:
    """Rewrite Unicode superscript runs as exponents.

    style="pow":   ``x²`` -> ``x**2`` and ``x⁻¹`` -> ``x**(-1)`` (maths)
    style="caret": ``m/s²`` -> ``m/s^2`` and ``s⁻¹`` -> ``s^-1`` (unit strings)
    """
    def repl(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        digits = match.group(0).translate(_SUPERSCRIPT_TABLE)
        if style == "caret":
            return "^" + digits
        sign = ""
        if digits[0] in "+-":
            sign, digits = digits[0], digits[1:]
        digits = digits.replace("+", "").replace("-", "")
        if not digits:
            raise NotationError("Dangling superscript sign")
        return f"**({sign}{digits})" if sign == "-" else f"**{digits}"
    return _SUPERSCRIPT_RUN.sub(repl, text)


def _map_chars(text: str) -> str:
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text)  # after superscripts were handled
    return "".join(_CHAR_MAP.get(ch, ch) for ch in text)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Maths expressions
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
_TOKEN = re.compile(
    r"""
    (?P<num>(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+)(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?)
  | (?P<name>[^\W\d]\w*)
  | (?P<op>\*\*|//|<=|>=|==|!=|[-+*/%^(),=<>\[\]{}])
  | (?P<ws>\s+)
  | (?P<other>.)
    """,
    re.X,
)


def _split_unknown_name(name: str, variables: frozenset[str] | None) -> list[str] | None:
    """Split an undeclared token such as ``xy`` or ``x2`` into known pieces."""
    if variables is None:
        m = re.fullmatch(r"([A-Za-z])(\d+)", name)        # legacy: "x2" -> x*2
        return [m.group(1), m.group(2)] if m else None
    known = set(variables) | CONSTANT_NAMES
    m = re.fullmatch(r"([A-Za-z_]\w*?)(\d+)", name)
    if m and m.group(1) in known:
        return [m.group(1), m.group(2)]
    if all(ch in known for ch in name):                    # "xy" with x and y declared
        return list(name)
    return None


def normalize_expression(text: str, variables: list[str] | tuple[str, ...] | None = None,
                         report: NormalizationReport | None = None) -> str:
    """Normalise a mathematical expression (no ``=``-splitting).

    ``variables`` lists declared variable names.  When given, undeclared run-together
    names such as ``xy`` are split into ``x*y`` only if every piece is declared, and
    declared names such as ``x1`` are always preserved.
    """
    original = str(text)
    notes: list[str] = []
    s = _INVISIBLE.sub("", original).strip()
    if not s:
        return ""
    if _SUPERSCRIPT_RUN.search(s):
        s = convert_superscripts(s, "pow"); notes.append("superscripts rewritten as powers")
    s = s.translate(_SUBSCRIPT_TABLE) if "₀" in s else s
    s = _map_chars(s)
    if s != original.strip() and any(c in original for c in "−–—×·÷"):
        notes.append("Unicode operators normalised")
    s = s.replace("π", " pi ").replace("√", " √ ")
    # |x| -> abs(x) for simple, non-nested bars
    if "|" in s:
        if s.count("|") % 2 or "||" in s:
            raise NotationError("Unbalanced or nested absolute-value bars; use abs(...) instead")
        parts = s.split("|"); out = parts[0]
        for i, part in enumerate(parts[1:], 1):
            out += ("abs(" if i % 2 else ")") + part
        s = out; notes.append("|x| rewritten as abs(x)")

    declared = frozenset(variables) if variables is not None else None
    tokens: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for m in _TOKEN.finditer(s):
        kind, tok = m.lastgroup, m.group()
        if kind == "ws":
            continue
        if kind == "other":
            if tok != "√":
                raise NotationError(f"Unsupported character {tok!r} in expression")
            kind = "sqrt"
        tokens.append((kind, tok))

    _PAREN = {"[": "(", "{": "(", "]": ")", "}": ")"}
    resolved: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    i = 0
    while i < len(tokens):
        kind, tok = tokens[i]
        if kind == "op":
            tok = {"^": "**", "==": "="}.get(tok, _PAREN.get(tok, tok))
            resolved.append(("op", tok)); i += 1; continue
        if kind == "sqrt":                       # "√x", "√2", "√(…)" -> sqrt(…)
            j = i + 1
            if j >= len(tokens):
                raise NotationError("'√' must be followed by a value")
            nk, nt = tokens[j]
            resolved += [("func", "sqrt"), ("op", "(")]
            if nk == "op" and nt in "([{":
                depth, end = 0, j
                while end < len(tokens):
                    t = tokens[end][1]
                    depth += (t in "([{" and tokens[end][0] == "op") - (t in ")]}" and tokens[end][0] == "op")
                    if depth == 0:
                        break
                    end += 1
                else:
                    raise NotationError("Unbalanced parentheses after '√'")
                for k2, t2 in tokens[j + 1:end]:
                    resolved.append(("op", _PAREN.get(t2, t2)) if k2 == "op" else (k2, t2))
                i = end + 1
            else:
                resolved.append((nk, nt)); i = j + 1
            resolved.append(("op", ")"))
            notes.append("√ rewritten as sqrt()")
            continue
        if kind == "name":
            canon = FUNCTION_ALIASES.get(tok, tok)
            nxt = tokens[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(tokens) else None
            is_call = nxt is not None and nxt[0] == "op" and nxt[1] in "([{"
            if declared is not None and tok in declared:
                resolved.append(("name", tok)); i += 1; continue
            if canon in FUNCTION_ARITY and is_call:
                if canon != tok:
                    notes.append(f"{tok} rewritten as {canon}")
                resolved.append(("func", canon)); i += 1; continue
            if tok in CONSTANT_NAMES or canon in FUNCTION_ARITY:
                resolved.append(("name", tok)); i += 1; continue
            if is_call and len(tok) > 1 and not (declared is not None and tok in declared) \
                    and _split_unknown_name(tok, declared) is None:
                resolved.append(("func", tok)); i += 1; continue        # unknown function: keep as a call, reject later
            pieces = _split_unknown_name(tok, declared)
            if pieces and len(pieces) > 1:
                for n, piece in enumerate(pieces):
                    resolved.append(("num" if piece.isdigit() else "name", piece))
                    if n < len(pieces) - 1:
                        resolved.append(("op", "*"))
                notes.append(f"implicit multiplication in {tok!r}")
                i += 1; continue
        resolved.append((kind, tok)); i += 1

    # implicit multiplication between tokens
    out: list[str] = []
    prev: tuple[str, str] | None = None
    for kind, tok in resolved:
        if prev is not None:
            pk, pt = prev
            prev_value_end = pk in ("num", "name") or (pk == "op" and pt == ")")
            cur_value_start = kind in ("num", "name", "func") or (kind == "op" and tok == "(")
            if prev_value_end and cur_value_start:
                out.append(" " if (pk == "num" and kind == "num") else "*")   # "2 3" stays invalid
            elif pk in ("name", "num") and kind in ("name", "num"):
                out.append(" ")
        out.append(tok)
        prev = (kind, tok)
    result = "".join(out)
    if report is not None:
        report.text = result
        report.notes.extend(n for n in notes if n not in report.notes)
    return result


def split_equation(source: str, variables: list[str] | tuple[str, ...] | None = None) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Split ``lhs = rhs`` into two normalised expressions (missing rhs -> ``0``)."""
    s = _INVISIBLE.sub("", str(source))
    s = convert_superscripts(s, "pow") if _SUPERSCRIPT_RUN.search(s) else s
    s = _map_chars(s)
    for bad in ("<=", ">=", "!=", "≤", "≥", "≠", "<", ">"):
        if bad in s:
            raise NotationError("Inequalities are not supported; provide an equation with a single '='")
    s = s.replace("==", "=")
    if s.count("=") > 1:
        raise NotationError("Provide one equation with a single '=' sign")
    if "=" not in s:
        if not s.strip():
            raise NotationError("Equation cannot be empty")
        return normalize_expression(s, variables), "0"
    lhs, rhs = s.split("=", 1)
    if not lhs.strip() or not rhs.strip():
        raise NotationError("Both sides of the equation must contain an expression")
    return normalize_expression(lhs, variables), normalize_expression(rhs, variables)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Numbers (used by the unit parser and by model-answer comparison)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
_NUM_PREFIX = re.compile(
    r"""^\s*
    (?P<sign>[+-])?\s*
    (?P<mant>(?:\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+|\d+)(?:\.\d+)?|\.\d+)
    (?:
        (?P<e>[eE][+-]?\d+)(?![A-Za-z0-9_])?
      | \s*[*xX]\s*10\s*(?:\^|\*\*)\s*\(?\s*(?P<p>[+-]?\d+)\s*\)?
    )?
    """, re.X)


def clean_numeric_text(text: str) -> str:
    """Prepare text containing a number: unicode minus, superscripts, NBSP, invisible chars."""
    s = _INVISIBLE.sub("", str(text))
    if _SUPERSCRIPT_RUN.search(s):
        s = convert_superscripts(s, "caret")
    s = _map_chars(s)
    return s.replace(" ", " ").strip()


def split_number_prefix(text: str) -> tuple[float, str]:
    """Split ``"-1.5×10^-3 kg"`` into ``(-0.0015, "kg")``.

    Accepts a leading sign (ASCII or Unicode minus), thousands separators
    (``1,000``), ``e``/``E`` notation and ``×10^n`` notation.  A decimal comma is
    rejected because it is ambiguous with a thousands separator or argument list.
    """
    s = clean_numeric_text(text)
    m = _NUM_PREFIX.match(s)
    if not m:
        raise NotationError(f"No numeric value found in {text!r}")
    if re.match(r"^\s*[+-]?\d+,\d{1,2}(?!\d)", s):
        raise NotationError(f"Ambiguous decimal comma in {text!r}; use a decimal point")
    mant = m.group("mant").replace(",", "")
    exp = m.group("e") or (("e" + m.group("p")) if m.group("p") is not None else "")
    try:
        value = float(f"{m.group('sign') or ''}{mant}{exp}")
    except (ValueError, OverflowError) as exc:
        raise NotationError(f"Number out of range in {text!r}") from exc
    if value in (float("inf"), float("-inf")) or value != value:
        raise NotationError(f"Number out of range in {text!r}")
    return value, s[m.end():].strip()


def parse_plain_number(text: str) -> float:
    """Parse a bare number such as ``"1.5e-3"``, ``"−3"``, ``"2×10^4"`` or ``"1,000"``."""
    value, rest = split_number_prefix(text)
    if rest:
        raise NotationError(f"Unexpected text {rest!r} after number in {text!r}")
    return value


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Chemistry
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
_ARROW = re.compile(r"\s*(?:<[-=]+>|-{1,2}>|=+>|[→⟶⟹⇒⇌⇄↔]|=)\s*")
_STATE = re.compile(r"\((?:s|l|g|aq)\)$")


def normalize_chemical_formula(text: str) -> str:
    """Normalise a formula: ``H₂O`` -> ``H2O``; hydrate dots unified to ``.``.

    Letters keep their case (``Co`` is cobalt, ``CO`` is carbon monoxide).
    """
    s = _INVISIBLE.sub("", str(text)).translate(_SUBSCRIPT_TABLE)
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", s)
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", "", s)
    s = re.sub(r"[·•⋅∙*]", ".", s)
    if not s:
        raise NotationError("Provide a chemical formula, e.g. H2O")
    bad = re.search(r"[^A-Za-z0-9().]", s)
    if bad:
        raise NotationError(f"Unsupported character {bad.group()!r} in chemical formula {text!r}")
    return s


def normalize_chemical_equation(text: str) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], list[str]]:
    """Split ``"H2 + O2 => H2O"`` into (reactants, products, notes).

    Accepts ``->``, ``-->``, ``=>``, ``→``, ``⟶``, ``⇌``, ``<=>`` and ``=`` as the single
    arrow, strips state symbols ``(s) (l) (g) (aq)`` and leading coefficients.
    """
    notes: list[str] = []
    s = _INVISIBLE.sub("", str(text)).translate(_SUBSCRIPT_TABLE)
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", s).replace("＋", "+")
    parts = _ARROW.split(s)
    if len(parts) != 2:
        raise NotationError("Provide a chemical equation with exactly one arrow, e.g. H2 + O2 -> H2O")
    sides: list[list[str]] = []
    for side in parts:
        species = []
        for raw in side.split("+"):
            f = re.sub(r"\s+", "", raw)
            if not f:
                raise NotationError("Empty species in chemical equation")
            if _STATE.search(f):
                f = _STATE.sub("", f); notes.append("state symbols ignored")
            m = re.match(r"^(\d+)(?=[A-Z(])", f)
            if m:
                f = f[m.end():]; notes.append("existing coefficients ignored and recomputed")
            species.append(normalize_chemical_formula(f))
        sides.append(species)
    return sides[0], sides[1], sorted(set(notes))


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/inputs/__init__.py ---



--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/inputs/constraints.py ---

"""Input validation primitives: required inputs, numeric bounds and physical constraints.

Every problem is reported as an :class:`Issue` with a ``category`` that separates

* ``"missing"`` - a required value was not supplied (or is empty / null), from
* ``"invalid"`` - a value *was* supplied but cannot be used (wrong type, not finite,
  wrong unit/dimension, outside its physical range ...).

Quantities may be given as plain numbers (already in the solver's unit), as strings such as
``"500 g"`` or ``"25 °C"``, or as ``{"value": 500, "unit": "g"}`` and are converted to the
unit the solver expects.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from app.inputs.notation import NotationError, parse_plain_number
from app.inputs.units import (DIMENSIONLESS, Dimension, DimensionError, Quantity, Unit, UnitError,
                              parse_quantity, parse_unit)

MAX_LIST = 10_000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Issue:
    category: str            # "missing" | "invalid"
    code: str
    field: str | None
    message: str

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"category": self.category, "code": self.code, "field": self.field, "message": self.message}


def missing(field: str, message: str | None = None, code: str = "missing_input") -> Issue:
    return Issue("missing", code, field, message or f"Required input '{field}' is missing")


def invalid(field: str | None, message: str, code: str = "invalid_input") -> Issue:
    return Issue("invalid", code, field, message)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ParamSpec:
    """Specification of one named input (normally an ``extra_params`` entry)."""
    name: str
    kind: str = "number"                  # number | integer | number_list | matrix | text
    unit: str | None = None               # unit the solver expects; makes the input quantity-capable
    required: bool = True
    min: float | None = None
    max: float | None = None
    min_open: bool = False                # True -> value must be > min
    max_open: bool = False
    nonzero: bool = False
    temperature: str = "absolute"         # "absolute" or "delta" for temperature units
    min_len: int = 1
    distinct: bool = False
    constraint: str = ""                  # plain-language physical constraint shown in messages

    @property
    def dim(self) -> Dimension:
        return parse_unit(self.unit).dim if self.unit else DIMENSIONLESS


def _fmt(v: float) -> str:
    return f"{v:.12g}"


def _bounds_issue(spec: ParamSpec, value: float, label: str) -> Issue | None:
    if spec.min is not None and (value <= spec.min if spec.min_open else value < spec.min):
        rel = ">" if spec.min_open else ">="
        why = f" ({spec.constraint})" if spec.constraint else ""
        return invalid(spec.name, f"{label} must be {rel} {_fmt(spec.min)}{why}; got {_fmt(value)}", "out_of_range")
    if spec.max is not None and (value >= spec.max if spec.max_open else value > spec.max):
        rel = "<" if spec.max_open else "<="
        why = f" ({spec.constraint})" if spec.constraint else ""
        return invalid(spec.name, f"{label} must be {rel} {_fmt(spec.max)}{why}; got {_fmt(value)}", "out_of_range")
    if spec.nonzero and value == 0:
        return invalid(spec.name, f"{label} must not be zero", "out_of_range")
    return None


@dataclass
class ParamOutcome:
    value: Any = None
    issues: list[Issue] | None = None
    had_unit: bool = False
    conversion: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    notes: list[str] | None = None


def _scalar(spec: ParamSpec, raw: Any, out: ParamOutcome) -> float | None:
    label = spec.name
    if isinstance(raw, bool):
        out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label} must be a number, not a boolean", "invalid_type"))
        return None
    if isinstance(raw, (int, float)):
        value = float(raw)
        if not math.isfinite(value):
            out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label} must be a finite number", "not_finite"))
            return None
        return value
    unit_text: str | None = None
    number_text: str | None = None
    if isinstance(raw, dict):
        if set(raw) - {"value", "unit"} or "value" not in raw:
            out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label} must be a number or {{\"value\": ..., \"unit\": ...}}", "invalid_type"))
            return None
        inner, unit_text = raw["value"], raw.get("unit")
        if isinstance(inner, bool) or not isinstance(inner, (int, float, str)):
            out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label}.value must be a number", "invalid_type"))
            return None
        try:
            number = float(inner) if not isinstance(inner, str) else parse_plain_number(inner)
        except (NotationError, ValueError):
            out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label}.value '{inner}' is not a number", "invalid_type"))
            return None
        if not math.isfinite(number):
            out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label} must be a finite number", "not_finite"))
            return None
        if unit_text in (None, ""):
            return number
        number_text = f"{number!r}"
        raw_label = f"{raw['value']} {unit_text}"
    elif isinstance(raw, str):
        raw_label = raw.strip()
        if not raw_label:
            out.issues.append(missing(label, f"Required input '{label}' is empty"))
            return None
        number_text = raw_label
    else:
        out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label} must be a number or a quantity string such as '5 {spec.unit or 'kg'}'", "invalid_type"))
        return None

    # numeric or quantity string
    notes: list[str] = []
    try:
        if unit_text:
            q = Quantity(float(number_text), parse_unit(unit_text, expected=spec.dim, notes=notes))
        else:
            q = parse_quantity(number_text, expected=spec.dim, notes=notes)
    except (UnitError, NotationError) as exc:
        text = str(exc)
        kind = "unsupported_unit" if isinstance(exc, UnitError) else "invalid_type"
        if kind == "invalid_type":
            text = f"{label} '{raw_label}' is not a number ({text})"
        else:
            text = f"{label}: {text}"
        out.issues.append(invalid(label, text, kind))
        return None
    if not q.unit.symbol:                              # plain number inside a string
        return q.value
    if spec.unit is None:
        out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label} takes a plain number, but '{raw_label}' has a unit", "unexpected_unit"))
        return None
    target = parse_unit(spec.unit)
    if q.unit.dim != target.dim:
        out.issues.append(invalid(
            label,
            f"{label}: '{raw_label}' has dimension {q.unit.dim}, but {target.dim} is required "
            f"(solver unit: {spec.unit})", "dimension_mismatch"))
        return None
    try:
        value = q.to(target, delta=(spec.temperature == "delta"))
    except UnitError as exc:
        out.issues.append(invalid(label, f"{label}: {exc}", "unsupported_unit"))
        return None
    out.had_unit = True
    out.notes = notes
    out.conversion = {"field": label, "from": raw_label, "to": f"{_fmt(value)} {spec.unit}", "value": value, "unit": spec.unit}
    return value


def validate_param(spec: ParamSpec, raw: Any) -> ParamOutcome:
    """Validate and convert one input according to ``spec``."""
    out = ParamOutcome(issues=[])
    if raw is None:
        if spec.required:
            out.issues.append(missing(spec.name, f"Required input '{spec.name}' is missing"
                                      + (f" (expected {spec.dim.name()} in {spec.unit})" if spec.unit else "")))
        return out
    if spec.kind in ("number", "integer"):
        value = _scalar(spec, raw, out)
        if value is None:
            return out
        if spec.kind == "integer":
            if abs(value) > 2 ** 53 or value != round(value):
                out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name} must be a whole number; got {_fmt(value)}", "not_integer"))
                return out
            value = int(round(value))
        issue = _bounds_issue(spec, float(value), spec.name)
        if issue:
            out.issues.append(issue)
            return out
        out.value = value
        return out
    if spec.kind == "number_list":
        if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
            out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name} must be a list of numbers", "invalid_type"))
            return out
        if len(raw) < spec.min_len:
            out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name} needs at least {spec.min_len} value(s); got {len(raw)}", "too_few_values"))
            return out
        if len(raw) > MAX_LIST:
            out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name} has too many values (limit {MAX_LIST})", "too_many_values"))
            return out
        values = []
        for i, item in enumerate(raw):
            if isinstance(item, bool) or not isinstance(item, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(float(item)):
                out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name}[{i}] must be a finite number; got {item!r}", "invalid_type"))
                return out
            issue = _bounds_issue(spec, float(item), f"{spec.name}[{i}]")
            if issue:
                out.issues.append(issue)
                return out
            values.append(float(item))
        if spec.distinct and len(set(values)) != len(values):
            out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name} values must be distinct", "not_distinct"))
            return out
        out.value = values
        return out
    if spec.kind == "matrix":
        if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)) or not raw or not all(isinstance(r, (list, tuple)) and r for r in raw):
            out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name} must be a non-empty list of equal-length rows", "invalid_type"))
            return out
        if len({len(r) for r in raw}) != 1:
            out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name} rows must all have the same length", "ragged_matrix"))
            return out
        rows = []
        for i, row in enumerate(raw):
            vals = []
            for j, item in enumerate(row):
                if isinstance(item, bool) or not isinstance(item, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(float(item)):
                    out.issues.append(invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name}[{i}][{j}] must be a finite number; got {item!r}", "invalid_type"))
                    return out
                vals.append(float(item))
            rows.append(vals)
        out.value = rows
        return out
    if spec.kind == "text":
        if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
            out.issues.append(missing(spec.name, f"Required input '{spec.name}' is empty") if isinstance(raw, str)
                              else invalid(spec.name, f"{spec.name} must be text", "invalid_type"))
            return out
        out.value = raw
        return out
    raise ValueError(f"Unknown ParamSpec kind {spec.kind!r}")  # programming error, not user input


def describe_issues(issues: list[Issue]) -> str:
    """One readable message: missing inputs first, then invalid ones."""
    miss = [i.message for i in issues if i.category == "missing"]
    bad = [i.message for i in issues if i.category == "invalid"]
    parts = []
    if miss:
        parts.append("Missing input: " + "; ".join(miss))
    if bad:
        parts.append("Invalid input: " + "; ".join(bad))
    return " | ".join(parts)


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/inputs/preflight.py ---

"""Universal preflight: every solver request passes through here before any calculation.

Pipeline for one :class:`~app.schemas.models.SolverRequest`
    1. normalise notation (``x²``/``x^2``/``x**2``, ``×``, ``−``, ``1.5×10⁻³`` ...)
    2. analyse variables (declared vs. used) and reject unsupported syntax/functions
    3. validate required inputs, numeric bounds and physical constraints, separating
       *missing* inputs from *invalid* ones
    4. parse quantities (``500 g``, ``2 m/s²``, ``25 °C``) and convert them to the unit the
       solver expects; incompatible dimensions are rejected, never silently combined
    5. check dimensional consistency of expressions/equations and of the expected result

On success the solver receives an equivalent, normalised request; on failure a
``status="failed"`` response with a clear message and machine-readable ``issues`` is
returned and the solver is not called.  Plain numbers keep their old meaning (already in
the solver's unit), so existing callers are unaffected.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import math
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from app.inputs import dimensions as dims_mod
from app.inputs.constraints import (Issue, ParamSpec, describe_issues, invalid, missing, validate_param)
from app.inputs.notation import (CONSTANT_NAMES, FUNCTION_ALIASES, FUNCTION_ARITY, NormalizationReport,
                                 NotationError, normalize_chemical_equation, normalize_chemical_formula,
                                 normalize_expression, parse_plain_number, split_equation)
from app.inputs.specs import (DEFINITE_INTEGRAL_FUNCTIONS, DOMAIN_SPECS, EXPRESSION_OPS, RESULT_RELATION, VARIABLE_MUST_APPEAR,
                              OpSpec, get_spec)
from app.inputs.units import (DIMENSIONLESS, Dimension, DimensionError, Quantity, UnitError, convert,
                              parse_quantity, parse_unit)
from app.schemas.models import SolverRequest, SolverResponse

_IDENT = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*$")
_RESERVED = frozenset(FUNCTION_ARITY) | frozenset(FUNCTION_ALIASES) | {"pi"}
_ALLOWED_NODES = (ast.Expression, ast.Constant, ast.Name, ast.BinOp, ast.UnaryOp, ast.Call, ast.Load,
                  ast.Add, ast.Sub, ast.Mult, ast.Div, ast.Pow, ast.Mod, ast.UAdd, ast.USub)
_INF_WORDS = {"oo": "oo", "+oo": "oo", "inf": "oo", "+inf": "oo", "infinity": "oo", "∞": "oo", "+∞": "oo",
              "-oo": "-oo", "-inf": "-oo", "-infinity": "-oo", "-∞": "-oo"}
MAX_NODES = 250


@dataclass
class PreflightResult:
    ok: bool
    request: SolverRequest
    issues: list[Issue] = field(default_factory=list)
    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    conversions: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    dimension: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    expected_unit: str | None = None
    output_unit: str | None = None

    @property
    def error_message(self) -> str:
        return describe_issues(self.issues)

    def to_details(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        out: dict[str, Any] = {"ok": self.ok}
        if self.issues:
            out["issues"] = [i.to_dict() for i in self.issues]
        if self.notes:
            out["notes"] = self.notes
        if self.conversions:
            out["conversions"] = self.conversions
        if self.dimension:
            out["dimension"] = self.dimension
        return out


class _Ctx:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.issues: list[Issue] = []
        self.notes: list[str] = []
        self.conversions: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.dimension: dict[str, str] = {}

    def note(self, *texts: str) -> None:
        for t in texts:
            if t and t not in self.notes:
                self.notes.append(t)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Expression analysis
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def analyze_expression(text: str, declared: list[str], field_name: str, ctx: _Ctx,
                       allowed_functions: frozenset[str] | None = None,
                       allow_names: bool = True) -> tuple[str | None, set[str]]:
    """Normalise ``text`` and check syntax, functions and variables.

    Returns ``(normalised_text, used_variable_names)``; ``normalised_text`` is None when
    issues were recorded.
    """
    report = NormalizationReport("")
    try:
        norm = normalize_expression(text, declared, report)
    except NotationError as exc:
        ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name}: {exc}", "notation_error"))
        return None, set()
    ctx.note(*report.notes)
    if not norm:
        ctx.issues.append(missing(field_name, f"Required input '{field_name}' is empty"))
        return None, set()
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(norm, mode="eval")
    except SyntaxError as exc:
        ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} could not be parsed ({exc.msg}); check for missing "
                                              f"operators or unbalanced brackets in '{text}'", "parse_error"))
        return None, set()
    except (RecursionError, MemoryError, ValueError):
        ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} is too deeply nested or malformed", "parse_error"))
        return None, set()
    nodes = list(ast.walk(tree))
    if len(nodes) > MAX_NODES:
        ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} is too complex (limit {MAX_NODES} elements)", "too_complex"))
        return None, set()

    before = len(ctx.issues)
    call_funcs = {id(n.func) for n in nodes if isinstance(n, ast.Call)}
    names: set[str] = set()
    undeclared: list[str] = []
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, _ALLOWED_NODES):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} uses unsupported syntax ({type(node).__name__}); "
                                                  f"only numbers, variables, + - * / ** % and supported functions are allowed",
                                      "unsupported_syntax"))
            return None, set()
        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
            if isinstance(node.value, bool) or not isinstance(node.value, (int, float)):
                ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} may only contain numeric constants", "unsupported_syntax"))
                return None, set()
            if isinstance(node.value, float) and not math.isfinite(node.value):
                ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} contains a non-finite constant", "not_finite"))
                return None, set()
        elif isinstance(node, ast.Call):
            if not isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) or node.keywords:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} uses an unsupported function call", "unsupported_function"))
                continue
            fname = node.func.id
            pool = allowed_functions if allowed_functions is not None else frozenset(FUNCTION_ARITY)
            if fname not in pool:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"Unsupported function '{fname}' in {field_name}. Supported: "
                                                      + ", ".join(sorted(pool)), "unsupported_function"))
                continue
            lo, hi = FUNCTION_ARITY[fname]
            if not lo <= len(node.args) <= hi:
                want = str(lo) if lo == hi else f"{lo}-{hi}"
                ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{fname}() takes {want} argument(s); got {len(node.args)}", "bad_arity"))
        elif isinstance(node, ast.Name) and id(node) not in call_funcs:
            n = node.id
            if n in declared:
                names.add(n)
            elif n in CONSTANT_NAMES:
                pass
            elif n in FUNCTION_ARITY:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"Function '{n}' must be called with parentheses, e.g. {n}(x)", "unsupported_syntax"))
            elif n not in undeclared:
                undeclared.append(n)
    if undeclared:
        hint = ""
        if len(undeclared) == 1:
            hint = f" If '{undeclared[0]}' is the unknown, set variable='{undeclared[0]}'; if it is a parameter, add it to `variables`."
        else:
            hint = " Declare them with `variable` / `variables`."
        ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"Undeclared variable(s) in {field_name}: {', '.join(undeclared)}.{hint}",
                                  "undeclared_variable"))
    if len(ctx.issues) > before:
        return None, names
    return norm, names


def analyze_equation(text: str, declared: list[str], field_name: str, ctx: _Ctx) -> tuple[str | None, set[str]]:
    try:
        lhs, rhs = split_equation(text, declared)
    except NotationError as exc:
        ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name}: {exc}", "notation_error"))
        return None, set()
    has_eq = "=" in text.replace("＝", "=")
    before = len(ctx.issues)
    nl, names_l = analyze_expression(lhs, declared, field_name, ctx)
    nr, names_r = analyze_expression(rhs, declared, field_name, ctx)
    if len(ctx.issues) > before or nl is None or nr is None:
        return None, names_l | names_r
    return (f"{nl} = {nr}" if has_eq else nl), names_l | names_r


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _declared(req: SolverRequest, ctx: _Ctx) -> list[str]:
    names: list[str] = []
    candidates = [req.variable, *(req.variables or [])]
    if req.solver == "partial_differentiate" and req.target:
        candidates.append(req.target)
    for n in candidates:
        if not isinstance(n, str) or not _IDENT.match(n):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("variables", f"Invalid variable name {n!r}; use letters, digits and underscores, "
                                                    f"starting with a letter", "invalid_variable_name"))
        elif n in _RESERVED:
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("variables", f"Variable name '{n}' is reserved (function or constant); choose another",
                                      "invalid_variable_name"))
        elif n not in names:
            names.append(n)
    return names


def _parse_point(value: Any, field_name: str, ctx: _Ctx) -> Any:
    """A limit/expansion point: number, +-infinity word, or constant expression like pi/2."""
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} must be a number, not a boolean", "invalid_type")); return None
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        if not math.isfinite(float(value)):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} must be finite (use 'oo' for infinity)", "not_finite")); return None
        return value
    if isinstance(value, str):
        key = value.strip().lower().replace(" ", "")
        if key in _INF_WORDS:
            return _INF_WORDS[key]
        try:
            return parse_plain_number(value)
        except NotationError:
            pass
        norm, names = analyze_expression(value, [], field_name, ctx)
        return norm
    ctx.issues.append(invalid(field_name, f"{field_name} must be a number, 'oo', or a constant expression", "invalid_type"))
    return None


def _convert_unit_op(req: SolverRequest, extra: dict[str, Any], ctx: _Ctx) -> None:
    value, from_u, to_u = extra.get("value"), extra.get("from_unit"), extra.get("to_unit")
    if value is None:
        ctx.issues.append(missing("value", "Required input 'value' is missing"))
    if not to_u:
        ctx.issues.append(missing("to_unit", "Required input 'to_unit' is missing"))
    if value is None or not to_u:
        return
    notes: list[str] = []
    try:
        if isinstance(value, str) and not from_u:                     # "100 km/h" with no from_unit
            q = parse_quantity(value, notes=notes)
            if not q.unit.symbol:
                ctx.issues.append(missing("from_unit", "Required input 'from_unit' is missing"))
                return
            number, from_u = q.value, q.unit.symbol
        else:
            if not from_u:
                ctx.issues.append(missing("from_unit", "Required input 'from_unit' is missing"))
                return
            if isinstance(value, bool):
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("value", "value must be a number, not a boolean", "invalid_type")); return
            if isinstance(value, str):
                try:
                    number = parse_plain_number(value)
                except NotationError:
                    ctx.issues.append(invalid("value", f"value '{value}' already includes a unit; omit from_unit or give a plain number",
                                              "conflicting_units")); return
            else:
                number = float(value)
        if not math.isfinite(number):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("value", "value must be a finite number", "not_finite")); return
        out = convert(number, str(from_u), str(to_u), delta=bool(extra.get("interval", False)), notes=notes)
    except DimensionError as exc:
        ctx.issues.append(invalid("to_unit", str(exc), "dimension_mismatch")); return
    except (UnitError, NotationError, ValueError) as exc:
        ctx.issues.append(invalid("from_unit/to_unit", str(exc), "unsupported_unit")); return
    ctx.note(*notes)
    extra["value"], extra["from_unit"], extra["to_unit"] = number, str(from_u), str(to_u)
    ctx.dimension["result"] = parse_unit(str(to_u)).dim.name()


def _build_dims(req: SolverRequest, extra: dict[str, Any], declared: list[str], ctx: _Ctx,
                subs_dims: dict[str, Dimension], plain_subs: set[str]) -> dict[str, Dimension] | None:
    raw_units = extra.get("units")
    dims: dict[str, Dimension] = {}
    if raw_units is not None:
        if not isinstance(raw_units, dict):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("units", "units must map variable names to unit strings, e.g. {\"m\": \"kg\"}", "invalid_type"))
            return None
        for var, unit in raw_units.items():
            if var not in declared:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("units", f"units refers to undeclared variable '{var}'", "undeclared_variable")); continue
            try:
                notes: list[str] = []
                dims[var] = parse_unit(str(unit), notes=notes).dim
                ctx.note(*notes)
            except UnitError as exc:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("units", f"units['{var}']: {exc}", "unsupported_unit"))
    for var, d in subs_dims.items():
        if var in dims and dims[var] != d:
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("substitutions", f"substitution for '{var}' has dimension {d} but units declares {dims[var]}",
                                      "dimension_mismatch"))
        dims[var] = d
    for var in plain_subs:
        dims.setdefault(var, DIMENSIONLESS)
    return dims


def _check_dimensions(req: SolverRequest, extra: dict[str, Any], declared: list[str], norm: dict[str, str],
                      ctx: _Ctx, subs_dims: dict[str, Dimension], plain_subs: set[str]) -> None:
    op = req.solver
    has_units = extra.get("units") is not None or bool(subs_dims) or extra.get("expected_unit") is not None
    if not has_units:
        return
    before = len(ctx.issues)
    dims = _build_dims(req, extra, declared, ctx, subs_dims, plain_subs)
    expected_dim: Dimension | None = None
    if extra.get("expected_unit") is not None:
        try:
            expected_dim = parse_unit(str(extra["expected_unit"])).dim
        except UnitError as exc:
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("expected_unit", str(exc), "unsupported_unit"))
    if dims is None or len(ctx.issues) > before:
        return
    try:
        if op == "solve_system":
            for i, eq in enumerate(req.equations or []):
                dims_mod.check_equation_dimensions(norm["equations"][i], dims)
            ctx.dimension["system"] = "all equations are dimensionally consistent"
            return
        text = norm["expression"]
        if op in ("solve_equation",):
            eq_dim = dims_mod.check_equation_dimensions(text, dims)
            ctx.dimension["equation"] = str(eq_dim)
            result_dim = dims.get(req.variable)
            if result_dim is None:
                raise dims_mod.MissingUnitError([req.variable])
        else:
            expr_dim = dims_mod.infer_dimension(text, dims)
            ctx.dimension["expression"] = str(expr_dim)
            rel = RESULT_RELATION.get(op)
            result_dim = expr_dim
            if rel in ("per_var", "times_var", "per_target"):
                var = req.target if rel == "per_target" and req.target else req.variable
                if var not in dims:
                    raise dims_mod.MissingUnitError([var])
                result_dim = expr_dim / dims[var] if rel != "times_var" else expr_dim * dims[var]
            if op == "binomial_expansion" and req.degree:
                result_dim = expr_dim ** req.degree
        ctx.dimension["result"] = str(result_dim)
        if expected_dim is not None and result_dim != expected_dim:
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("expected_unit", f"Result has dimension {result_dim} but expected_unit "
                                                       f"'{extra['expected_unit']}' is {expected_dim}", "dimension_mismatch"))
    except dims_mod.MissingUnitError as exc:
        ctx.issues.append(missing("units", f"No unit declared for variable(s): {', '.join(exc.names)}. Add them to "
                                           f"extra_params.units (or give them as quantities in substitutions)", "missing_unit"))
    except DimensionError as exc:
        ctx.issues.append(invalid("expression", str(exc), "dimension_mismatch"))
    except UnitError as exc:
        ctx.issues.append(invalid("units", str(exc), "unsupported_unit"))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# The preflight
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _preflight(req: SolverRequest) -> PreflightResult:
    op = req.solver
    ctx = _Ctx()
    extra = dict(req.extra_params)
    updates: dict[str, Any] = {}
    spec: OpSpec | None = get_spec(op)
    declared = _declared(req, ctx)
    norm: dict[str, Any] = {}
    subs_dims: dict[str, Dimension] = {}
    plain_subs: set[str] = set()
    used_names: set[str] = set()

    # ---- expression / equation(s) ---------------------------------------
    if op in EXPRESSION_OPS:
        if not req.expression:
            ctx.issues.append(missing("expression"))
        else:
            funcs = DEFINITE_INTEGRAL_FUNCTIONS if op == "definite_integral" else None
            if op == "solve_equation":
                text, used_names = analyze_equation(req.expression, declared, "expression", ctx)
            else:
                text, used_names = analyze_expression(req.expression, declared, "expression", ctx, funcs)
            if text is not None:
                norm["expression"] = text
                updates["expression"] = text
                if op in VARIABLE_MUST_APPEAR and req.variable not in used_names:
                    ctx.issues.append(invalid("variable", f"The variable '{req.variable}' does not appear in the expression; "
                                                          f"set `variable` to the unknown you want to solve for", "variable_not_in_expression"))
    elif op == "solve_system":
        if not req.equations:
            ctx.issues.append(missing("equations"))
        if not req.variables:
            ctx.issues.append(missing("variables", "Required input 'variables' is missing (list the unknowns)"))
        if req.equations and req.variables:
            eqs, ok = [], True
            for i, eq in enumerate(req.equations):
                t, _ = analyze_equation(eq, list(req.variables), f"equations[{i}]", ctx)
                ok = ok and t is not None
                eqs.append(t)
            if ok:
                norm["equations"] = eqs
                updates["equations"] = eqs
                if len(eqs) < len(req.variables):
                    ctx.note(f"{len(eqs)} equation(s) for {len(req.variables)} unknowns: the system is underdetermined")
                elif len(eqs) > len(req.variables):
                    ctx.note(f"{len(eqs)} equations for {len(req.variables)} unknowns: the system is overdetermined")

    # ---- required top-level fields --------------------------------------
    if spec:
        for name in spec.top_required:
            value = getattr(req, name, None)
            if value is None or value == []:
                ctx.issues.append(missing(name))
    if op in ("descriptive_stats", "mean", "median", "mode", "variance", "standard_deviation", "percentile"):
        data = req.data if req.data is not None else req.values
        if not data:
            ctx.issues.append(missing("data", "Required input 'data' (or 'values') is missing or empty"))
    if op in ("bisection_root", "numerical_root", "numerical_integral", "trapezoid_integral",
              "simpson_integral", "definite_integral"):
        lo, hi = req.lower_bound, req.upper_bound
        if lo is not None and hi is not None:
            if op == "definite_integral":
                if lo > hi:
                    ctx.issues.append(invalid("lower_bound", "Lower bound must not exceed upper bound", "out_of_range"))
            elif lo >= hi:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("lower_bound", "lower_bound must be less than upper_bound", "out_of_range"))
    if op == "numerical_root" and (req.method or "brentq") in ("brentq", "brent", "bisect", "bisection"):
        if req.lower_bound is None or req.upper_bound is None:
            ctx.issues.append(missing("lower_bound/upper_bound", "This root-finding method requires lower_bound and upper_bound"))
    if op == "numerical_root" and (req.method or "") == "newton" and req.initial_guess is None:
        ctx.issues.append(missing("initial_guess", "Newton method requires initial_guess"))

    # ---- named parameters (quantities, bounds, physical constraints) ----
    had_unit: dict[str, bool] = {}
    values: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if op == "unit_convert":
        _convert_unit_op(req, extra, ctx)
    elif op == "chemistry_molar_mass":
        formula = extra.get("formula")
        if formula is None or (isinstance(formula, str) and not formula.strip()):
            ctx.issues.append(missing("formula", "Required input 'formula' is missing, e.g. H2O"))
        elif not isinstance(formula, str):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("formula", "formula must be text, e.g. 'H2O'", "invalid_type"))
        else:
            try:
                extra["formula"] = normalize_chemical_formula(formula)
            except NotationError as exc:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("formula", str(exc), "notation_error"))
    elif op == "chemistry_balance_equation":
        equation = extra.get("equation")
        if equation is None or (isinstance(equation, str) and not equation.strip()):
            ctx.issues.append(missing("equation", "Required input 'equation' is missing, e.g. H2 + O2 -> H2O"))
        elif not isinstance(equation, str):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("equation", "equation must be text", "invalid_type"))
        else:
            try:
                left, right, notes = normalize_chemical_equation(equation)
                extra["equation"] = " + ".join(left) + " -> " + " + ".join(right)
                ctx.note(*notes)
            except NotationError as exc:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("equation", str(exc), "notation_error"))
    elif spec and spec.params:
        for p in spec.params:
            raw = extra.get(p.name)
            if op == "convert_number_base" and p.name == "value" and isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
                raw = str(raw)
            out = validate_param(p, raw)
            ctx.issues.extend(out.issues or [])
            if out.value is not None:
                values[p.name] = out.value
                extra[p.name] = out.value
            if out.conversion:
                ctx.conversions.append(out.conversion)
            ctx.note(*(out.notes or []))
            had_unit[p.name] = out.had_unit
        for group in spec.same_units:
            present = [n for n in group if n in values]
            if len(group) > 1 and 0 < sum(had_unit.get(n, False) for n in present) < len(present):
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("/".join(group), f"If one of {', '.join(group)} is given with a unit, give all of them "
                                                           f"with units (plain numbers are assumed to be in the solver's unit)",
                                          "inconsistent_units"))
        if not any(i.category == "invalid" and i.field in {p.name for p in spec.params} for i in ctx.issues):
            for rule in spec.rules:
                rule(values, extra, ctx.issues)
        if op in ("variance", "standard_deviation") and "ddof" in values:
            data = req.data if req.data is not None else req.values
            if data and values["ddof"] >= len(data):
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("ddof", f"ddof ({values['ddof']}) must be smaller than the number of observations ({len(data)})",
                                          "out_of_range"))
        if op == "sequence_sum":
            values.setdefault("start", 1); values.setdefault("stop", 10)
    if op == "normal_probability":
        side = extra.get("side", "less")
        if side not in ("less", "greater", "between"):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("side", "side must be 'less', 'greater' or 'between'", "invalid_choice"))
        elif side == "between":
            lo, hi = values.get("lower"), values.get("upper")
            if lo is None: ctx.issues.append(missing("lower"))
            if hi is None: ctx.issues.append(missing("upper"))
            if lo is not None and hi is not None and lo > hi:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("lower", "For 'between', lower must be <= upper", "out_of_range"))
        elif values.get("x") is None:
            ctx.issues.append(missing("x", "Required input 'x' is missing (extra_params.x)"))
    if op in ("limit", "taylor_series"):
        raw_point = extra.get("point")
        if raw_point is None:
            if op == "limit":
                ctx.issues.append(missing("point", "Required input 'point' is missing (the value the variable approaches)"))
        else:
            point = _parse_point(raw_point, "point", ctx)
            if point is not None:
                extra["point"] = point
        direction = extra.get("direction")
        if op == "limit" and direction is not None and direction not in ("+", "-", "+-"):
            ctx.issues.append(invalid("direction", "direction must be '+', '-' or '+-'", "invalid_choice"))

    # ---- substitutions for evaluate/arithmetic ---------------------------
    if op in ("evaluate", "arithmetic"):
        subs_raw = extra.get("substitutions")
        subs: dict[str, float] = {}
        bad_subs: set[str] = set()
        if subs_raw is not None:
            if not isinstance(subs_raw, dict):
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("substitutions", "substitutions must map variable names to numbers or quantities", "invalid_type"))
            else:
                for var, raw in subs_raw.items():
                    if var not in declared:
                        ctx.issues.append(invalid("substitutions", f"Substitution for undeclared variable '{var}'", "undeclared_variable")); continue
                    if isinstance(raw, dict):
                        raw = f"{raw.get('value')} {raw.get('unit') or ''}".strip()
                    bad_subs.add(var)
                    if isinstance(raw, bool) or not isinstance(raw, (int, float, str)):
                        ctx.issues.append(invalid("substitutions", f"substitutions['{var}'] must be a number or a quantity such as '5 kg'", "invalid_type")); continue
                    try:
                        notes: list[str] = []
                        q = parse_quantity(str(raw), notes=notes)
                        ctx.note(*notes)
                    except (UnitError, NotationError) as exc:
                        ctx.issues.append(invalid("substitutions", f"substitutions['{var}']: {exc}", "invalid_input")); continue
                    if not math.isfinite(q.value):
                        ctx.issues.append(invalid("substitutions", f"substitutions['{var}'] must be finite", "not_finite")); continue
                    bad_subs.discard(var)
                    if q.unit.symbol:
                        subs[var] = q.to_si()
                        subs_dims[var] = q.unit.dim
                        ctx.conversions.append({"field": f"substitutions.{var}", "from": str(raw),
                                                "to": f"{subs[var]:.12g} (SI)", "value": subs[var]})
                    else:
                        subs[var] = q.value; plain_subs.add(var)
                extra["substitutions"] = subs
        unresolved = sorted(n for n in used_names if n not in subs and n not in bad_subs)
        if norm.get("expression") and unresolved:
            ctx.issues.append(missing("substitutions", f"No value for variable(s): {', '.join(unresolved)}. "
                                                       f"Provide extra_params.substitutions, e.g. {{\"{unresolved[0]}\": 2}}",
                                      "missing_value"))

    # ---- dimensional consistency ----------------------------------------
    if norm and (op in EXPRESSION_OPS or op == "solve_system"):
        _check_dimensions(req, extra, declared, norm, ctx, subs_dims, plain_subs)
    expected_unit = None
    output_unit = spec.output_unit if spec else None
    if spec and op in DOMAIN_SPECS:
        out_dim = DIMENSIONLESS if output_unit == "1" else (parse_unit(output_unit).dim if output_unit else None)
        if out_dim is not None:
            ctx.dimension["result"] = out_dim.name()
        if extra.get("expected_unit") is not None:
            try:
                exp_dim = parse_unit(str(extra["expected_unit"])).dim
                if out_dim is None:
