                    ctx.note("expected_unit ignored: this operation returns several quantities")
                elif exp_dim != out_dim:
                    ctx.issues.append(invalid("expected_unit", f"{op} returns {out_dim} but expected_unit "
                                                               f"'{extra['expected_unit']}' is {exp_dim}", "dimension_mismatch"))
                else:
                    expected_unit = str(extra["expected_unit"])
            except UnitError as exc:
                ctx.issues.append(invalid("expected_unit", str(exc), "unsupported_unit"))

    ok = not ctx.issues
    new_req = req.model_copy(update={**updates, "extra_params": extra}) if ok else req
    return PreflightResult(ok, new_req, ctx.issues, ctx.notes, ctx.conversions, ctx.dimension, expected_unit, output_unit)


def preflight(request: SolverRequest) -> PreflightResult:
    """Run the shared input layer; never raises, never lets unchecked input through on internal error."""
    try:
        return _preflight(request)
    except Exception as exc:                                    # noqa: BLE001 - fail closed
        return PreflightResult(False, request, [invalid(None, f"Input validation failed unexpectedly ({type(exc).__name__}); "
                                                              f"the request was not calculated", "preflight_error")])


def failure_response(request: SolverRequest, result: PreflightResult) -> SolverResponse:
    return SolverResponse(request_id=request.request_id, status="failed", solver=request.solver,
                          error=result.error_message, details={"preflight": result.to_details()})


def _annotate(response: SolverResponse, result: PreflightResult) -> SolverResponse:
    if response.status != "success":
        return response
    details = dict(response.details or {})
    info = result.to_details()
    if result.expected_unit and result.output_unit and response.numeric_result is not None:
        try:
            value = Quantity(response.numeric_result, parse_unit(result.output_unit)).to(result.expected_unit)
            info["converted_result"] = {"value": value, "unit": result.expected_unit}
        except UnitError:
            pass
    if len(info) > 1:                                  # more than just {"ok": true}
        details["preflight"] = info
    return response.model_copy(update={"details": details})


class PreflightSolver:
    """Wraps any solver so that its input always goes through :func:`preflight` first."""

    def __init__(self, inner: Any):
        self._inner = inner
        self.name = inner.name

    def __getattr__(self, item: str) -> Any:               # operations, etc.
        return getattr(self._inner, item)

    def solve(self, request: SolverRequest) -> SolverResponse:
        result = preflight(request)
        if not result.ok:
            return failure_response(request, result)
        return _annotate(self._inner.solve(result.request), result)


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/orchestrator/model_client.py ---

"""Model-client contract and a deterministic development-only mock.

A real Ollama or llama.cpp adapter can implement ModelClient later without
changing the solver or self-healing coordinator.
"""
from typing import Protocol, Sequence


class ModelClient(Protocol):
    def answer_problem(self, question: str, feedback: str | None = None) -> str:
        """Return a proposed numeric answer for a problem."""
        ...


class NoModelConfigured:
    """Explicit placeholder used when no model runtime is configured."""

    def answer_problem(self, question: str, feedback: str | None = None) -> str:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No SLM client is configured. Inject a ModelClient to enable repair attempts."
        )


class ScriptedMockModelClient:
    """Deterministic mock for local demos and tests; NOT an actual language model.

    Each call returns the next configured answer. It is useful for demonstrating
    retries before the team chooses Ollama, llama.cpp, or another runtime.
    """

    def __init__(self, answers: Sequence[str] = ("9",)) -> None:
        if not answers:
            raise ValueError("At least one scripted mock answer is required")
        self._answers = list(answers)
        self._index = 0
        self.calls: list[dict[str, str | None]] = []

    def answer_problem(self, question: str, feedback: str | None = None) -> str:
        self.calls.append({"question": question, "feedback": feedback})
        if self._index >= len(self._answers):
            raise RuntimeError("Scripted mock has no more answers configured")
        answer = self._answers[self._index]
        self._index += 1
        return answer

--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/orchestrator/router.py ---

import re
from app.schemas.models import RouteResponse


class QuestionRouter:
    """Small transparent baseline router; replace/refine during evaluation."""
    OUT_OF_SCOPE_HINTS = ("football", "cricket score", "weather today", "celebrity gossip")
    SOLVER_HINTS = (
        "integrate", "integral", "differentiate", "derivative", "eigenvalue",
        "eigenvalues", "matrix determinant", "calculate", "solve the equation",
    )

    def route(self, question: str) -> RouteResponse:
        normalized = question.lower().strip()
        if any(hint in normalized for hint in self.OUT_OF_SCOPE_HINTS):
            return RouteResponse(mode="out_of_scope", reason="Question appears outside the supported STEM scope")
        if any(hint in normalized for hint in self.SOLVER_HINTS):
            return RouteResponse(mode="problem_solving", reason="Question contains a supported calculation cue")
        # This is a baseline assumption, not semantic understanding. A future
        # classifier/model should improve ambiguous and unsupported cases.
        return RouteResponse(mode="textbook_qa", reason="Default route for conceptual or formula questions")


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/orchestrator/self_healing.py ---

import math

import sympy as sp

from app.inputs.notation import NotationError, split_number_prefix
from app.inputs.units import UnitError, parse_quantity
from app.solvers.core_math import safe_expr
from app.schemas.models import RepairAttempt, SolveResponse, SolverRequest
from app.solvers.dispatcher import SolverDispatcher
from app.orchestrator.model_client import ModelClient


def parse_numeric_answer(answer: str | None, expected_unit: str | None = None) -> float | None:
    """Parse a model answer: ``9``, ``-3``, ``1.5e-3``, ``9×10^2``, ``1/3``, ``sqrt(2)/2``, ``9 N``.

    If the answer carries a unit it is converted to ``expected_unit``; with no known expected unit,
    or with an incompatible one, the answer is NOT accepted (it cannot be verified).
    """
    if answer is None:
        return None
    text = str(answer).strip()
    if not text:
        return None
    try:
        value, rest = split_number_prefix(text)
        if not rest:
            return value if math.isfinite(value) else None
        if rest[:1] in "/+-*^(" or rest[:2] == "**":           # an expression such as 1/3 or 2*pi
            raise NotationError("expression")
        if expected_unit is None:
            return None
        converted = parse_quantity(f"{value!r} {rest}").to(expected_unit)
        return converted if math.isfinite(converted) else None
    except (NotationError, UnitError):
        pass
    try:                                                      # constant expressions, no variables
        expr = safe_expr(text, [])
        if expr.free_symbols:
            return None
        number = float(sp.N(expr))
        return number if math.isfinite(number) else None
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError, ArithmeticError):
        return None


def answers_match(model_answer: str | None, solver_value: float, tolerance: float = 1e-8,
                  expected_unit: str | None = None) -> bool:
    value = parse_numeric_answer(model_answer, expected_unit)
    # small absolute slack only: a wrong "0" must not match a tiny but non-zero result
    return value is not None and math.isclose(value, solver_value, rel_tol=tolerance, abs_tol=1e-12)


def describe_request(request: SolverRequest) -> str:
    parts = [f"Operation: {request.solver}"]
    if request.expression:
        parts.append(f"expression: {request.expression} (variable {request.variable})")
    if request.lower_bound is not None or request.upper_bound is not None:
        parts.append(f"bounds: {request.lower_bound} to {request.upper_bound}")
    if request.extra_params:
        parts.append(f"inputs: {request.extra_params}")
    return "; ".join(parts)


class SelfHealingSolver:
    def __init__(self, dispatcher: SolverDispatcher, model_client: ModelClient | None = None, max_attempts: int = 2):
        if max_attempts < 0 or max_attempts > 5:
            raise ValueError("max_attempts must be between 0 and 5")
        self.dispatcher = dispatcher
        self.model_client = model_client
        self.max_attempts = max_attempts

    def solve(self, request: SolverRequest, initial_model_answer: str | None = None,
              question: str | None = None) -> SolveResponse:
        solver_result = self.dispatcher.dispatch(request)
        if solver_result.status != "success" or solver_result.numeric_result is None:
            return SolveResponse(status="failed", solver_result=solver_result, note="Solver did not produce a usable result; it is not labelled verified.")
        if not solver_result.verification or not solver_result.verification.passed:
            return SolveResponse(status="failed", solver_result=solver_result, note="Solver verification did not pass; result is not labelled verified.")

        expected_unit = (solver_result.details or {}).get("unit")
        if not isinstance(expected_unit, str) or "*" in expected_unit and False:
            expected_unit = None
        attempts: list[RepairAttempt] = []
        proposed = initial_model_answer
        for attempt_no in range(self.max_attempts + 1):
            matched = answers_match(proposed, solver_result.numeric_result, expected_unit=expected_unit)
            feedback = None
            if not matched:
                feedback = f"Your answer did not match the deterministic solver result ({solver_result.exact_result}). Recheck your calculation and answer with a numeric value."
            attempts.append(RepairAttempt(
                attempt=attempt_no + 1,
                model_answer=proposed,
                solver_answer=solver_result.exact_result,
                matched=matched,
                feedback=feedback,
            ))
            if matched:
                return SolveResponse(status="verified", solver_result=solver_result, attempts=attempts)
            if attempt_no >= self.max_attempts or self.model_client is None:
                break
            try:
                proposed = self.model_client.answer_problem(
                    question=question or describe_request(request),
                    feedback=feedback,
                )
            except Exception:
                break

        return SolveResponse(
            status="solver_result_unmatched",
            solver_result=solver_result,
            attempts=attempts,
            note="The solver result passed its checks, but the model answer did not match or no model client was available for repair.",
        )


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/practice/service.py ---

import math
from app.schemas.models import (
    PracticeGenerateRequest, PracticeGradeRequest, PracticeGradeResponse,
    PracticeQuestion, PracticeSet, SolverRequest,
)
from app.solvers.dispatcher import SolverDispatcher


class PracticeService:
    """Small deterministic practice generator for the initial integral solver."""
    TEMPLATES = [
        ("x", 0, 2, "Evaluate the definite integral of x from 0 to 2."),
        ("x**2", 0, 3, "Evaluate the definite integral of x^2 from 0 to 3."),
        ("5", 0, 2, "Evaluate the definite integral of 5 from 0 to 2."),
        ("2*x", 0, 2, "Evaluate the definite integral of 2*x from 0 to 2."),
        ("sin(x)", 0, 1, "Evaluate the definite integral of sin(x) from 0 to 1."),
    ]
    def __init__(self, dispatcher: SolverDispatcher | None = None):
        self.dispatcher = dispatcher or SolverDispatcher()

    def generate(self, request: PracticeGenerateRequest) -> PracticeSet:
        # Fixed, deterministic examples for the first milestone. Replace or
        # expand with corpus-grounded/model-generated questions later.
        questions = []
        for index, (expr, lower, upper, prompt) in enumerate(self.TEMPLATES[:request.count], start=1):
            solver_request = SolverRequest(
                request_id=f"practice-{index}", solver="definite_integral",
                expression=expr, variable="x", lower_bound=lower, upper_bound=upper,
            )
            result = self.dispatcher.dispatch(solver_request)
            if result.status != "success" or result.numeric_result is None:
                continue
            questions.append(PracticeQuestion(
                question_id=f"integral-{index}", prompt=prompt, solver_request=solver_request,
            ))
        return PracticeSet(questions=questions)

    def grade(self, request: PracticeGradeRequest) -> PracticeGradeResponse:
        try:
            index = int(request.question_id.removeprefix("integral-")) - 1
            if index < 0 or index >= len(self.TEMPLATES):
                raise ValueError
            expr, lower, upper, _ = self.TEMPLATES[index]
        except (ValueError, IndexError):
            raise ValueError("Unknown practice question ID")
        solver_request = SolverRequest(
            request_id=f"grade-{request.question_id}", solver="definite_integral",
            expression=expr, variable="x", lower_bound=lower, upper_bound=upper,
        )
        result = self.dispatcher.dispatch(solver_request)
        if result.status != "success" or result.numeric_result is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Could not calculate the answer key")
        expected = result.numeric_result
        correct = math.isclose(
            request.submitted_value, expected,
            rel_tol=request.tolerance, abs_tol=request.tolerance,
        )
        return PracticeGradeResponse(
            correct=correct, expected_value=expected, submitted_value=request.submitted_value,
            feedback="Correct!" if correct else "Not quite. Review the steps and try again.",
        )

import os

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from app.schemas.models import (
    PracticeGenerateRequest, PracticeGradeRequest, PracticeGradeResponse,
    PracticeSet, RouteRequest, RouteResponse, SolveRequest, SolveResponse,
    SolverRequest, SolverResponse,
)
from app.orchestrator.router import QuestionRouter
from app.orchestrator.self_healing import SelfHealingSolver
from app.orchestrator.model_client import ScriptedMockModelClient
from app.practice.service import PracticeService
from app.solvers.dispatcher import SolverDispatcher
from app.solvers.domain_stem import interpret_question

app = FastAPI(title="StemMatrix Person B API", version="0.4.0")


@app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
async def validation_error_handler(request: Request, exc: RequestValidationError):
    """422 with JSON-safe details (the default handler echoes the raw input, which crashes on NaN/Infinity)."""
    errors = [{"loc": list(e.get("loc", ())), "msg": str(e.get("msg", "")), "type": str(e.get("type", ""))} for e in exc.errors()]
    return JSONResponse(status_code=422, content={"detail": errors})


router = QuestionRouter()
dispatcher = SolverDispatcher()

# Default is safely disabled. The scripted mock is for demos/tests only and is
# never presented as a real SLM. A real runtime adapter can be plugged in later.
model_mode = os.getenv("STEMMATRIX_MODEL", "none").strip().lower()
model_client = None
if model_mode == "mock":
    raw_answers = os.getenv("STEMMATRIX_MOCK_ANSWERS", "9")
    scripted_answers = [answer.strip() for answer in raw_answers.split(",") if answer.strip()]
    if scripted_answers:
        model_client = ScriptedMockModelClient(scripted_answers)
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("STEMMATRIX_MOCK_ANSWERS must contain at least one answer")
elif model_mode != "none":
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Unsupported STEMMATRIX_MODEL value. Use 'none' or 'mock' for now; "
        "the real local-model adapter has not been selected yet."
    )

healing_solver = SelfHealingSolver(dispatcher=dispatcher, model_client=model_client, max_attempts=2)
practice_service = PracticeService(dispatcher)


@app.get("/api/health")
def health():
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "model_configured": model_client is not None,
        "model_mode": model_mode,
        "model_note": (
            "Scripted mock only; not a real SLM"
            if model_mode == "mock"
            else "No model client configured"
        ),
        "solvers": dispatcher.registry.names(),
    }


@app.post("/api/route", response_model=RouteResponse)
def route_question(request: RouteRequest):
    return router.route(request.question)


@app.post("/api/solvers/dispatch", response_model=SolverResponse)
def dispatch_solver(request: SolverRequest):
    return dispatcher.dispatch(request)


@app.post("/api/interpret")
def interpret_natural_language(request: RouteRequest):
    """Map supported natural-language STEM prompts to a deterministic solver request.

    Returns a clarification response when the question is ambiguous or unsupported.
    """
    import uuid
    parsed = interpret_question(request.question)
    if parsed.get("status") == "needs_clarification":
        return parsed
    payload = {"request_id": "nl-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12], **parsed}
    payload.pop("confidence", None)
    try:
        solver_request = SolverRequest(**payload)
    except Exception as exc:
        return {"status": "needs_clarification", "message": f"Could not form a valid solver request: {exc}", "interpretation": parsed}
    response = dispatcher.dispatch(solver_request)
    return {"interpretation": parsed, "solver_response": response.model_dump()}


@app.post("/api/solve", response_model=SolveResponse)
def solve(request: SolveRequest):
    return healing_solver.solve(request.solver_request, initial_model_answer=request.model_answer, question=request.question)


@app.post("/api/practice", response_model=PracticeSet)
def generate_practice(request: PracticeGenerateRequest):
    return practice_service.generate(request)


@app.post("/api/practice/grade", response_model=PracticeGradeResponse)
def grade_practice(request: PracticeGradeRequest):
    try:
        return practice_service.grade(request)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc

from typing import Any, Literal
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator
import math

# Solver names are validated by the registry at execution time so new solver
# capabilities can be added without editing this schema for every operation.
SolverName = Literal['definite_integral', 'evaluate', 'arithmetic', 'simplify', 'expand', 'factor', 'fraction_simplify', 'percentage', 'gcd_lcm', 'convert_number_base', 'solve_equation', 'solve_system', 'polynomial_roots', 'partial_fraction', 'sequence_sum', 'binomial_expansion', 'differentiate', 'partial_differentiate', 'indefinite_integral', 'limit', 'taylor_series', 'matrix_add', 'matrix_multiply', 'matrix_transpose', 'matrix_determinant', 'matrix_inverse', 'matrix_rank', 'matrix_eigenvalues', 'solve_linear_system', 'vector_dot', 'vector_cross', 'vector_norm', 'numerical_root', 'bisection_root', 'newton_root', 'numerical_integral', 'trapezoid_integral', 'simpson_integral', 'interpolate', 'linear_regression', 'descriptive_stats', 'mean', 'median', 'mode', 'variance', 'standard_deviation', 'z_score', 'normal_probability', 'binomial_probability', 'correlation', 'percentile', 'unit_convert', 'physics_kinematics', 'physics_force', 'physics_weight', 'physics_kinetic_energy', 'physics_potential_energy', 'physics_momentum', 'physics_density', 'physics_pressure', 'physics_ohms_law', 'physics_electrical_power', 'physics_wave_speed', 'physics_ideal_gas', 'physics_work', 'physics_hydrostatic_pressure', 'physics_heat_energy', 'chemistry_molar_mass', 'chemistry_molarity', 'chemistry_dilution', 'chemistry_ideal_gas', 'chemistry_ph', 'chemistry_balance_equation', 'chemistry_stoichiometry', 'engineering_reynolds', 'engineering_heat_transfer', 'engineering_efficiency', 'engineering_voltage_divider', 'engineering_bending_stress', 'engineering_flow_rate', 'engineering_safety_factor']
QuestionMode = Literal["textbook_qa", "problem_solving", "out_of_scope"]


class SolverRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", strict=True)

    request_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)
    solver: SolverName
    # General-purpose structured input. Existing integration request fields remain
    # supported for backwards compatibility.
    expression: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=1000)
    variable: str = Field(default="x", pattern=r"^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$")
    lower_bound: int | float | None = None
    upper_bound: int | float | None = None
    equations: list[str] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=30)
    variables: list[str] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=20)
    matrix: list[list[float]] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=50)
    vector: list[float] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=50)
    values: list[float] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=10000)
    data: list[float] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=10000)
    target: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=80)
    method: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=40)
    initial_guess: float | None = None
    tolerance: float = Field(default=1e-8, gt=0, le=0.1)
    max_iterations: int = Field(default=100, ge=1, le=10000)
    degree: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=20)
    probabilities: list[float] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=1000)
    weights: list[float] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=10000)
    extra_params: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def finite_numeric_inputs(self):
        for name in ("lower_bound", "upper_bound", "initial_guess", "tolerance"):
            value = getattr(self, name)
            if value is not None and not math.isfinite(float(value)):
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite")
        for name in ("matrix", "vector", "values", "data", "weights", "probabilities"):
            values = getattr(self, name)
            if values is not None:
                flattened = [v for row in values for v in row] if name == "matrix" else values
                if any(not math.isfinite(float(v)) for v in flattened):
                    raise ValueError(f"{name} must contain only finite numbers")
        return self


class VerificationResult(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    passed: bool
    message: str


class SolverResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    request_id: str
    status: Literal["success", "failed"]
    solver: str
    exact_result: str | None = None
    numeric_result: float | None = None
    result: Any | None = None
    details: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    verification: VerificationResult | None = None
    error: str | None = None


class RouteRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    question: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class RouteResponse(BaseModel):
    mode: QuestionMode
    reason: str


class SolveRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    question: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)
    solver_request: SolverRequest
    model_answer: str | None = None


class RepairAttempt(BaseModel):
    attempt: int
    model_answer: str | None
    solver_answer: str | None
    matched: bool
    feedback: str | None = None


class SolveResponse(BaseModel):
    status: Literal["verified", "solver_result_unmatched", "failed"]
    solver_result: SolverResponse
    attempts: list[RepairAttempt] = Field(default_factory=list)
    note: str | None = None


class PracticeGenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    topic: Literal["definite_integral"] = "definite_integral"
    count: int = Field(default=3, ge=1, le=10)


class PracticeQuestion(BaseModel):
    question_id: str
    prompt: str
    solver_request: SolverRequest


class PracticeSet(BaseModel):
    questions: list[PracticeQuestion]


class PracticeGradeRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    question_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    submitted_value: float = Field(allow_inf_nan=False)
    # Capped: a tolerance near 1 would accept almost any answer as "correct".
    tolerance: float = Field(default=1e-6, ge=0, le=0.05, allow_inf_nan=False)


class PracticeGradeResponse(BaseModel):
    correct: bool
    expected_value: float
    submitted_value: float
    feedback: str

--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/solvers/dispatcher.py ---

from app.schemas.models import SolverRequest, SolverResponse
from app.solvers.registry import SolverRegistry


class SolverDispatcher:
    def __init__(self, registry: SolverRegistry | None = None):
        self.registry = registry or SolverRegistry()

    def dispatch(self, request: SolverRequest) -> SolverResponse:
        try:
            solver = self.registry.get(request.solver)
        except ValueError as exc:
            return SolverResponse(
                request_id=request.request_id,
                status="failed",
                solver=request.solver,
                error=str(exc),
            )
        return solver.solve(request)


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/solvers/registry.py ---

from app.inputs.preflight import PreflightSolver
from app.solvers.base import Solver
from app.solvers.core_math import CoreMathSolver
from app.solvers.integration import DefiniteIntegralSolver
from app.solvers.domain_stem import DomainSTEMSolver


class SolverRegistry:
    """Maps public operation names to solver implementations."""
    def __init__(self, solvers: list[Solver] | None = None):
        self._solvers: dict[str, Solver] = {}
        if solvers is not None:
            for solver in solvers:
                self.register(solver)
        else:
            self.register(DefiniteIntegralSolver())
            domain = DomainSTEMSolver()
            for operation in sorted(domain.operations):
                self.register_alias(operation, domain)
            core = CoreMathSolver()
            for operation in sorted(core.operations):
                self.register_alias(operation, core)

    def register(self, solver: Solver) -> None:
        self.register_alias(solver.name, solver)

    def register_alias(self, name: str, solver: Solver) -> None:
        if name in self._solvers:
            raise ValueError(f"Solver already registered: {name}")
        # Every solver is wrapped so its input always passes through the shared preflight.
        self._solvers[name] = solver if isinstance(solver, PreflightSolver) else PreflightSolver(solver)

    def get(self, name: str) -> Solver:
        try:
            return self._solvers[name]
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported solver: {name}") from exc

    def names(self) -> list[str]:
        return sorted(self._solvers)
