--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/solvers/integration.py ---

"""Restricted-expression definite integration using SymPy.

Expressions are parsed through Python's AST and only a small allow-list of
operators/functions is accepted. Do not replace this with eval().
"""
import ast
import math
import operator

import sympy as sp

from app.schemas.models import SolverRequest, SolverResponse, VerificationResult

BINARY_OPERATORS = {
    ast.Add: operator.add,
    ast.Sub: operator.sub,
    ast.Mult: operator.mul,
    ast.Div: operator.truediv,
    ast.Pow: operator.pow,
}
UNARY_OPERATORS = {ast.UAdd: operator.pos, ast.USub: operator.neg}
ALLOWED_FUNCTIONS = {
    "sin": sp.sin,
    "cos": sp.cos,
    "tan": sp.tan,
    "exp": sp.exp,
    "log": sp.log,
    "sqrt": sp.sqrt,
}


def parse_expression(expression: str, variable: str):
    if len(expression) > 300:
        raise ValueError("Expression is too long")
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(expression, mode="eval")
    except (SyntaxError, ValueError, MemoryError, RecursionError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("Expression could not be parsed") from exc

    symbol = sp.Symbol(variable)
    node_count = sum(1 for _ in ast.walk(tree))
    if node_count > 100:
        raise ValueError("Expression is too complex")

    def visit(node):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Expression):
            return visit(node.body)
        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
            if isinstance(node.value, bool) or not isinstance(node.value, (int, float)):
                raise ValueError("Only numeric constants are allowed")
            if isinstance(node.value, float) and not math.isfinite(node.value):
                raise ValueError("Non-finite constants are not allowed")
            return sp.sympify(str(node.value))
        if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
            if node.id == variable:
                return symbol
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported name: {node.id}")
        if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
            operation = BINARY_OPERATORS.get(type(node.op))
            if operation is None:
                raise ValueError("Unsupported arithmetic operator")
            left, right = visit(node.left), visit(node.right)
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow) and right.is_number:
                try:
                    if abs(float(right)) > 20:
                        raise ValueError("Exponent magnitude is limited to 20")
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    raise ValueError("Unsupported exponent")
            return operation(left, right)
        if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp):
            operation = UNARY_OPERATORS.get(type(node.op))
            if operation is None:
                raise ValueError("Unsupported unary operator")
            return operation(visit(node.operand))
        if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
            if not isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) or node.keywords:
                raise ValueError("Unsupported function call")
            function = ALLOWED_FUNCTIONS.get(node.func.id)
            if function is None or len(node.args) != 1:
                raise ValueError("Only supported one-argument functions are allowed")
            return function(visit(node.args[0]))
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported expression element: {type(node).__name__}")

    return visit(tree)


class DefiniteIntegralSolver:
    name = "definite_integral"

    def solve(self, request: SolverRequest) -> SolverResponse:
        try:
            if request.solver != self.name:
                raise ValueError("Request uses the wrong solver")
            if request.lower_bound is None or request.upper_bound is None:
                raise ValueError("Both lower_bound and upper_bound are required")
            if request.lower_bound > request.upper_bound:
                raise ValueError("Lower bound must not exceed upper bound")

            x = sp.Symbol(request.variable)
            expression = parse_expression(request.expression, request.variable)
            result = sp.integrate(expression, (x, request.lower_bound, request.upper_bound))
            if result.has(sp.Integral) or result.is_finite is not True:
                raise ValueError("Could not obtain a finite exact result")

            numeric = float(sp.N(result, 15))
            if not math.isfinite(numeric):
                raise ValueError("Result is not a finite numeric value")

            # This consistency check uses SymPy's antiderivative too; it is a
            # useful sanity check, not an independent proof of correctness.
            antiderivative = sp.integrate(expression, x)
            endpoint_result = (
                antiderivative.subs(x, request.upper_bound)
                - antiderivative.subs(x, request.lower_bound)
            )
            passed = sp.simplify(endpoint_result - result) == 0

            return SolverResponse(
                request_id=request.request_id,
                status="success",
                solver=self.name,
                exact_result=str(result),
                numeric_result=numeric,
                verification=VerificationResult(
                    passed=bool(passed),
                    message="Symbolic consistency check passed" if passed else "Symbolic consistency check failed",
                ),
            )
        except (ValueError, TypeError, SyntaxError, ArithmeticError, OverflowError, RecursionError) as exc:
            return SolverResponse(
                request_id=request.request_id,
                status="failed",
                solver=self.name,
                error=str(exc),
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            # Avoid leaking internals in the API response. Log details in a
            # production deployment; this starter returns a generic message.
            return SolverResponse(
                request_id=request.request_id,
                status="failed",
                solver=self.name,
                error=f"Solver failed ({type(exc).__name__})",
            )


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/solvers/domain_stem.py ---

"""Deterministic physics, chemistry, engineering and unit-conversion solvers.

All inputs are explicit. Results include formula/assumption metadata; they are educational
calculations and not a substitute for validated safety-critical design tools.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import math, re
from typing import Any
import numpy as np
import sympy as sp
from app.schemas.models import SolverRequest, SolverResponse, VerificationResult

ATOMIC_WEIGHTS = {
'H':1.008,'He':4.002602,'Li':6.94,'Be':9.0121831,'B':10.81,'C':12.011,'N':14.007,'O':15.999,'F':18.998403,'Ne':20.1797,
'Na':22.989769,'Mg':24.305,'Al':26.981538,'Si':28.085,'P':30.973762,'S':32.06,'Cl':35.45,'Ar':39.948,'K':39.0983,'Ca':40.078,
'Sc':44.955908,'Ti':47.867,'V':50.9415,'Cr':51.9961,'Mn':54.938044,'Fe':55.845,'Co':58.933194,'Ni':58.6934,'Cu':63.546,'Zn':65.38,
'Ga':69.723,'Ge':72.630,'As':74.921595,'Se':78.971,'Br':79.904,'Kr':83.798,'Rb':85.4678,'Sr':87.62,'Y':88.90584,'Zr':91.224,
'Nb':92.90637,'Mo':95.95,'Ru':101.07,'Rh':102.9055,'Pd':106.42,'Ag':107.8682,'Cd':112.414,'In':114.818,'Sn':118.710,'Sb':121.760,
'Te':127.60,'I':126.90447,'Xe':131.293,'Cs':132.90545,'Ba':137.327,'La':138.90547,'Ce':140.116,'W':183.84,'Pt':195.084,'Au':196.96657,
'Hg':200.592,'Tl':204.38,'Pb':207.2,'Bi':208.9804,'U':238.02891,
}

def convert_units(value, from_unit, to_unit, interval=False):
    """Convert between compatible units using the shared unit parser (dimension-checked)."""
    from app.inputs.units import UnitError, convert, parse_unit
    try: val=float(value)
    except (TypeError,ValueError) as e: raise ValueError('value must be numeric') from e
    notes=[]
    fu=parse_unit(str(from_unit),notes=notes); tu=parse_unit(str(to_unit),expected=fu.dim,notes=notes)
    out=convert(val,str(from_unit),str(to_unit),delta=interval)
    if not math.isfinite(out): raise ValueError('Conversion result is non-finite')
    res={'value':out,'unit':str(to_unit).strip(),'dimension':fu.dim.name(),'input_value':val,'input_unit':str(from_unit).strip()}
    if notes: res['notes']=notes
    return res

def _p(r,k,default=None): return r.extra_params.get(k,default)
def _nums(r,*keys):
    out={}
    for k in keys:
        v=_p(r,k)
        if v is None: raise ValueError(f'Missing required extra_params.{k}')
        if isinstance(v,bool): raise ValueError(f'extra_params.{k} must be numeric, not a boolean')
        try: v=float(v)
        except (TypeError,ValueError) as e: raise ValueError(f'extra_params.{k} must be numeric') from e
        if not math.isfinite(v): raise ValueError(f'extra_params.{k} must be finite')
        out[k]=v
    return out

def _resp(r,result,details=None,passed=True,message='Formula evaluated; input assumptions must hold'):
    numeric=float(result) if isinstance(result,(int,float,np.number)) and math.isfinite(float(result)) else None
    return SolverResponse(request_id=r.request_id,status='success',solver=r.solver,result=result,exact_result=str(result) if not isinstance(result,dict) else None,numeric_result=numeric,details=details or {},verification=VerificationResult(passed=bool(passed),message=message))

def formula_counts(formula: str) -> dict[str,int]:
    """Element counts of a formula such as Fe2(SO4)3 or CuSO4.5H2O (hydrate parts after '.')."""
    from app.inputs.notation import normalize_chemical_formula, NotationError
    try: f=normalize_chemical_formula(formula)
    except NotationError as e: raise ValueError(str(e)) from e
    total: dict[str,int]={}
    for part in f.split('.'):
        m=re.match(r'^(\d+)(?=[A-Z(])',part); mult=1
        if m: mult=int(m.group(1)); part=part[m.end():]
        if not part: raise ValueError(f'Empty part in formula {formula!r}')
        toks=re.findall(r'([A-Z][a-z]?|\(|\)|\d+)',part)
        if ''.join(toks)!=part: raise ValueError(f'Unsupported formula notation in {part!r}; use element symbols, digit subscripts, parentheses and hydrate dots')
        def group(i=0,nested=False):
            d={}
            while i<len(toks):
                t=toks[i]
                if t==')':
                    if not nested: raise ValueError('Unmatched closing parenthesis')
                    return d,i+1
                if t=='(':
                    sub,i=group(i+1,True); k=1
                    if i<len(toks) and toks[i].isdigit(): k=int(toks[i]); i+=1
                    for e,c in sub.items(): d[e]=d.get(e,0)+c*k
                    continue
                if t.isdigit(): raise ValueError(f'Unexpected number {t} in formula {formula!r}')
                if t not in ATOMIC_WEIGHTS: raise ValueError(f'Unknown/unsupported element: {t}')
                i+=1; k=1
                if i<len(toks) and toks[i].isdigit(): k=int(toks[i]); i+=1
                if k==0: raise ValueError('Subscript 0 is not valid in a formula')
                d[t]=d.get(t,0)+k
            if nested: raise ValueError('Unmatched opening parenthesis')
            return d,i
        counts,end=group()
        if end!=len(toks): raise ValueError('Could not parse complete formula')
        for e,c in counts.items(): total[e]=total.get(e,0)+c*mult
    return total

class DomainSTEMSolver:
    name='domain_stem'
    operations={
      'unit_convert','physics_kinematics','physics_force','physics_weight','physics_kinetic_energy','physics_potential_energy','physics_momentum','physics_density','physics_pressure','physics_ohms_law','physics_electrical_power','physics_wave_speed','physics_ideal_gas','physics_work','physics_hydrostatic_pressure','physics_heat_energy',
      'chemistry_molar_mass','chemistry_molarity','chemistry_dilution','chemistry_ideal_gas','chemistry_ph','chemistry_balance_equation','chemistry_stoichiometry',
      'engineering_reynolds','engineering_heat_transfer','engineering_efficiency','engineering_voltage_divider','engineering_bending_stress','engineering_flow_rate','engineering_safety_factor'}
    def solve(self,r):
      try:
        op=r.solver; p=r.extra_params
        if op=='unit_convert':
          q=_nums(r,'value'); return _resp(r,convert_units(q['value'],p.get('from_unit',''),p.get('to_unit',''),bool(p.get('interval',False))),{ 'dimension_check':'passed'})
        if op=='physics_kinematics':
          u,v,a,t,s=(_p(r,k) for k in ('initial_velocity','final_velocity','acceleration','time','displacement'))
          cand=[]
          if v is None and all(z is not None for z in (u,a,t)): cand.append(('final_velocity',float(u)+float(a)*float(t),'v = u + a*t'))
          if u is None and all(z is not None for z in (v,a,t)): cand.append(('initial_velocity',float(v)-float(a)*float(t),'u = v - a*t'))
          if a is None and all(z is not None for z in (v,u,t)) and float(t)!=0: cand.append(('acceleration',(float(v)-float(u))/float(t),'a = (v-u)/t'))
          if t is None and all(z is not None for z in (v,u,a)) and float(a)!=0: cand.append(('time',(float(v)-float(u))/float(a),'t = (v-u)/a'))
          if s is None and all(z is not None for z in (u,a,t)): cand.append(('displacement',float(u)*float(t)+.5*float(a)*float(t)**2,'s = u*t + 0.5*a*t^2'))
          if not cand: raise ValueError('Provide inputs sufficient to solve one missing kinematics quantity')
          target=_p(r,'target'); chosen=next((c for c in cand if c[0]==target),cand[0]); return _resp(r,{'quantity':chosen[0],'value':chosen[1]},{'formula':chosen[2],'assumption':'constant acceleration; consistent units required'})
        if op=='physics_force':
          q=_nums(r,'mass','acceleration'); val=q['mass']*q['acceleration']; return _resp(r,val,{'formula':'F=m*a','unit':'N','residual':abs(val-q['mass']*q['acceleration'])})
        if op=='physics_weight':
          q=_nums(r,'mass'); g=float(_p(r,'gravity',9.80665)); return _resp(r,q['mass']*g,{'formula':'W=m*g','unit':'N','gravity_m_s2':g})
        if op in ('physics_kinetic_energy','physics_momentum'):
          q=_nums(r,'mass','velocity'); val=.5*q['mass']*q['velocity']**2 if op=='physics_kinetic_energy' else q['mass']*q['velocity']; return _resp(r,val,{'formula':'KE=0.5*m*v^2' if op=='physics_kinetic_energy' else 'p=m*v','unit':'J' if op=='physics_kinetic_energy' else 'kg*m/s'})
        if op=='physics_potential_energy':
          q=_nums(r,'mass','height'); g=float(_p(r,'gravity',9.80665)); return _resp(r,q['mass']*g*q['height'],{'formula':'PE=m*g*h','unit':'J'})
        if op=='physics_density':
          q=_nums(r,'mass','volume');
          if q['volume']<=0: raise ValueError('volume must be positive')
          return _resp(r,q['mass']/q['volume'],{'formula':'rho=m/V','unit':'kg/m^3'})
        if op=='physics_pressure':
          q=_nums(r,'force','area');
          if q['area']<=0: raise ValueError('area must be positive')
          return _resp(r,q['force']/q['area'],{'formula':'P=F/A','unit':'Pa'})
        if op=='physics_ohms_law':
          d={k:_p(r,k) for k in ('voltage','current','resistance')}; missing=[k for k,v in d.items() if v is None]
          if len(missing)!=1: raise ValueError('Provide exactly two of voltage, current, resistance')
          v,i,rr=(float(d[k]) if d[k] is not None else None for k in ('voltage','current','resistance'))
          if missing[0]=='voltage': name,val,form='voltage',i*rr,'V=I*R'
          elif missing[0]=='current':
            if rr==0: raise ValueError('resistance must be non-zero')
            name,val,form='current',v/rr,'I=V/R'
          else:
            if i==0: raise ValueError('current must be non-zero')
            name,val,form='resistance',v/i,'R=V/I'
          return _resp(r,{'quantity':name,'value':val},{'formula':form})
        if op=='physics_electrical_power':
          v,i,rr=(_p(r,k) for k in ('voltage','current','resistance'))
          if v is not None and i is not None: val=float(v)*float(i); form='P=V*I'
          elif i is not None and rr is not None: val=float(i)**2*float(rr); form='P=I^2*R'
          elif v is not None and rr is not None:
            if float(rr)==0: raise ValueError('resistance must be non-zero')
            val=float(v)**2/float(rr); form='P=V^2/R'
          else: raise ValueError('Provide voltage and current, current and resistance, or voltage and resistance')
          return _resp(r,val,{'formula':form,'unit':'W'})
        if op=='physics_wave_speed':
          q=_nums(r,'frequency','wavelength'); return _resp(r,q['frequency']*q['wavelength'],{'formula':'v=f*lambda','unit':'m/s'})
        if op=='physics_ideal_gas':
          q=_nums(r,'pressure','volume','moles','temperature');
          if min(q['volume'],q['moles'],q['temperature'])<=0: raise ValueError('volume, moles, and absolute temperature must be positive')
          R=float(_p(r,'gas_constant',8.31446261815324)); lhs=q['pressure']*q['volume']; rhs=q['moles']*R*q['temperature']; residual=lhs-rhs; rel=abs(residual)/max(abs(lhs),abs(rhs),1.0)
          return _resp(r,{'PV':lhs,'nRT':rhs},{'formula':'P*V=n*R*T','residual':residual,'relative_residual':rel,'units':'Pa, m^3, mol, K'},rel<=1e-6,'Ideal-gas equality residual checked; SI units required')
        if op=='physics_work':
          q=_nums(r,'force','displacement'); val=q['force']*q['displacement']*math.cos(math.radians(float(_p(r,'angle_degrees',0)))); return _resp(r,val,{'formula':'W=F*s*cos(theta)','unit':'J'})
        if op=='physics_hydrostatic_pressure':
          q=_nums(r,'density','depth'); return _resp(r,q['density']*float(_p(r,'gravity',9.80665))*q['depth'],{'formula':'p=rho*g*h','unit':'Pa'})
        if op=='physics_heat_energy':
          q=_nums(r,'mass','specific_heat','temperature_change'); return _resp(r,q['mass']*q['specific_heat']*q['temperature_change'],{'formula':'Q=m*c*delta_T','unit':'J'})
        if op=='chemistry_molar_mass':
          formula=str(_p(r,'formula','')).strip()
          if not formula: raise ValueError('Provide formula, e.g. H2O')
          counts=formula_counts(formula); mass=sum(ATOMIC_WEIGHTS[e]*c for e,c in counts.items())
          return _resp(r,{'molar_mass_g_mol':mass,'formula':formula},{'unit':'g/mol','element_counts':counts,'atomic_weights':'standard atomic weights for common elements; unsupported elements rejected'})
        if op=='chemistry_molarity':
          q=_nums(r,'moles','volume_liters');
          if q['moles']<0 or q['volume_liters']<=0: raise ValueError('moles must be non-negative and volume_liters positive')
          return _resp(r,q['moles']/q['volume_liters'],{'formula':'M=n/V','unit':'mol/L'})
        if op=='chemistry_dilution':
          q=_nums(r,'initial_concentration','initial_volume','final_volume');
          if q['initial_volume']<0 or q['final_volume']<=0: raise ValueError('initial_volume must be non-negative and final_volume positive')
          return _resp(r,q['initial_concentration']*q['initial_volume']/q['final_volume'],{'formula':'C1*V1=C2*V2','unit':'same concentration unit as C1'})
        if op=='chemistry_ideal_gas':
          q=_nums(r,'pressure','volume','temperature');
          if q['volume']<=0 or q['temperature']<=0: raise ValueError('volume and temperature in kelvin must be positive')
          R=float(_p(r,'gas_constant',0.08205736608096)); return _resp(r,q['pressure']*q['volume']/(R*q['temperature']),{'formula':'n=PV/(RT)','unit':'mol','units':'L, atm, K'})
        if op=='chemistry_ph':
          q=_nums(r,'concentration');
          if q['concentration']<=0: raise ValueError('concentration must be positive')
          return _resp(r,-math.log10(q['concentration']),{'formula':'pH=-log10([H+])','assumption':'strong acid approximation; concentration in mol/L'})
        if op=='chemistry_balance_equation':
          from app.inputs.notation import normalize_chemical_equation, NotationError
          try: lhs,rhs,_=normalize_chemical_equation(str(_p(r,'equation','')).replace('->','→'))
          except NotationError as e: raise ValueError(str(e)) from e
          species=lhs+rhs
          cs=[formula_counts(f) for f in species]; elements=sorted({e for d in cs for e in d})
          mat=sp.Matrix([[d.get(e,0)*(1 if j<len(lhs) else -1) for j,d in enumerate(cs)] for e in elements]); ns=mat.nullspace()
          if not ns: raise ValueError('No balancing solution found')
          if len(ns)>1: raise ValueError('Equation has more than one independent balance (it combines several reactions); balance each reaction separately')
          vec=ns[0]
          if any(v<=0 for v in vec): raise ValueError('Could not find positive coefficients for this equation')
          den=sp.ilcm(*[v.q for v in vec]); coeff=[int(v*den) for v in vec]; g=math.gcd(*coeff); coeff=[v//g for v in coeff]
          out=lambda ss,cc:' + '.join((str(c) if c!=1 else '')+s for s,c in zip(ss,cc))
          return _resp(r,{'balanced_equation':out(lhs,coeff[:len(lhs)])+' -> '+out(rhs,coeff[len(lhs):]),'coefficients':coeff},{'elements_balanced':elements,'method':'integer nullspace'})
        if op=='chemistry_stoichiometry':
          q=_nums(r,'given_moles','given_coefficient','target_coefficient');
          if q['given_moles']<0 or min(q['given_coefficient'],q['target_coefficient'])<=0: raise ValueError('moles must be non-negative and coefficients positive')
          return _resp(r,q['given_moles']*q['target_coefficient']/q['given_coefficient'],{'formula':'n_target=n_given*coefficient_target/coefficient_given','unit':'mol','assumption':'balanced equation and limiting reagent considered separately'})
        if op=='engineering_reynolds':
          q=_nums(r,'density','velocity','characteristic_length','dynamic_viscosity');
          if min(q['density'],q['characteristic_length'],q['dynamic_viscosity'])<=0: raise ValueError('density, length, and viscosity must be positive')
          val=q['density']*abs(q['velocity'])*q['characteristic_length']/q['dynamic_viscosity']; return _resp(r,val,{'formula':'Re=rho*v*L/mu','unit':'dimensionless','regime_hint':'thresholds depend on geometry/flow'})
        if op=='engineering_heat_transfer':
          q=_nums(r,'heat_transfer_coefficient','area','temperature_difference');
          if min(q['heat_transfer_coefficient'],q['area'])<0: raise ValueError('coefficient and area must be non-negative')
          return _resp(r,q['heat_transfer_coefficient']*q['area']*q['temperature_difference'],{'formula':'Q_dot=h*A*delta_T','unit':'W'})
        if op=='engineering_efficiency':
          q=_nums(r,'useful_output','total_input');
          if q['total_input']<=0 or q['useful_output']<0: raise ValueError('input must be positive and output non-negative')
          val=100*q['useful_output']/q['total_input']; return _resp(r,val,{'formula':'efficiency=output/input*100','unit':'%','physical_range_check':val<=100},val<=100+1e-9,'Efficiency above 100% flags possible input inconsistency')
        if op=='engineering_voltage_divider':
          q=_nums(r,'input_voltage','r1','r2');
          if min(q['r1'],q['r2'])<0 or q['r1']+q['r2']==0: raise ValueError('resistances must be non-negative and not both zero')
          return _resp(r,q['input_voltage']*q['r2']/(q['r1']+q['r2']),{'formula':'Vout=Vin*R2/(R1+R2)','unit':'V','assumption':'unloaded ideal divider'})
        if op=='engineering_bending_stress':
          q=_nums(r,'bending_moment','second_moment_area','distance_from_neutral_axis');
          if q['second_moment_area']<=0: raise ValueError('second_moment_area must be positive')
          return _resp(r,abs(q['bending_moment']*q['distance_from_neutral_axis']/q['second_moment_area']),{'formula':'sigma=M*y/I','unit':'Pa','assumption':'linear elastic beam; magnitude only'})
        if op=='engineering_flow_rate':
          q=_nums(r,'area','average_velocity');
          if q['area']<0: raise ValueError('area must be non-negative')
          return _resp(r,q['area']*q['average_velocity'],{'formula':'Q=A*v','unit':'m^3/s'})
        if op=='engineering_safety_factor':
          q=_nums(r,'failure_strength','working_stress');
          if min(q.values())<=0: raise ValueError('strength and working stress must be positive')
          return _resp(r,q['failure_strength']/q['working_stress'],{'formula':'FoS=strength/stress','unit':'dimensionless'})
        raise ValueError(f'Unsupported STEM operation: {op}')
      except (ValueError,TypeError,KeyError,ZeroDivisionError,OverflowError) as e:  # UnitError subclasses ValueError
        return SolverResponse(request_id=r.request_id,status='failed',solver=r.solver,error=str(e))


_NUM=r'(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?)'
_UNIT=r'([A-Za-z°µμΩ][A-Za-z°µμΩ/^0-9²³⁻·*\-]*)'
_UNITP=r'[A-Za-z°µμΩ][A-Za-z°µμΩ/^0-9²³⁻·*\-]*(?:\s+per\s+[A-Za-z]+)?'

def _qty(num,unit):
    """Keep the unit the user wrote (case-sensitive) so the shared unit layer can convert it."""
    return f"{num} {unit}".strip() if unit else float(num)

def interpret_question(question: str) -> dict[str,Any]:
    """Conservative NL-to-solver mapping for a documented set of templates.

    Quantities keep their units (e.g. ``"10 g"``); the shared preflight converts them to the
    unit each solver expects, so wording like "mass 500 g" is handled correctly.
    """
    try:
      return _interpret(question)
    except (ValueError,TypeError):
      return _clarify()

def _clarify():
    return {'status':'needs_clarification','message':'I could not safely map this wording to a supported calculation. Include the quantity, values and units, or use a structured solver request.','supported_patterns':['unit conversion','force from mass and acceleration','Ohm law current','kinetic energy','molar mass of supported formulas','balancing simple chemical equations','Reynolds number','explicit algebraic equations']}

def _interpret(question: str) -> dict[str,Any]:
    from app.inputs.notation import clean_numeric_text
    q=question.strip().replace('→','->'); q=clean_numeric_text(q) if any(c in q for c in '−×⁻') else q
    m=re.search(_NUM+r'\s*('+_UNITP+r')\s+(?i:to|into)\s+('+_UNITP+')',q)
    if m: return {'solver':'unit_convert','extra_params':{'value':float(m.group(1)),'from_unit':m.group(2).strip(),'to_unit':m.group(3).strip()},'confidence':'template'}
    m=re.search(r'(?:force|newton(?:.?s)? second law).*?mass\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+r'?.*?acceleration\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+'?',q,re.I|re.S)
    if m: return {'solver':'physics_force','extra_params':{'mass':_qty(m.group(1),m.group(2)),'acceleration':_qty(m.group(3),m.group(4))},'confidence':'template'}
    m=re.search(r'(?:ohm.?s law|calculate current).*?(?:voltage|potential difference)\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+r'?.*?resistance\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+'?',q,re.I|re.S)
    if m: return {'solver':'physics_ohms_law','extra_params':{'voltage':_qty(m.group(1),m.group(2)),'resistance':_qty(m.group(3),m.group(4))},'confidence':'template'}
    m=re.search(r'(?:kinetic energy).*?mass\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+r'?.*?(?:velocity|speed)\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+'?',q,re.I|re.S)
    if m: return {'solver':'physics_kinetic_energy','extra_params':{'mass':_qty(m.group(1),m.group(2)),'velocity':_qty(m.group(3),m.group(4))},'confidence':'template'}
    m=re.search(r'(?:molar mass|molecular mass).*?\bof\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z0-9()\u2080-\u2089·.]*)',q,re.I)
    if m: return {'solver':'chemistry_molar_mass','extra_params':{'formula':m.group(1)},'confidence':'template'}
    m=re.search(r'(?:balance|balancing)\s+(?:the\s+)?chemical equation\s*[:\-]?\s*(.+)',q,re.I)
    if m: return {'solver':'chemistry_balance_equation','extra_params':{'equation':m.group(1).strip()},'confidence':'template'}
    m=re.search(r'(?:calculate|find|what is)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:reynolds number).*?density\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+r'?.*?velocity\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+r'?.*?(?:length|diameter)\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+r'?.*?viscosity\s*(?:of\s*)?'+_NUM+r'\s*'+_UNIT+'?',q,re.I|re.S)
    if m: return {'solver':'engineering_reynolds','extra_params':{'density':_qty(m.group(1),m.group(2)),'velocity':_qty(m.group(3),m.group(4)),'characteristic_length':_qty(m.group(5),m.group(6)),'dynamic_viscosity':_qty(m.group(7),m.group(8))},'confidence':'template'}
    m=re.search(r'(?:solve|find the roots? of|solve the equation)\s+(.+)',q,re.I)
    if m and any(c in m.group(1) for c in '=^*+-²'): return {'solver':'solve_equation','expression':m.group(1).strip(),'variable':'x','confidence':'template'}
    return _clarify()


--- STEM-Matrix-main/stemmatrix-unix/backend/app/solvers/core_math.py ---

"""Core deterministic mathematics solvers for StemMatrix.

Inputs are structured requests; expression parsing uses a restricted AST allow-list
and never eval(). These solvers are educational computational tools, not a CAS
security boundary: deploy with process/resource limits for untrusted workloads.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import ast
import math
import operator
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import sympy as sp
from scipy import integrate as scipy_integrate
from scipy import optimize, stats

from app.inputs.notation import FUNCTION_ARITY, NotationError, normalize_expression
from app.inputs.notation import split_equation as _split_equation
from app.schemas.models import SolverRequest, SolverResponse, VerificationResult

_BINOPS = {ast.Add: operator.add, ast.Sub: operator.sub, ast.Mult: operator.mul,
           ast.Div: operator.truediv, ast.Pow: operator.pow, ast.Mod: operator.mod}
_UNOPS = {ast.UAdd: operator.pos, ast.USub: operator.neg}
_FUNCS = {
    "sin": sp.sin, "cos": sp.cos, "tan": sp.tan, "sec": sp.sec, "csc": sp.csc, "cot": sp.cot,
    "asin": sp.asin, "acos": sp.acos, "atan": sp.atan, "atan2": sp.atan2,
    "sinh": sp.sinh, "cosh": sp.cosh, "tanh": sp.tanh, "asinh": sp.asinh, "acosh": sp.acosh, "atanh": sp.atanh,
    "exp": sp.exp, "log": sp.log, "ln": sp.log, "log10": lambda v: sp.log(v, 10), "log2": lambda v: sp.log(v, 2),
    "sqrt": sp.sqrt, "cbrt": sp.cbrt, "abs": sp.Abs,
    "floor": sp.floor, "ceiling": sp.ceiling, "factorial": sp.factorial,
}
MAX_FACTORIAL = 1000
MAX_RESULT_DIGITS = 1000


def normalize_math_expression(source: str, variables: list[str] | None = None) -> str:
    """Normalize common human math notation without evaluating user code.

    Thin wrapper around the shared notation normalizer (``app.inputs.notation``).
    """
    try:
        return normalize_expression(str(source), variables)
    except NotationError as exc:
        raise ValueError(str(exc)) from exc


def split_equation(source: str, variables: list[str] | None = None) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Split an equation into sides; a missing right-hand side means zero."""
    try:
        return _split_equation(source, variables)
    except NotationError as exc:
        raise ValueError(str(exc)) from exc


def safe_expr(source: str, variables: list[str] | None = None) -> sp.Expr:
    source = normalize_math_expression(source, variables)
    if not source or len(source) > 1000:
        raise ValueError("Expression must contain 1–1000 characters")
    allowed_names = set(variables or [])
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(source, mode="eval")
    except (SyntaxError, ValueError, RecursionError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("Expression could not be parsed") from exc
    if sum(1 for _ in ast.walk(tree)) > 250:
        raise ValueError("Expression is too complex")
    symbols = {name: sp.Symbol(name) for name in allowed_names}

    def visit(node):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Expression): return visit(node.body)
        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
            if isinstance(node.value, bool) or not isinstance(node.value, (int, float)):
                raise ValueError("Only numeric constants are allowed")
            if isinstance(node.value, float) and not math.isfinite(node.value):
                raise ValueError("Non-finite constants are not allowed")
            return sp.sympify(str(node.value))
        if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
            if node.id in symbols: return symbols[node.id]
            if node.id == "pi": return sp.pi
            if node.id in ("E", "e"): return sp.E
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported name: {node.id}")
        if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
            op = _BINOPS.get(type(node.op))
            if op is None: raise ValueError("Unsupported arithmetic operator")
            left, right = visit(node.left), visit(node.right)
            if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow) and right.is_number:
                try:
                    exponent = abs(float(right))
                    if left.is_number:
                        # numeric power: bound the size of the result (digits), not just the exponent
                        mag = max(abs(float(sp.N(left))) if left.is_real else 1.0, 2.0)
                        if exponent * math.log10(mag) > MAX_RESULT_DIGITS: raise ValueError("Result of power is too large")
                    elif exponent > 100: raise ValueError("Exponent magnitude is limited to 100")
                except (TypeError, OverflowError): raise ValueError("Unsupported exponent")
            return op(left, right)
        if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp):
            op = _UNOPS.get(type(node.op))
            if op is None: raise ValueError("Unsupported unary operator")
            return op(visit(node.operand))
        if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
            if not isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) or node.keywords:
                raise ValueError("Unsupported function call")
            fn = _FUNCS.get(node.func.id)
            lo, hi = FUNCTION_ARITY.get(node.func.id, (1, 1))
            if fn is None or not lo <= len(node.args) <= hi:
                raise ValueError("Unsupported function or argument count")
            args = [visit(a) for a in node.args]
            if node.func.id == "factorial" and args[0].is_number:
                try:
                    if float(args[0]) > MAX_FACTORIAL: raise ValueError(f"factorial argument is limited to {MAX_FACTORIAL}")
                except TypeError: pass
            return fn(*args)
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported expression element: {type(node).__name__}")
    return visit(tree)


def _jsonable(value: Any):
    if isinstance(value, dict): return {str(k): _jsonable(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)): return [_jsonable(v) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray): return value.tolist()
    if isinstance(value, np.generic): return value.item()
    if isinstance(value, (sp.Basic,)): return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, float) and not math.isfinite(value): return str(value)
    return value


def _point(value: Any):
    """Convert a limit/expansion point to a SymPy value WITHOUT sympify() on strings (which uses eval)."""
    if isinstance(value, bool): raise ValueError("point must be a number, not a boolean")
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        if not math.isfinite(float(value)): raise ValueError("point must be finite")
        return sp.nsimplify(value) if isinstance(value, float) else sp.Integer(value)
    if isinstance(value, str):
        key = value.strip().lower().replace(" ", "")
        if key in ("oo", "+oo", "inf", "+inf", "infinity", "∞"): return sp.oo
        if key in ("-oo", "-inf", "-infinity", "-∞"): return -sp.oo
        return safe_expr(value, [])
    raise ValueError("point must be a number, 'oo', or a constant expression")


def _floats(values, name):
    out = []
    for v in values:
        if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(float(v)):
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must contain only finite numbers")
        out.append(float(v))
    return out


def _trapezoid(y, x):
    return (np.trapezoid if hasattr(np, "trapezoid") else np.trapz)(y, x)


class CoreMathSolver:
    """One registry-backed solver supporting a broad core-mathematics catalogue."""
    name = "core_math"
    operations = {
        # foundations / arithmetic
        "evaluate", "arithmetic", "simplify", "expand", "factor", "fraction_simplify",
        "percentage", "gcd_lcm", "convert_number_base",
        # algebra / equations
        "solve_equation", "solve_system", "polynomial_roots", "partial_fraction",
        "sequence_sum", "binomial_expansion",
        # calculus
        "differentiate", "partial_differentiate", "indefinite_integral", "limit", "taylor_series",
        # linear algebra
        "matrix_add", "matrix_multiply", "matrix_transpose", "matrix_determinant", "matrix_inverse",
        "matrix_rank", "matrix_eigenvalues", "solve_linear_system", "vector_dot", "vector_cross",
        "vector_norm",
        # numerical methods
        "numerical_root", "bisection_root", "newton_root", "numerical_integral", "trapezoid_integral",
        "simpson_integral", "interpolate", "linear_regression",
        # statistics / probability
        "descriptive_stats", "mean", "median", "mode", "variance", "standard_deviation",
        "z_score", "normal_probability", "binomial_probability", "correlation", "percentile",
    }

    def solve(self, request: SolverRequest) -> SolverResponse:
        op = request.solver
        if op not in self.operations:
            return SolverResponse(request_id=request.request_id, status="failed", solver=op,
                                  error=f"Unsupported core mathematics operation: {op}")
        try:
            result, details, exact, numeric, verification = self._execute(op, request)
            return SolverResponse(request_id=request.request_id, status="success", solver=op,
                exact_result=exact, numeric_result=numeric, result=_jsonable(result),
                details=_jsonable(details), verification=VerificationResult(passed=verification[0], message=verification[1]))
        except (ValueError, TypeError, ZeroDivisionError, ArithmeticError, OverflowError, IndexError, KeyError, np.linalg.LinAlgError) as exc:
            return SolverResponse(request_id=request.request_id, status="failed", solver=op, error=str(exc))
        except Exception as exc:
            return SolverResponse(request_id=request.request_id, status="failed", solver=op,
                                  error=f"Calculation failed ({type(exc).__name__})")

    def _execute(self, op: str, r: SolverRequest):
        expr = lambda: safe_expr(r.expression or "", [r.variable] + (r.variables or []) + ([r.target] if r.target else []))
        x = sp.Symbol(r.variable)
        details: dict[str, Any] = {}
        passed, message = True, "Operation completed; input/operation checks passed"
        exact = None; numeric = None
        if op in {"evaluate", "arithmetic"}:
            value = expr()
            subs = {sp.Symbol(k): sp.nsimplify(v) if isinstance(v, float) else v for k, v in (r.extra_params.get("substitutions") or {}).items()}
            if subs: value = value.subs(subs)
            if value.free_symbols: raise ValueError("Expression has unresolved variables")
            value = sp.simplify(value)
            if value.has(sp.zoo) or value.has(sp.nan) or value.has(sp.oo) or value.has(-sp.oo):
                raise ValueError("Expression is undefined or infinite (division by zero, log(0), tan(pi/2) ...)")
            exact = str(value)
            if value.is_number and value.is_real:
                numeric = float(sp.N(value)); result = numeric
            else: result = str(value)
        elif op in {"simplify", "expand", "factor", "fraction_simplify"}:
            value = expr()
            result_expr = {"simplify": sp.simplify, "expand": sp.expand, "factor": sp.factor,
                           "fraction_simplify": sp.cancel}[op](value)
            result = str(result_expr); exact = result
            passed = sp.simplify(value - result_expr) == 0
            message = "Symbolic equivalence check passed" if passed else "Symbolic equivalence check failed"
        elif op == "percentage":
            for key in ("value", "percent"):
                if r.extra_params.get(key) is None: raise ValueError(f"Missing required extra_params.{key}")
            value = float(r.extra_params["value"]); pct = float(r.extra_params["percent"])
            result = value * pct / 100; numeric = float(result); exact = str(result)
            details = {"value": value, "percent": pct, "formula": "value * percent / 100"}
        elif op == "gcd_lcm":
            vals = r.extra_params.get("integers")
            if not isinstance(vals, list) or not vals or any(not isinstance(v, int) or isinstance(v, bool) for v in vals):
                raise ValueError("extra_params.integers must be a non-empty list of integers")
            g = math.gcd(*vals); l = math.lcm(*vals)
            result = {"gcd": g, "lcm": l}; details = {"integers": vals}
        elif op == "convert_number_base":
            val = r.extra_params.get("value"); base = int(r.extra_params.get("base", 10)); source_base = int(r.extra_params.get("source_base", 10))
            if not 2 <= base <= 36 or not 2 <= source_base <= 36: raise ValueError("Bases must be between 2 and 36")
            n = int(str(val), source_base)
            digits = "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ"; out = "0" if n == 0 else ""
            sign = "-" if n < 0 else ""; n = abs(n)
            while n: out = digits[n % base] + out; n //= base
            result = sign + out; details = {"source_base": source_base, "target_base": base}
        elif op == "solve_equation":
            source = r.expression or ""
            lhs_text, rhs_text = split_equation(source)
            symbols = [r.variable] + (r.variables or [])
            lhs = safe_expr(lhs_text, symbols)
            rhs = safe_expr(rhs_text, symbols)
            solutions = sp.solve(sp.Eq(lhs, rhs), x)
            result = [str(s) for s in solutions]; exact = str(result); details = {"equation": source, "variable": r.variable}
            residuals = [sp.simplify(lhs.subs(x, sol) - rhs.subs(x, sol)) for sol in solutions]
            def _zero(res):
                if res == 0: return True
                try: return abs(complex(sp.N(res, 30))) < 1e-9
                except (TypeError, ValueError): return False
            passed = bool(solutions) and all(_zero(res) for res in residuals)
            if not solutions and sp.simplify(lhs - rhs) == 0:
                result = ["all values"]; exact = "identity"; passed = True
                solutions = []; residuals = []
                details["note"] = "The equation holds for every value of the variable (identity)"
            details["symbolic_residuals"] = [str(res) for res in residuals]
            details["solution_count"] = len(solutions)
            if result == ["all values"]: details["solution_count"] = "infinite"
            message = ("Solutions substituted into the equation; residuals are zero" if passed
                       else "No solutions returned or at least one solution failed substitution check")
        elif op == "solve_system":
            if not r.equations or not r.variables: raise ValueError("Provide equations and variables")
            syms = [sp.Symbol(v) for v in r.variables]
            eqs = []
            for equation in r.equations:
                lhs_text, rhs_text = split_equation(equation)
                lhs = safe_expr(lhs_text, r.variables)
                rhs = safe_expr(rhs_text, r.variables)
                eqs.append(sp.Eq(lhs, rhs))
            sols = sp.solve(eqs, syms, dict=True)
            result = [{str(k): str(v) for k, v in s.items()} for s in sols]; exact = str(result)
            details = {"equations": r.equations, "variables": r.variables}
            passed = all(all(sp.simplify(eq.lhs.subs(s) - eq.rhs.subs(s)) == 0 for eq in eqs) for s in sols)
            message = "System solutions substituted into original equations" if passed else "System substitution check failed"
        elif op == "polynomial_roots":
            roots = sp.solve(sp.Eq(expr(), 0), x)
            result = [str(v) for v in roots]; exact = str(result); details = {"variable": r.variable}
            residuals = [sp.simplify(expr().subs(x, root)) for root in roots]
            def _z(res):
                if res == 0: return True
                try: return abs(complex(sp.N(res, 30))) < 1e-9
                except (TypeError, ValueError): return False
            passed = bool(roots) and all(_z(res) for res in residuals)
            details["symbolic_residuals"] = [str(res) for res in residuals]
            details["root_count"] = len(roots)
            message = "Roots substituted into polynomial; residuals are zero" if passed else "No roots returned or root verification failed"
        elif op == "partial_fraction":
            result = str(sp.apart(expr(), x)); exact = result
        elif op == "sequence_sum":
            start = r.extra_params.get("start", 1); stop = r.extra_params.get("stop", 10)
            if any(isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, int) and not (isinstance(v, float) and v.is_integer()) for v in (start, stop)):
                raise ValueError("start and stop must be whole numbers")
            start, stop = int(start), int(stop)
            if start > stop: raise ValueError("start must not exceed stop")
            if stop - start > 100000: raise ValueError("Sequence range is too large")
            val = expr(); result = str(sp.summation(val, (x, start, stop))); exact = result
        elif op == "binomial_expansion":
            if r.degree is None: raise ValueError("Provide degree for binomial expansion")
            result = str(sp.expand(expr() ** r.degree)); exact = result
        elif op == "differentiate":
            value = sp.diff(expr(), x); result = str(value); exact = result
            passed = sp.simplify(sp.diff(expr(), x) - value) == 0; message = "Derivative symbolic check passed"
        elif op == "partial_differentiate":
            var = r.target or r.variable; sym = sp.Symbol(var)
            value = sp.diff(expr(), sym); result = str(value); exact = result; details = {"variable": var}
        elif op == "indefinite_integral":
            value = sp.integrate(expr(), x)
            if value.has(sp.Integral): raise ValueError("No supported symbolic antiderivative found")
            result = str(value); exact = result; details = {"constant_of_integration": "+ C"}
            passed = sp.simplify(sp.diff(value, x) - expr()) == 0
            message = "Differentiated antiderivative matches integrand" if passed else "Antiderivative verification failed"
        elif op == "limit":
            point = r.extra_params.get("point")
            if point is None: raise ValueError("Provide extra_params.point")
            direction = str(r.extra_params.get("direction", "+-"))
            if direction not in ("+", "-", "+-"): raise ValueError("direction must be '+', '-' or '+-'")
            result = str(sp.limit(expr(), x, _point(point), dir=direction)); exact = result
        elif op == "taylor_series":
            point = _point(r.extra_params.get("point", 0)); degree = r.degree or 5
            result = str(sp.series(expr(), x, point, degree + 1).removeO()); exact = result
        elif op.startswith("matrix_") or op in {"solve_linear_system", "vector_dot", "vector_cross", "vector_norm"}:
            result, details, exact, numeric, passed, message = self._linear_algebra(op, r)
        elif op in {"numerical_root", "bisection_root", "newton_root"}:
            result, details, exact, numeric, passed, message = self._root(op, r)
        elif op in {"numerical_integral", "trapezoid_integral", "simpson_integral"}:
            result, details, exact, numeric, passed, message = self._numeric_integral(op, r)
        elif op == "interpolate":
            xs = r.extra_params.get("x_values"); ys = r.extra_params.get("y_values"); at = r.extra_params.get("at")
            if not (isinstance(xs, list) and isinstance(ys, list) and len(xs) == len(ys) >= 2 and at is not None): raise ValueError("Provide matching x_values/y_values lists and at")
            xs = _floats(xs, "x_values"); ys = _floats(ys, "y_values"); at = _floats([at], "at")[0]
            if len(set(xs)) != len(xs): raise ValueError("x_values must be distinct")
            poly = sp.interpolate(list(zip(xs, ys)), x); result = float(poly.subs(x, at)); numeric = result
            exact = str(sp.simplify(poly.subs(x, at))); details = {"interpolating_polynomial": str(sp.expand(poly)), "at": at}
        elif op == "linear_regression":
            xs = np.asarray(r.extra_params.get("x_values", []), dtype=float); ys = np.asarray(r.extra_params.get("y_values", []), dtype=float)
            if len(xs) != len(ys) or len(xs) < 2: raise ValueError("Provide at least two paired x_values and y_values")
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(xs)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(ys)): raise ValueError("x_values and y_values must be finite")
            if np.ptp(xs) == 0: raise ValueError("x_values are all identical; a regression line is undefined")
            slope, intercept = np.polyfit(xs, ys, 1); pred = slope * xs + intercept
            result = {"slope": float(slope), "intercept": float(intercept), "r_squared": float(1 - np.sum((ys-pred)**2) / np.sum((ys-ys.mean())**2)) if np.sum((ys-ys.mean())**2) else 1.0}
            details = {"sample_count": len(xs), "model": "y = slope*x + intercept"}
        elif op in {"descriptive_stats", "mean", "median", "mode", "variance", "standard_deviation", "percentile"}:
            data = self._data(r); arr = np.asarray(data, dtype=float)
            if op == "mean": result = float(np.mean(arr))
            elif op == "median": result = float(np.median(arr))
            elif op == "mode":
                vals, counts = np.unique(arr, return_counts=True); maxc = counts.max(); result = vals[counts == maxc].tolist()
            elif op in ("variance", "standard_deviation"):
                ddof = r.extra_params.get("ddof", 0)
                if isinstance(ddof, bool) or int(ddof) != ddof or ddof < 0: raise ValueError("ddof must be a non-negative whole number")
                if int(ddof) >= arr.size: raise ValueError("ddof must be smaller than the number of observations")
                result = float((np.var if op == "variance" else np.std)(arr, ddof=int(ddof)))
            elif op == "percentile":
                p = float(r.extra_params.get("percentile", 50))
                if not 0 <= p <= 100: raise ValueError("percentile must be in [0, 100]")
                result = float(np.percentile(arr, p))
            else:
                result = {"count": int(arr.size), "mean": float(np.mean(arr)), "median": float(np.median(arr)),
                    "mode": self._modes(arr), "min": float(np.min(arr)), "max": float(np.max(arr)),
                    "range": float(np.ptp(arr)), "population_variance": float(np.var(arr)),
                    "sample_variance": float(np.var(arr, ddof=1)) if arr.size > 1 else None,
                    "population_std_dev": float(np.std(arr)), "sample_std_dev": float(np.std(arr, ddof=1)) if arr.size > 1 else None,
                    "q1": float(np.percentile(arr, 25)), "q3": float(np.percentile(arr, 75))}
            if isinstance(result, (int, float, np.number)): numeric = float(result); exact = str(result)
        elif op == "z_score":
            for key in ("value", "mean", "standard_deviation"):
                if r.extra_params.get(key) is None: raise ValueError(f"Missing required extra_params.{key}")
            value = float(r.extra_params["value"]); mean = float(r.extra_params["mean"]); sd = float(r.extra_params["standard_deviation"])
            if sd <= 0: raise ValueError("standard_deviation must be positive")
            result = (value - mean) / sd; numeric = float(result); exact = str(result)
        elif op == "normal_probability":
            mean = float(r.extra_params.get("mean", 0)); sd = float(r.extra_params.get("standard_deviation", 1))
            if sd <= 0: raise ValueError("standard_deviation must be positive")
            side = r.extra_params.get("side", "less")
            if side == "less":
                if "x" not in r.extra_params: raise ValueError("Provide extra_params.x")
                z = (float(r.extra_params["x"]) - mean) / sd
                result = float(stats.norm.cdf(z))
            elif side == "greater":
                if "x" not in r.extra_params: raise ValueError("Provide extra_params.x")
                z = (float(r.extra_params["x"]) - mean) / sd
                result = float(stats.norm.sf(z))
            elif side == "between":
                lo, hi = r.extra_params.get("lower"), r.extra_params.get("upper")
                if lo is None or hi is None or lo > hi: raise ValueError("For between, provide lower <= upper")
                result = float(stats.norm.cdf((hi-mean)/sd) - stats.norm.cdf((lo-mean)/sd))
            else: raise ValueError("side must be less, greater, or between")
            numeric = result; exact = str(result); details = {"mean": mean, "standard_deviation": sd, "side": side}
        elif op == "binomial_probability":
            raw_n, raw_k = r.extra_params.get("n", -1), r.extra_params.get("k", -1)
            if any(isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float)) or int(v) != v for v in (raw_n, raw_k)):
                raise ValueError("n and k must be whole numbers")
            n = int(raw_n); k = int(raw_k); p = float(r.extra_params.get("p", -1))
            if n < 0 or k < 0 or k > n or not 0 <= p <= 1: raise ValueError("Require n >= 0, 0 <= k <= n, and 0 <= p <= 1")
            result = float(stats.binom.pmf(k, n, p)); numeric = result; exact = str(result)
            details = {"n": n, "k": k, "p": p, "formula": "C(n,k) p^k (1-p)^(n-k)"}
        elif op == "correlation":
            xs = np.asarray(r.extra_params.get("x_values", []), dtype=float); ys = np.asarray(r.extra_params.get("y_values", []), dtype=float)
            if len(xs) != len(ys) or len(xs) < 2: raise ValueError("Provide at least two paired x_values and y_values")
            if np.std(xs) == 0 or np.std(ys) == 0: raise ValueError("Correlation is undefined for constant data")
            result = float(np.corrcoef(xs, ys)[0, 1]); numeric = result; exact = str(result)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Operation not implemented: {op}")
        if numeric is None and isinstance(result, (float, int, np.number)) and math.isfinite(float(result)):
            numeric = float(result)
        return result, details, exact, numeric, (bool(passed), message)

    @staticmethod
    def _data(r):
        data = r.data if r.data is not None else r.values
        if not data: raise ValueError("Provide a non-empty data or values list")
        return data

    @staticmethod
    def _modes(arr):
        vals, counts = np.unique(arr, return_counts=True); maxc = counts.max()
        return vals[counts == maxc].tolist()

    def _linear_algebra(self, op, r):
        passed, message, details, numeric, exact = True, "Linear-algebra consistency checks passed", {}, None, None
        if op == "solve_linear_system":
            if r.matrix is None or r.vector is None: raise ValueError("Provide matrix and vector")
            A = np.asarray(r.matrix, dtype=float); b = np.asarray(r.vector, dtype=float)
            if A.ndim != 2 or A.shape[0] != A.shape[1] or b.shape != (A.shape[0],): raise ValueError("Require square matrix A and matching vector b")
            sol = np.linalg.solve(A, b); residual = A @ sol - b
            result = sol.tolist(); numeric = None; exact = str(result); details = {"residual_norm": float(np.linalg.norm(residual))}
            passed = bool(np.linalg.norm(residual) <= r.tolerance * max(1.0, np.linalg.norm(b)))
        elif op.startswith("matrix_"):
            if r.matrix is None: raise ValueError("Provide matrix")
            A = np.asarray(r.matrix, dtype=float)
            if A.ndim != 2 or not A.size: raise ValueError("Matrix must be non-empty and rectangular")
            if any(len(row) != len(r.matrix[0]) for row in r.matrix): raise ValueError("Matrix rows must have equal lengths")
            if op == "matrix_transpose": result = A.T.tolist()
            elif op == "matrix_determinant":
                if A.shape[0] != A.shape[1]: raise ValueError("Determinant requires a square matrix")
                numeric = float(np.linalg.det(A)); result = numeric
            elif op == "matrix_inverse":
                if A.shape[0] != A.shape[1]: raise ValueError("Inverse requires a square matrix")
                inv = np.linalg.inv(A); result = inv.tolist()
                cond = float(np.linalg.cond(A)); resid = float(np.linalg.norm(A @ inv - np.eye(A.shape[0])))
                details = {"identity_residual_norm": resid, "condition_number": cond}
                if resid > 1e-8 or cond > 1e10:
                    passed = False; message = f"Matrix is numerically ill-conditioned (condition number {cond:.3g}); inverse is unreliable"
            elif op == "matrix_rank": result = int(np.linalg.matrix_rank(A))
            elif op == "matrix_eigenvalues":
                if A.shape[0] != A.shape[1]: raise ValueError("Eigenvalues require a square matrix")
                result = [{"real": float(v.real), "imaginary": float(v.imag)} for v in np.linalg.eigvals(A)]
            elif op == "matrix_add":
                B = np.asarray(r.extra_params.get("matrix_b"), dtype=float)
                if B.shape != A.shape: raise ValueError("Matrices must have matching dimensions")
                result = (A + B).tolist()
            elif op == "matrix_multiply":
                B = np.asarray(r.extra_params.get("matrix_b"), dtype=float)
                if B.ndim != 2 or A.shape[1] != B.shape[0]: raise ValueError("Provide compatible extra_params.matrix_b")
                result = (A @ B).tolist()
            else: raise ValueError("Unsupported matrix operation")
            exact = str(result)
        elif op.startswith("vector_"):
            a = np.asarray(r.values or [], dtype=float); b = np.asarray(r.extra_params.get("vector_b", []), dtype=float)
            if a.size == 0: raise ValueError("Provide values as the first vector")
            if op in {"vector_dot", "vector_cross"} and a.shape != b.shape: raise ValueError("Vectors must have matching dimensions")
            if op == "vector_dot": result = float(np.dot(a, b))
            elif op == "vector_cross":
                if a.size != 3: raise ValueError("Cross product requires 3D vectors")
                result = np.cross(a, b).tolist()
            else: result = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
            exact = str(result); numeric = float(result) if isinstance(result, (float, int, np.number)) else None
        return result, details, exact, numeric, passed, message

    def _root(self, op, r):
        # Newton's method is an open method and only needs an initial guess.
        # Bisection and Brent's method require a bracketing interval.
        if op == "newton_root":
            if r.initial_guess is None:
                raise ValueError("Newton's method requires initial_guess")
        elif r.lower_bound is None or r.upper_bound is None:
            raise ValueError("This root-finding method requires lower_bound and upper_bound")

        if op != "newton_root" and r.lower_bound >= r.upper_bound:
            raise ValueError("lower_bound must be less than upper_bound")

        expr = safe_expr(r.expression or "", [r.variable])
        fn_expr = sp.lambdify(sp.Symbol(r.variable), expr, modules=["numpy"])

        def fn(v):
            y = float(fn_expr(v))
            if not math.isfinite(y):
                raise ValueError("Function returned a non-finite value")
            return y

        if op == "newton_root":
            root = float(optimize.newton(fn, r.initial_guess, tol=r.tolerance, maxiter=r.max_iterations))
            method_used = "newton"
        elif op == "bisection_root":
            root = float(optimize.bisect(fn, r.lower_bound, r.upper_bound, xtol=r.tolerance, maxiter=r.max_iterations))
            method_used = "bisection"
        else:
            method = r.method or "brentq"
            if method == "newton":
                if r.initial_guess is None:
                    raise ValueError("Newton method requires initial_guess")
                root = float(optimize.newton(fn, r.initial_guess, tol=r.tolerance, maxiter=r.max_iterations))
            elif method in {"bisect", "bisection"}:
                root = float(optimize.bisect(fn, r.lower_bound, r.upper_bound, xtol=r.tolerance, maxiter=r.max_iterations))
            elif method in {"brentq", "brent"}:
                root = float(optimize.brentq(fn, r.lower_bound, r.upper_bound, xtol=r.tolerance, maxiter=r.max_iterations))
            else:
                raise ValueError("method must be one of: brentq, bisect, newton")
            method_used = method

        residual = abs(fn(root))
        passed = residual <= max(r.tolerance * 10, 1e-7)
        return root, {"method": method_used, "residual": residual}, str(root), root, passed, "Root residual within tolerance" if passed else "Root residual exceeds tolerance"

    def _numeric_integral(self, op, r):
        if r.lower_bound is None or r.upper_bound is None: raise ValueError("Provide lower_bound and upper_bound")
        if r.lower_bound >= r.upper_bound: raise ValueError("lower_bound must be less than upper_bound")
        expr = safe_expr(r.expression or "", [r.variable]); fn_expr = sp.lambdify(sp.Symbol(r.variable), expr, modules=["numpy"])
        def fn(v):
            out = np.asarray(fn_expr(v), dtype=float)
            if out.ndim == 0: out = np.full(np.shape(v), float(out))   # constant integrand
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(out)): raise ValueError("Integrand returned non-finite values")
            return out
        a, b = float(r.lower_bound), float(r.upper_bound)
        if op == "numerical_integral":
            val, error = scipy_integrate.quad(lambda t: float(fn(t)), a, b, epsabs=r.tolerance, limit=min(r.max_iterations, 1000))
            details = {"method": "adaptive_quadrature", "estimated_absolute_error": float(error)}
        else:
            n = int(r.extra_params.get("n", 1000))
            if n < 2 or n > 1_000_000: raise ValueError("extra_params.n must be between 2 and 1000000")
            if op == "simpson_integral" and n % 2: n += 1
            xs = np.linspace(a, b, n + 1); ys = fn(xs)
            if op == "trapezoid_integral": val = float(_trapezoid(ys, xs)); method = "composite_trapezoid"
            else: val = float(scipy_integrate.simpson(ys, x=xs)); method = "composite_simpson"
            details = {"method": method, "subintervals": n}
        if not math.isfinite(float(val)): raise ValueError("Integral result is not finite")
        return float(val), details, str(float(val)), float(val), True, "Numerical integration completed; error estimate/step method reported"

--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/lib/api.ts ---

import type {
  Book,
  ChatRequest,
  ChatStreamEvent,
  FeedbackRequest,
  Flashcard,
  GradeRequest,
  GradeResult,
  HealthStatus,
  PracticeQuestion,
  PracticeRequest,
  SourcePassage,
} from "@/lib/types";

export async function streamChat(
  body: ChatRequest,
  onEvent: (event: ChatStreamEvent) => void,
  signal: AbortSignal,
): Promise<void> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/chat", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
    signal,
  });
  if (!response.ok || !response.body) {
    throw new Error("The preview server could not answer.");
  }

  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = "";

  while (true) {
    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    const chunks = buffer.split("\n\n");
    buffer = chunks.pop() ?? "";
    for (const chunk of chunks) {
      const event = parseSse(chunk);
      if (event) onEvent(event);
    }
  }
}

function parseSse(chunk: string): ChatStreamEvent | null {
  let name = "";
  let data = "";
  for (const line of chunk.split("\n")) {
    if (line.startsWith("event: ")) name = line.slice(7).trim();
    if (line.startsWith("data: ")) data += line.slice(6);
  }
  if (!data) return null;
  const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(data);
  switch (name) {
    case "meta":
    case "segment":
    case "attempt":
    case "done":
      return { event: name, data: parsed } as ChatStreamEvent;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

export async function listBooks(): Promise<Book[]> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/books");
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not load books.");
  return (await response.json()) as Book[];
}

export async function getSource(chunkId: string): Promise<SourcePassage> {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/sources/${encodeURIComponent(chunkId)}`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("That passage is not in the preview corpus.");
  return (await response.json()) as SourcePassage;
}

export async function createPractice(body: PracticeRequest): Promise<PracticeQuestion[]> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/practice", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not build a practice set.");
  return (await response.json()) as PracticeQuestion[];
}

export async function gradePractice(body: GradeRequest): Promise<GradeResult> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/practice/grade", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not grade that answer.");
  return (await response.json()) as GradeResult;
}

export async function listFlashcards(): Promise<Flashcard[]> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/flashcards");
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not load flashcards.");
  return (await response.json()) as Flashcard[];
}

export async function sendFeedback(body: FeedbackRequest): Promise<void> {
  await fetch("/api/feedback", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

export async function getHealth(): Promise<HealthStatus> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/health");
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Health check failed.");
  return (await response.json()) as HealthStatus;
}

export const SUBJECTS = ["Engineering mathematics", "Physics"] as const;

export type Subject = (typeof SUBJECTS)[number];

export type SourceType = "textbook" | "solver" | "model";

export type ChatMode = "textbook" | "problem" | "out_of_scope";

export type PracticeQuestionType = "mcq" | "msq" | "nat" | "short";

export type Citation = {
  book: string;
  chapter: string;
  section: string;
  page: number;
  chunkId: string;
};

  id: string;
  text: string;
  sourceType: SourceType;
  citations: Citation[];
  displayMath?: string;
};

  attempt: number;
  modelAnswer: string;
  solverAnswer: string;
  match: boolean;
  solver: string;
  params: SolverParams;
  feedback?: string;
};

  role: "user" | "assistant";
  text: string;
};

export type ChatRequest = {
  question: string;
  subject: Subject | null;
  bookId: string | null;

};

  messageId: string;
  mode: ChatMode;
  retrievalNote?: string;
};

  note?: string;
};

  | { event: "meta"; data: ChatMeta }
  | { event: "segment"; data: AnswerSegment }
  | { event: "attempt"; data: SolverAttempt }
  | { event: "done"; data: ChatDone };

export type BookChapter = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
};

export type Book = {
  id: string;

  id: string;

  bookId: string;

};

export type PracticeRequest = {
  bookId: string | null;
  chapterId: string | null;
  types: PracticeQuestionType[];
};

  questionId: string;

};

--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/lib/format.ts ---

import type { ChatMode, SourceType } from "@/lib/types";

export function modeLabel(mode: ChatMode): string {
  switch (mode) {
    case "textbook":
      return "Textbook Q&A";
    case "problem":
      return "Problem solving";
    case "out_of_scope":
      return "Outside the supported subjects";
    default: {
      const neverMode: never = mode;
      return neverMode;
    }
  }
}

export function sourceLabel(source: SourceType): string {
  switch (source) {
    case "textbook":
      return "Textbook";
    case "solver":
      return "Solver";
    case "model":
      return "Model";
    default: {
      const neverSource: never = source;
      return neverSource;
    }
  }
}

export function sourceHint(source: SourceType): string {
  switch (source) {
    case "textbook":
      return "Supported by a retrieved passage";
    case "solver":
      return "Computed and checked";
    case "model":
      return "No textbook or solver behind this part";
    default: {
      const neverSource: never = source;
      return neverSource;
    }
  }
}

export function shortBook(title: string): string {
  return title.replace(/^OpenStax /, "");
}

export function bookById(bookId: string): Book | undefined {
  return books.find((book) => book.id === bookId);
}

export function passageById(chunkId: string): PassageRecord | undefined {
  return passages.find((passage) => passage.chunkId === chunkId);
}

export function citationFor(chunkId: string): Citation {
  const passage = passageById(chunkId);
  const book = passage ? bookById(passage.bookId) : undefined;
  if (!passage || !book) {
    throw new Error(`Missing preview passage ${chunkId}`);
  }
  return {
    book: book.title,
    chapter: passage.chapter,
    section: passage.section,
    page: passage.page,
    chunkId: passage.chunkId,
  };
}

export function toSourcePassage(chunkId: string): SourcePassage | undefined {
  const passage = passageById(chunkId);
  const book = passage ? bookById(passage.bookId) : undefined;
  if (!passage || !book) return undefined;
  return {
    chunkId: passage.chunkId,
    book: book.title,
    bookId: book.id,
    subject: book.subject,
    chapter: passage.chapter,
    section: passage.section,
    page: passage.page,
    text: passage.text,
  };
}

export function citationMatches(
  citation: Citation,
  subject: Subject | null,
  bookId: string | null,
): boolean {
  const passage = passageById(citation.chunkId);
  const book = passage ? bookById(passage.bookId) : undefined;
  if (!passage || !book) return false;
  if (subject && book.subject !== subject) return false;
  if (bookId && book.id !== bookId) return false;
  return true;
}

--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/lib/mock/router.ts ---

import { bookById, citationFor, citationMatches } from "@/lib/mock/corpus";
import { SUBJECTS, type AnswerSegment, type ChatMode, type ChatRequest, type Citation, type HistoryTurn, type SolverAttempt, type Subject } from "@/lib/types";

export type PlannedAnswer = {
  mode: ChatMode;
  retrievalNote?: string;
  attempts: SolverAttempt[];
  segments: AnswerSegment[];
  note?: string;
};

function normalize(value: string): string {
  return value.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
}

function isSubject(value: string): value is Subject {
  return SUBJECTS.some((subject) => subject === value);
}

export function parseChatRequest(value: unknown): ChatRequest | null {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (typeof record.question !== "string") return null;
  const question = record.question.trim();
  if (!question || question.length > 2000) return null;

  const subject = typeof record.subject === "string" && isSubject(record.subject) ? record.subject : null;
  const bookId =
    typeof record.bookId === "string" && bookById(record.bookId) ? record.bookId : null;

  const history: HistoryTurn[] = [];
  if (Array.isArray(record.history)) {
    for (const turn of record.history.slice(-8)) {
      if (!turn || typeof turn !== "object") continue;
      const item = turn as Record<string, unknown>;
      if ((item.role !== "user" && item.role !== "assistant") || typeof item.text !== "string") continue;
      history.push({ role: item.role, text: item.text.slice(0, 1500) });
    }
  }

  return { question, subject, bookId, history };
}

function cite(chunkId: string): Citation {
  return citationFor(chunkId);
}

function buildSegments(
  rows: Array<Omit<AnswerSegment, "id">>,
): AnswerSegment[] {
  return rows.map((row, index) => ({ ...row, id: `seg-${index + 1}` }));
}

function lowConfidence(text: string): PlannedAnswer {
  return {
    mode: "textbook",
    retrievalNote:
      "Nothing relevant was found in the indexed textbooks. This answer is from the model alone.",
    attempts: [],
    segments: buildSegments([
      { text, sourceType: "model", citations: [] },
    ]),
  };
}

function refusal(): PlannedAnswer {
  return {
    mode: "out_of_scope",
    attempts: [],
    segments: buildSegments([
      {
        text: "StemMatrix covers engineering mathematics and physics from the indexed textbooks: calculus, linear algebra, and electricity and magnetism. That question sits outside those subjects.",
        sourceType: "model",
        citations: [],
      },
    ]),
  };
}

function priorUserText(history: HistoryTurn[]): string {
  const turn = [...history].reverse().find((item) => item.role === "user");
  return turn ? normalize(turn.text) : "";
}

  return {
    mode: "textbook",
    attempts: [],
    segments: buildSegments([
      {
        text: simple
          ? "If a magnetic change tries to push more flux through a loop, the induced current makes a field that pushes back. The induced effect fights the change."
          : "The induced emf drives a current whose magnetic field opposes the change in flux that produced it. That opposition is the content of the minus sign in Faraday's law.",
        sourceType: "textbook",
        citations: [cite("phys2-lenz")],
      },
      {
        text: "Faraday's law gives the size of the induced emf. Lenz's law gives the direction.",
        sourceType: "textbook",
        citations: [cite("phys2-faraday"), cite("phys2-lenz")],
      },
    ]),
  };
}

function electricFieldAnswer(simple: boolean): PlannedAnswer {
  return {
    mode: "textbook",
    attempts: [],
    segments: buildSegments([
      {
        text: simple
          ? "An electric field is a map of the push a positive charge would feel at each point. Near a positive source the push is outward, and it gets weaker quickly as you move away."
          : "An electric field is the electric force per unit positive test charge. For a point charge the field is radial, points away from a positive source, and has magnitude $E = k|q|/r^2$.",
        sourceType: "textbook",
        citations: [cite("phys2-efield")],
        displayMath: simple ? undefined : "E = \\dfrac{k|q|}{r^2}",
      },
    ]),
  };
}

      {
        text: "A magnetic field pushes on a moving charge. The force is perpendicular to both the velocity and the field, so a charge at rest feels no magnetic force.",
        sourceType: "textbook",

        displayMath: "\\vec{F} = q\\,\\vec{v}\\times\\vec{B}",
      },
