function exampleFor(topic: string): PlannedAnswer | null {
  if (topic.includes("lenz") || topic.includes("faraday") || topic.includes("induced")) {
    return {
      mode: "textbook",
      attempts: [],
      segments: buildSegments([
        {
          text: "Suppose the flux into the page through a coil increases. Lenz's law says the induced current makes a field out of the page, opposing that increase.",
          sourceType: "textbook",
          citations: [cite("phys2-lenz")],
        },
      ]),
    };
  }
  if (topic.includes("electric field")) {
    return {
      mode: "textbook",
      attempts: [],
      segments: buildSegments([
        {
          text: "Place a positive source at the origin. A positive test charge to its right is pushed further right. Move the test charge twice as far away and the push drops to a quarter.",
          sourceType: "textbook",
          citations: [cite("phys2-efield")],
        },
      ]),
    };
  }
  if (topic.includes("x") && (topic.includes("integrat") || topic.includes("integral"))) {
    return {
      mode: "problem",
      attempts: [
        {
          attempt: 1,
          modelAnswer: "$1/3$",
          solverAnswer: "$1/3$",
          match: true,
          solver: "SymPy",
          params: { type: "definite_integral", expr: "x**2", lower: 0, upper: 1 },
        },
      ],
      segments: buildSegments([
        {
          text: "The same power rule on a smaller interval gives one third.",
          sourceType: "textbook",
          citations: [cite("calc1-power")],
          displayMath: "\\int_0^1 x^2\\,dx = \\frac{1}{3}",
        },
        {
          text: "The solver matches the model.",
          sourceType: "solver",
          citations: [],
        },
      ]),
    };
  }
  return null;
}

function simplerFor(topic: string): PlannedAnswer | null {
  if (topic.includes("lenz")) return lenzAnswer(true);
  if (topic.includes("electric field")) return electricFieldAnswer(true);
  if (topic.includes("integrat") || topic.includes("eigen") || topic.includes("derivative")) {
    return {
      mode: "textbook",
      attempts: [],
      retrievalNote: undefined,
      segments: buildSegments([
        {
          text: "The number in a checked answer comes from the solver. The sentences next to a book name come from a retrieved passage. A sentence with the Model label is the language model's own wording.",
          sourceType: "model",
          citations: [],
        },
      ]),
    };
  }
  return null;
}

const STEM_PATTERN =
  /(integrat|deriv|matrix|eigen|field|flux|force|charge|current|voltage|calculus|physics|equation|formula|theorem|vector|probabilit|limit|differenti|\bsin\b|\bcos\b|magnetic|electric|ohm|energy|momentum|induct|circuit|gauss|ampere|coulomb|lenz|faraday|\blaw\b)/;

function planCore(question: string, history: HistoryTurn[]): PlannedAnswer {
  const q = normalize(question);
  const topic = priorUserText(history);
  const wantsSimpler = /(simpler|more simply|simple terms|easier to follow|explain that)/.test(q);
  const wantsExample = /\bexample\b/.test(q);

  if (wantsSimpler && topic) {
    const simpler = simplerFor(topic);
    if (simpler) return simpler;
  }
  if (wantsExample && topic) {
    const example = exampleFor(topic);
    if (example) return example;
  }

  const sineOfSquare = /sin\s*\(\s*x\s*(\*\*|\^)\s*2\s*\)|sin\s*x\s*(\*\*|\^)\s*2/.test(q);
  if ((q.includes("integrat") || q.includes("integral")) && sineOfSquare) return fresnelExhausted();
  if ((q.includes("integrat") || q.includes("integral")) && /x\s*(\*\*|\^)\s*2/.test(q) && q.includes("0") && q.includes("3")) {
    return integralRepair();
  }
  if (q.includes("eigen")) return eigenvalueRepair();
  if ((q.includes("deriv") || q.includes("d/dx")) && q.includes("sin") && !sineOfSquare) return derivativeMatch();
  if ((q.includes("integrat") || q.includes("integral")) && q.includes("sin") && (q.includes("pi") || q.includes("π"))) {
    return sineIntegral();
  }
  if (q.includes("integration by parts") || q.includes("integrate by parts")) return partsFormula();
  if (q.includes("lenz")) return lenzAnswer(false);
  if (q.includes("faraday")) {
    return {
      mode: "textbook",
      attempts: [],
      segments: buildSegments([
        {
          text: "The induced emf around a closed path equals the negative rate of change of magnetic flux through any surface bounded by that path.",
          sourceType: "textbook",
          citations: [cite("phys2-faraday")],
          displayMath: "\\mathcal{E} = -\\frac{d\\Phi_B}{dt}",
        },
      ]),
    };
  }
  if (q.includes("electric field")) return electricFieldAnswer(false);
  if (q.includes("magnetic field")) return magneticField();
  if ((q.includes("integrat") || q.includes("integral")) && q.includes("sin")) {
    return {
      mode: "textbook",
      attempts: [],
      segments: buildSegments([
        {
          text: "An antiderivative of $\\sin x$ is $-\\cos x$.",
          sourceType: "textbook",
          citations: [cite("calc1-sin")],
          displayMath: "\\int \\sin x\\,dx = -\\cos x + C",
        },
      ]),
    };
  }

  if (STEM_PATTERN.test(q)) {
    return lowConfidence(
      "No indexed passage matched this question. Treat the wording here as the model's own, with no page to open and no solver check.",
    );
  }
  return refusal();
}

function applyFilter(plan: PlannedAnswer, subject: Subject | null, bookId: string | null): PlannedAnswer {
  if ((!subject && !bookId) || plan.mode === "out_of_scope") return plan;

  const segments = plan.segments.flatMap((segment) => {
    if (segment.sourceType !== "textbook") return [segment];
    const citations = segment.citations.filter((citation) => citationMatches(citation, subject, bookId));
    if (citations.length === 0) return [];
    return [{ ...segment, citations }];
  });

  if (segments.some((segment) => segment.sourceType === "textbook")) {
    return { ...plan, segments };
  }

  if (segments.length === 0) {
    const book = bookId ? bookById(bookId) : undefined;
    const scope = book?.shortTitle ?? subject ?? "the current filter";
    return {
      mode: "textbook",
      attempts: [],
      retrievalNote: `Nothing relevant was found in ${scope}.`,
      segments: buildSegments([
        {
          text: "No passage in the current filter supports an answer. Clear the filter, or pick a book that contains the topic.",
          sourceType: "model",
          citations: [],
        },
      ]),
    };
  }

  return {
    ...plan,
    segments,
    retrievalNote: "No passage in the selected books supported the formula context. Solver and model parts are unchanged.",
  };
}

export function planAnswer(request: ChatRequest): PlannedAnswer {
  return applyFilter(planCore(request.question, request.history), request.subject, request.bookId);
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/lib/mock/practice.ts ---

import { citationFor } from "@/lib/mock/corpus";
import type {
  AnswerSegment,
  GradeResult,
  PracticeOption,
  PracticeQuestion,
  PracticeQuestionType,
} from "@/lib/types";

type BankQuestion = {
  id: string;
  type: PracticeQuestionType;
  prompt: string;
  bookId: string;
  book: string;
  chapterId: string;
  chapter: string;
  options?: PracticeOption[];
  answer: string | string[];
  expected: string;
  segments: AnswerSegment[];
};

function segment(
  id: string,
  text: string,
  sourceType: AnswerSegment["sourceType"],
  chunkId?: string,
  displayMath?: string,
): AnswerSegment {
  return {
    id,
    text,
    sourceType,
    citations: chunkId ? [citationFor(chunkId)] : [],
    displayMath,
  };
}

function toPublic(question: BankQuestion): PracticeQuestion {
  return {
    id: question.id,
    type: question.type,
    prompt: question.prompt,
    bookId: question.bookId,
    book: question.book,
    chapter: question.chapter,
    options: question.options,
  };
}

export function selectPracticeQuestions(input: {
  bookId: string | null;
  chapterId: string | null;
  types: PracticeQuestionType[];
}): PracticeQuestion[] {
  const types = input.types.length > 0 ? input.types : (["mcq", "msq", "nat", "short"] as const);
  const filtered = bank.filter((question) => {
    if (!types.includes(question.type)) return false;
    if (input.bookId && question.bookId !== input.bookId) return false;
    if (input.chapterId && question.chapterId !== input.chapterId) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const picked: BankQuestion[] = [];
  for (const type of types) {
    const found = filtered.find((question) => question.type === type);
    if (found) picked.push(found);
  }
  for (const question of filtered) {
    if (picked.length >= 4) break;
    if (!picked.includes(question)) picked.push(question);
  }
  return picked.slice(0, 4).map(toPublic);
}

function numbersMatch(given: string, expected: string): boolean {
  const value = Number(given.trim());
  const target = Number(expected);
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || !Number.isFinite(target)) return false;
  return Math.abs(value - target) < 0.01;
}

function shortMatches(given: string, keywords: string[]): boolean {
  const haystack = given.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "");
  return keywords.every((keyword) => haystack.includes(keyword.replace(/\s+/g, "")));
}

export function gradePractice(questionId: string, answer: string | string[]): GradeResult | null {
  const question = bank.find((item) => item.id === questionId);
  if (!question) return null;

  let correct = false;
  switch (question.type) {
    case "mcq":
      correct = typeof answer === "string" && answer === question.answer;
      break;
    case "nat":
      correct = typeof answer === "string" && typeof question.answer === "string" && numbersMatch(answer, question.answer);
      break;
    case "msq": {
      const given = Array.isArray(answer) ? [...answer].sort() : [];
      const expected = Array.isArray(question.answer) ? [...question.answer].sort() : [];
      correct = given.length === expected.length && given.every((id, index) => id === expected[index]);
      break;
    }
    case "short":
      correct =
        typeof answer === "string" &&
        Array.isArray(question.answer) &&
        shortMatches(answer, question.answer);
      break;
    default: {
      const neverType: never = question.type;
      return neverType;
    }
  }

  return {
    correct,
    expected: question.expected,
    segments: question.segments,
  };
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/source-badge.tsx ---

import { cn } from "cn";
import { sourceHint, sourceLabel } from "@/lib/format";
import type { SourceType } from "@/lib/types";

const tones: Record<SourceType, string> = {
  textbook: "bg-textbook-soft text-textbook",
  solver: "bg-solver-soft text-solver",
  model: "bg-model-soft text-model",
};

export function SourceBadge({ source }: { source: SourceType }) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide uppercase",
        tones[source],
      )}
      title={sourceHint(source)}
    >
      {sourceLabel(source)}
    </span>
  );
}

export function sourceBorder(source: SourceType): string {
  switch (source) {
    case "textbook":
      return "border-textbook";
    case "solver":
      return "border-solver";
    case "model":
      return "border-model";
    default: {
      const neverSource: never = source;
      return neverSource;
    }
  }
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/session-provider.tsx ---

"use client";

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useMemo,
  useSyncExternalStore,
  type Dispatch,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import type { ChatMessage, StudySession, Subject } from "@/lib/types";

const STORAGE_KEY = "stemmatrix.sessions.v1";

type Store = {
  activeId: string;
  sessions: StudySession[];
};

type Action =
  | { type: "new-chat" }
  | { type: "select"; id: string }
  | { type: "remove"; id: string }
  | { type: "set-filter"; subject: Subject | null; bookId: string | null }
  | { type: "add-exchange"; sessionId: string; user: ChatMessage; assistant: ChatMessage }
  | {
      type: "patch";
      sessionId: string;
      messageId: string;
      update: (message: ChatMessage) => ChatMessage;
    };

      segments: [

          ],
        },
      ],
    },
  ],
};

          params: { type: "definite_integral", expr: "x**2", lower: 0, upper: 3 },
        },
      ],
    },

function blankSession(subject: Subject | null, bookId: string | null): StudySession {
  return {
    id: `chat-${crypto.randomUUID()}`,
    title: "New question",
    subject,
    bookId,
    updatedAt: Date.now(),
    messages: [],
  };
}

function initialStore(): Store {
  return {
    activeId: "draft",
    sessions: [
      {
        id: "draft",
        title: "New question",
        subject: null,
        bookId: null,
        updatedAt: 0,
        messages: [],
      },
      sampleLenz,
      sampleIntegral,
    ],
  };
}

function isStore(value: unknown): value is Store {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
  const record = value as { activeId?: unknown; sessions?: unknown };
  return typeof record.activeId === "string" && Array.isArray(record.sessions);
}

function mapSession(store: Store, sessionId: string, update: (session: StudySession) => StudySession): Store {
  return {
    ...store,
    sessions: store.sessions.map((session) => (session.id === sessionId ? update(session) : session)),
  };
}

function reducer(state: Store, action: Action): Store {
  switch (action.type) {
    case "select":
      return state.sessions.some((session) => session.id === action.id)
        ? { ...state, activeId: action.id }
        : state;
    case "new-chat": {
      const current = state.sessions.find((session) => session.id === state.activeId);
      if (current && current.messages.length === 0) return state;
      const session = blankSession(current?.subject ?? null, current?.bookId ?? null);
      return { activeId: session.id, sessions: [session, ...state.sessions] };
    }
    case "remove": {
      const remaining = state.sessions.filter((session) => session.id !== action.id);
      if (remaining.length === 0) {
        const session = blankSession(null, null);
        return { activeId: session.id, sessions: [session] };
      }
      const activeId = state.activeId === action.id ? remaining[0].id : state.activeId;
      return { activeId, sessions: remaining };
    }
    case "set-filter":
      return mapSession(state, state.activeId, (session) => ({
        ...session,
        subject: action.subject,
        bookId: action.bookId,
      }));
    case "add-exchange": {
      const next = mapSession(state, action.sessionId, (session) => {
        const title =
          session.messages.length === 0 ? action.user.text.slice(0, 72) : session.title;
        return {
          ...session,
          title,
          updatedAt: Date.now(),
          messages: [...session.messages, action.user, action.assistant],
        };
      });
      const session = next.sessions.find((item) => item.id === action.sessionId);
      if (!session) return next;
      return {
        ...next,
        sessions: [session, ...next.sessions.filter((item) => item.id !== action.sessionId)],
      };
    }
    case "patch":
      return mapSession(state, action.sessionId, (session) => ({
        ...session,
        messages: session.messages.map((message) =>
          message.id === action.messageId ? action.update(message) : message,
        ),
      }));
    default: {
      const neverAction: never = action;
      return neverAction;
    }
  }
}

type SessionContextValue = {
  store: Store;
  dispatch: Dispatch<Action>;
};

const serverStore = initialStore();
let clientStore: Store | null = null;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function loadClient(): Store {
  if (clientStore) return clientStore;
  clientStore = serverStore;
  const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  if (!raw) return clientStore;
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (isStore(parsed) && parsed.sessions.length > 0) clientStore = parsed;
  } catch {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  }
  return clientStore;
}

function subscribe(listener: () => void) {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

function publish(next: Store) {
  clientStore = next;
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
  for (const listener of listeners) listener();
}

const SessionContext = createContext<SessionContextValue | null>(null);

export function SessionProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const store = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, loadClient, () => serverStore);
  const dispatch = useCallback((action: Action) => {
    publish(reducer(loadClient(), action));
  }, []);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ store, dispatch }), [store, dispatch]);
  return <SessionContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</SessionContext.Provider>;
}

export function useSessions() {
  const context = useContext(SessionContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useSessions must be used inside SessionProvider");
  }
  return context;
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { getHealth } from "@/lib/api";
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { cn } from "cn";

const LINKS = [
  { href: "/ask", label: "Ask" },
  { href: "/practice", label: "Practice" },
  { href: "/flashcards", label: "Flashcards" },
];

export function AppShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [modelNote, setModelNote] = useState("Checking the preview server");

  useEffect(() => {
    void getHealth()
      .then((health) => {
        setModelNote(
          health.model === "not_connected"
            ? "Scripted answers. The on-device model is not connected."
            : "Model connected.",
        );
      })
      .catch(() => setModelNote("The preview server did not respond."));
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-dvh flex-col bg-background">
      <header className="flex h-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b px-4">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-6">
          <Link href="/" className="font-heading text-xl tracking-tight">
            StemMatrix
          </Link>
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-1">
            {LINKS.map((link) => {
              const active = pathname === link.href;
              return (
                <Link
                  key={link.href}
                  href={link.href}
                  className={cn(
                    "rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 text-sm",
                    active ? "bg-muted font-medium text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground",
                  )}
                >
                  {link.label}
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </nav>
        </div>
        <Tooltip>
          <TooltipTrigger className="shrink-0 rounded-full border border-border bg-card px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            Preview
          </TooltipTrigger>
          <TooltipContent>{modelNote}</TooltipContent>
        </Tooltip>
      </header>
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/math-text.tsx ---

"use client";

import katex from "katex";

type Piece =
  | { kind: "text"; value: string }
  | { kind: "math"; value: string; display: boolean };

function parseMath(input: string): Piece[] {
  const pieces: Piece[] = [];
  const pattern = /\$\$([\s\S]+?)\$\$|\$([^$\n]+?)\$/g;
  let last = 0;
  for (const match of input.matchAll(pattern)) {
    const index = match.index ?? 0;
    if (index > last) {
      pieces.push({ kind: "text", value: input.slice(last, index) });
    }
    if (match[1] !== undefined) {
      pieces.push({ kind: "math", value: match[1], display: true });
    } else if (match[2] !== undefined) {
      pieces.push({ kind: "math", value: match[2], display: false });
    }
    last = index + match[0].length;
  }
  if (last < input.length) {
    pieces.push({ kind: "text", value: input.slice(last) });
  }
  return pieces;
}

export function MathText({
  text,
  className,
}: {
  text: string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const pieces = parseMath(text);
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      {pieces.map((piece, index) => {
        if (piece.kind === "text") {
          return (
            <span key={index} className="whitespace-pre-wrap">
              {piece.value}
            </span>
          );
        }
        const html = katex.renderToString(piece.value, {
          throwOnError: false,
          displayMode: piece.display,
        });
        return (
          <span
            key={index}
            className={piece.display ? "my-3 block overflow-x-auto" : "inline"}
            dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/practice/practice-screen.tsx ---

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { MathText } from "@/components/math-text";
import { SourceBadge } from "@/components/source-badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { createPractice, gradePractice, listBooks } from "@/lib/api";
import type {
  Book,
  GradeResult,
  PracticeQuestion,
  PracticeQuestionType,
} from "@/lib/types";
import { cn } from "cn";

const TYPE_OPTIONS: { id: PracticeQuestionType; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "mcq", label: "Multiple choice" },
  { id: "msq", label: "Multiple select" },
  { id: "nat", label: "Numeric" },
  { id: "short", label: "Short answer" },
];

export function PracticeScreen() {
  const [books, setBooks] = useState<Book[]>([]);
  const [bookId, setBookId] = useState<string>("");
  const [chapterId, setChapterId] = useState<string>("");
  const [types, setTypes] = useState<PracticeQuestionType[]>(["mcq", "msq", "nat", "short"]);
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<PracticeQuestion[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState<string | string[]>("");
  const [result, setResult] = useState<GradeResult | null>(null);
  const [score, setScore] = useState(0);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    void listBooks()
      .then(setBooks)
      .catch(() => setError("Could not load books."));
  }, []);

  const book = books.find((item) => item.id === bookId);
  const question = questions[index];
  const finished = questions.length > 0 && index >= questions.length;

  function toggleType(type: PracticeQuestionType, checked: boolean) {
    setTypes((current) => {
      if (checked) return current.includes(type) ? current : [...current, type];
      return current.filter((item) => item !== type);
    });
  }

  async function generate() {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    setResult(null);
    setScore(0);
    setIndex(0);
    setAnswer("");
    try {
      const next = await createPractice({
        bookId: bookId || null,
        chapterId: chapterId || null,
        types,
      });
      setQuestions(next);
      setAnswer(next[0]?.type === "msq" ? [] : "");
      if (next.length === 0) {
        setError("No questions match that book, chapter, and question type.");
      }
    } catch (reason: unknown) {
      setError(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : "Could not build a practice set.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function submit() {
    if (!question || result) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const graded = await gradePractice({ questionId: question.id, answer });
      setResult(graded);
      if (graded.correct) setScore((current) => current + 1);
    } catch (reason: unknown) {
      setError(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : "Could not grade that answer.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function nextQuestion() {
    const upcoming = questions[index + 1];
    setResult(null);
    setAnswer(upcoming?.type === "msq" ? [] : "");
    setIndex((current) => current + 1);
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid h-full min-h-0 lg:grid-cols-[18rem_minmax(0,1fr)]">
      <aside className="min-h-0 overflow-y-auto border-b bg-sidebar p-4 lg:border-r lg:border-b-0">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">Practice</p>
        <h2 className="font-heading mt-1 text-2xl">Build a set</h2>
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm" htmlFor="practice-book">
            Book
            <select
              id="practice-book"
              value={bookId}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setBookId(event.target.value);
                setChapterId("");
              }}
              className="h-9 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card px-2"
            >
              <option value="">All books</option>
              {books.map((item) => (
                <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>
                  {item.shortTitle}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm" htmlFor="practice-chapter">
            Chapter
            <select
              id="practice-chapter"
              value={chapterId}
              onChange={(event) => setChapterId(event.target.value)}
              className="h-9 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card px-2"
              disabled={!book}
            >
              <option value="">All chapters</option>
              {book?.chapters.map((chapter) => (
                <option key={chapter.id} value={chapter.id}>
                  {chapter.title}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <legend className="text-sm">Question types</legend>
            {TYPE_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
              <Label key={option.id} className="font-normal">
                <Checkbox
                  checked={types.includes(option.id)}
                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => toggleType(option.id, checked)}
                />
                {option.label}
              </Label>
            ))}
          </fieldset>
          <Button type="button" onClick={() => void generate()} disabled={busy || types.length === 0}>
            New set
          </Button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <section className="min-h-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6">
        <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-2xl flex-col gap-4">
          {error ? <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
          {!question && !finished ? (
            <div className="pt-8">
              <h2 className="font-heading text-3xl">Check a chapter</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-xl text-base leading-7 text-muted-foreground">
                Multiple choice, multiple select, a typed number, or a short answer. Numeric items are graded against the solver. The others are graded against the textbook.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : null}
          {finished ? (
            <div className="pt-8">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">Set complete</p>
              <h2 className="font-heading mt-2 text-4xl">
                {score} of {questions.length}
              </h2>
              <Button type="button" className="mt-6" onClick={() => void generate()} disabled={busy}>
                Another set
              </Button>
            </div>
          ) : null}
          {question ? (
            <article className="rounded-2xl bg-card p-5 ring-1 ring-border">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
                {index + 1} of {questions.length} · {question.chapter}
              </p>
              <MathText text={question.prompt} className="font-reading mt-3 text-xl leading-8" />
              <div className="mt-5">
                <AnswerControl question={question} answer={answer} onAnswer={setAnswer} locked={result !== null} />
              </div>
              {result ? (
                <div className="mt-5 border-t pt-4">
                  <p className={cn("text-sm font-semibold", result.correct ? "text-solver" : "text-destructive")}>
                    {result.correct ? "Correct" : "Not the expected answer"}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Expected: {result.expected}</p>
                  <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
                    {result.segments.map((segment) => (
                      <div key={segment.id} className="border-l-2 border-border pl-3">
                        <SourceBadge source={segment.sourceType} />
                        <MathText text={segment.text} className="font-reading mt-2 text-base leading-7" />
                        {segment.displayMath ? <MathText text={`$$${segment.displayMath}$$`} /> : null}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                  <Button type="button" className="mt-4" onClick={nextQuestion}>
                    {index + 1 === questions.length ? "See score" : "Next question"}
                  </Button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <Button type="button" className="mt-5" onClick={() => void submit()} disabled={busy || !hasAnswer(question, answer)}>
                  Check
                </Button>
              )}
            </article>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

function hasAnswer(question: PracticeQuestion, answer: string | string[]): boolean {
  switch (question.type) {
    case "mcq":
    case "nat":
    case "short":
      return typeof answer === "string" && answer.trim().length > 0;
    case "msq":
      return Array.isArray(answer) && answer.length > 0;
    default: {
      const neverType: never = question.type;
      return neverType;
    }
  }
}

function AnswerControl({
  question,
  answer,
  onAnswer,
  locked,
}: {
  question: PracticeQuestion;
  answer: string | string[];
  onAnswer: (answer: string | string[]) => void;
  locked: boolean;
}) {
  switch (question.type) {
    case "mcq":
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {question.options?.map((option) => {
            const selected = answer === option.id;
            return (
              <label
                key={option.id}
                className={cn(
                  "flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border px-3 py-3",
                  selected ? "border-foreground bg-muted" : "border-border",
                )}
              >
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name={question.id}
                  value={option.id}
                  checked={selected}
                  disabled={locked}
                  onChange={() => onAnswer(option.id)}
                  className="mt-1"
                />
                <MathText text={option.text} />
              </label>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      );
    case "msq": {
      const selected = Array.isArray(answer) ? answer : [];
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {question.options?.map((option) => {
            const checked = selected.includes(option.id);
            return (
              <Label
                key={option.id}
                className={cn(
                  "items-start rounded-xl border px-3 py-3 font-normal",
                  checked ? "border-foreground bg-muted" : "border-border",
                )}
              >
                <Checkbox
                  checked={checked}
                  disabled={locked}
                  onCheckedChange={(next) => {
                    onAnswer(next ? [...selected, option.id] : selected.filter((id) => id !== option.id));
                  }}
                  className="mt-1"
                />
                <MathText text={option.text} />
              </Label>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      );
    }
    case "nat":
      return (
        <Input
          inputMode="decimal"
          value={typeof answer === "string" ? answer : ""}
          disabled={locked}
          placeholder="Number"
          onChange={(event) => onAnswer(event.target.value)}
          className="max-w-xs bg-background"
        />
      );
    case "short":
      return (
        <Textarea
          value={typeof answer === "string" ? answer : ""}
          disabled={locked}
          placeholder="Write the formula or the statement"
          onChange={(event) => onAnswer(event.target.value)}
          className="bg-background"
        />
      );
    default: {
      const neverType: never = question.type;
      return neverType;
    }
  }
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/chat/answer-message.tsx ---

"use client";

import { ThumbsDownIcon, ThumbsUpIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { MathText } from "@/components/math-text";
import { SourceBadge, sourceBorder } from "@/components/source-badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { modeLabel, shortBook } from "@/lib/format";
import type { AnswerSegment, ChatMessage, Citation } from "@/lib/types";
import { cn } from "cn";

export function AnswerMessage({
  message,
  onCite,
  onFeedback,
  onFollowUp,
}: {
  message: ChatMessage;
  onCite: (citation: Citation) => void;
  onFeedback: (value: "up" | "down") => void;
  onFollowUp: (text: string) => void;
}) {
  if (message.role === "user") {
    return (
      <div className="ml-auto max-w-[36rem] rounded-2xl bg-primary px-4 py-3 text-primary-foreground">
        <MathText text={message.text} className="text-sm leading-6" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (message.error) {
    return <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{message.error}</p>;
  }

  const pendingLabel = statusLabel(message);

  return (
    <article className="max-w-[42rem]" aria-live={message.pending ? "polite" : undefined}>
      {message.mode ? (
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
          {modeLabel(message.mode)}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {message.retrievalNote ? (
        <p className="mt-2 rounded-lg bg-model-soft px-3 py-2 text-sm leading-6 text-model">
          {message.retrievalNote}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {message.mode === "out_of_scope" ? (
        <p className="font-reading mt-3 text-lg leading-8">{message.segments[0]?.text}</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-4">
          {message.segments.map((segment) => (
            <SegmentBlock key={segment.id} segment={segment} onCite={onCite} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {message.note ? (
        <p className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-solver-soft px-3 py-2 text-sm leading-6 text-solver">
          {message.note}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {message.pending ? (
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{pendingLabel}</p>
      ) : null}
      {!message.pending && message.mode && message.mode !== "out_of_scope" ? (
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant={message.feedback === "up" ? "secondary" : "ghost"}
            size="sm"
            aria-pressed={message.feedback === "up"}
            onClick={() => onFeedback("up")}
          >
            <ThumbsUpIcon />
            Helpful
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant={message.feedback === "down" ? "secondary" : "ghost"}
            size="sm"
            aria-pressed={message.feedback === "down"}
            onClick={() => onFeedback("down")}
          >
            <ThumbsDownIcon />
            Not helpful
          </Button>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => onFollowUp("Explain that more simply")}>
            Explain more simply
          </Button>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => onFollowUp("Show an example")}>
            Show an example
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </article>
  );
}

function SegmentBlock({
  segment,
  onCite,
}: {
  segment: AnswerSegment;
  onCite: (citation: Citation) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <section className={cn("border-l-2 pl-4", sourceBorder(segment.sourceType))}>
      <SourceBadge source={segment.sourceType} />
      <MathText text={segment.text} className="font-reading mt-2 text-[1.05rem] leading-7" />
      {segment.displayMath ? (
        <MathText text={`$$${segment.displayMath}$$`} className="font-reading" />
      ) : null}
      {segment.citations.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {segment.citations.map((citation) => (
            <button
              key={`${segment.id}-${citation.chunkId}`}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onCite(citation)}
              className="rounded-full bg-textbook-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-left text-xs font-medium text-textbook hover:opacity-80"
            >
              {shortBook(citation.book)} · p. {citation.page}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function statusLabel(message: ChatMessage): string {
  switch (message.mode) {
    case "problem":
      return message.attempts.length > 0
        ? "Checking the answer against the solver"
        : "Setting up the solver";
    case "textbook":
      return "Looking through the textbooks";
    case "out_of_scope":
      return "Checking the subject";
    case undefined:
      return "Reading the question";
    default: {
      const neverMode: never = message.mode;
      return neverMode;
    }
  }
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/chat/inspect-panel.tsx ---

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { MathText } from "@/components/math-text";
import { getSource } from "@/lib/api";
import { shortBook } from "@/lib/format";
import type { SolverAttempt, SourcePassage } from "@/lib/types";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { cn } from "cn";

export function InspectPanel({
  tab,
  onTab,
  chunkId,
  attempts,
  note,
}: {
  tab: "passage" | "verification";
  onTab: (tab: "passage" | "verification") => void;
  chunkId: string | null;
  attempts: SolverAttempt[];
  note?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <Tabs
      value={tab}
      onValueChange={(value) => {
        if (value === "passage" || value === "verification") onTab(value);
      }}
      className="h-full min-h-0"
    >
      <div className="border-b px-4 pt-4">
        <p className="font-heading text-lg text-foreground">Where this came from</p>
        <TabsList className="my-3 w-full">
          <TabsTrigger value="passage" className="flex-1">
            Passage
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="verification" className="flex-1">
            Verification
          </TabsTrigger>
        </TabsList>
      </div>
      <TabsContent value="passage" className="min-h-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4">
        <PassageView chunkId={chunkId} />
      </TabsContent>
      <TabsContent value="verification" className="min-h-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4">
        <VerificationView attempts={attempts} note={note} />
      </TabsContent>
    </Tabs>
  );
}

function PassageView({ chunkId }: { chunkId: string | null }) {
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<{
    id: string;
    passage: SourcePassage | null;
    error: string | null;
  } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!chunkId) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    void getSource(chunkId)
      .then((next) => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoaded({ id: chunkId, passage: next, error: null });
      })
      .catch((reason: unknown) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoaded({
            id: chunkId,
            passage: null,
            error: reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : "Could not load the passage.",
          });
        }
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [chunkId]);

  if (!chunkId) {
    return (
      <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">
        Click a citation on an answer to open the textbook passage, with its book, chapter, and page.
      </p>
    );
  }

  const current = loaded?.id === chunkId ? loaded : null;
  if (!current) return <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Opening the passage…</p>;
  if (current.error || !current.passage) {
    return <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{current.error ?? "Could not load the passage."}</p>;
  }
  const passage = current.passage;

  return (
    <article>
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-textbook uppercase">
        {passage.subject}
      </p>
      <h3 className="font-heading mt-1 text-xl leading-snug">{shortBook(passage.book)}</h3>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        {passage.chapter} · {passage.section} · p. {passage.page}
      </p>
      <MathText text={passage.text} className="font-reading mt-4 text-base leading-7" />
      <p className="mt-4 font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{passage.chunkId}</p>
    </article>
  );
}

function VerificationView({ attempts, note }: { attempts: SolverAttempt[]; note?: string }) {
  if (attempts.length === 0) {
    return (
      <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">
        Numerical questions show each solver attempt here: what the model said, what the solver computed, and the note sent back when they disagree.
      </p>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ol className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {attempts.map((attempt) => (
        <li key={attempt.attempt} className="rounded-xl bg-card p-3 ring-1 ring-border">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Attempt {attempt.attempt}</p>
            <span
              className={cn(
                "rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide uppercase",
                attempt.match ? "bg-solver-soft text-solver" : "bg-model-soft text-model",
              )}
            >
              {attempt.match ? "Match" : "Mismatch"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{attempt.solver}</p>
          <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
                Model
              </p>
              <MathText text={attempt.modelAnswer} className="text-sm" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
                Solver
              </p>
              <MathText text={attempt.solverAnswer} className="text-sm" />
            </div>
          </div>
          {attempt.feedback ? (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-foreground">{attempt.feedback}</p>
          ) : null}
          <dl className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-3 gap-y-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            {Object.entries(attempt.params).map(([key, value]) => (
              <div key={key} className="contents">
                <dt>{key}</dt>
                <dd className="truncate">{String(value)}</dd>
              </div>
            ))}
          </dl>
        </li>
      ))}
      {note ? <li className="text-sm leading-6 text-foreground">{note}</li> : null}
    </ol>
  );
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/chat/session-list.tsx ---

"use client";

import { PlusIcon, Trash2Icon } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ScrollArea } from "@/components/ui/scroll-area";
import type { StudySession } from "@/lib/types";
import { cn } from "cn";

function stamp(updatedAt: number): string {
  if (updatedAt === 0) return "Draft";
  if (updatedAt < 10) return "Sample";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en", { month: "short", day: "numeric" }).format(updatedAt);
}

export function SessionList({
  sessions,
  activeId,
  onSelect,
  onNew,
  onRemove,
}: {
  sessions: StudySession[];
  activeId: string;
  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
  onNew: () => void;
  onRemove: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-3">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">Chats</p>
        <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onNew}>
          <PlusIcon />
          New
        </Button>
      </div>
      <ScrollArea className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 px-2 pb-4">
          {sessions.map((session) => {
            const active = session.id === activeId;
            return (
              <li key={session.id}>
                <div
                  className={cn(
                    "group flex items-start gap-1 rounded-lg pr-1",
                    active ? "bg-card ring-1 ring-border" : "hover:bg-muted",
                  )}
                >
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onSelect(session.id)}
                    className="min-w-0 flex-1 px-2.5 py-2 text-left"
                  >
                    <span className="block truncate text-sm font-medium">{session.title}</span>
                    <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {stamp(session.updatedAt)}
                      {session.messages.length > 0
                        ? ` · ${session.messages.length} messages`
                        : ""}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    variant="ghost"
                    size="icon-sm"
                    className="mt-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100"
                    aria-label={`Delete ${session.title}`}
                    onClick={() => onRemove(session.id)}
                  >
                    <Trash2Icon />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      </ScrollArea>
    </div>
  );
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/chat/composer.tsx ---

"use client";

import { SendIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import type { Book, Subject } from "@/lib/types";
import { SUBJECTS } from "@/lib/types";

export function Composer({
  value,
  onChange,
  onSubmit,
  disabled,
  subject,
  bookId,
  books,
  onSubject,
  onBook,
}: {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  onSubmit: () => void;
  disabled: boolean;
  subject: Subject | null;
  bookId: string | null;
  books: Book[];
  onSubject: (subject: Subject | null) => void;
  onBook: (bookId: string | null) => void;
}) {
  const visibleBooks = subject ? books.filter((book) => book.subject === subject) : books;

  return (
    <form
      className="border-t bg-background/95 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur-sm"
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        onSubmit();
      }}
    >
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[42rem] flex-col gap-2">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="subject-filter">
            Subject
          </label>
          <select
            id="subject-filter"
            value={subject ?? ""}
            onChange={(event) => {
              const next = event.target.value;
              if (next === "") {
                onSubject(null);
                return;
              }
              const match = SUBJECTS.find((item) => item === next);
              if (match) onSubject(match);
            }}
            className="h-8 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card px-2 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="">All subjects</option>
            {SUBJECTS.map((item) => (
              <option key={item} value={item}>
                {item}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="book-filter">
            Book
          </label>
          <select
            id="book-filter"
            value={bookId ?? ""}
            onChange={(event) => onBook(event.target.value === "" ? null : event.target.value)}
            className="h-8 max-w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-card px-2 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="">All books</option>
            {visibleBooks.map((book) => (
              <option key={book.id} value={book.id}>
                {book.shortTitle}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
          <Textarea
            value={value}
            onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ask about a concept, a formula, or a numerical problem"
            rows={2}
            disabled={disabled}
            className="max-h-40 min-h-16 bg-card"
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey) {
                event.preventDefault();
                onSubmit();
              }
            }}
          />
          <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={disabled || value.trim().length === 0}>
            <SendIcon />
            Ask
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/chat/chat-screen.tsx ---

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { MenuIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { AnswerMessage } from "@/components/chat/answer-message";
import { Composer } from "@/components/chat/composer";
import { InspectPanel } from "@/components/chat/inspect-panel";
import { SessionList } from "@/components/chat/session-list";
import { useSessions } from "@/components/session-provider";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetHeader, SheetTitle } from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { listBooks, sendFeedback, streamChat } from "@/lib/api";
import type { Book, ChatMessage, Citation, HistoryTurn, Subject } from "@/lib/types";

const SUGGESTIONS = [
  "What is Lenz's law?",
  "Integrate x^2 from 0 to 3",
  "Formula for integration by parts",
  "Eigenvalues of [[2, 1], [1, 2]]",
];

function historyOf(messages: ChatMessage[]): HistoryTurn[] {
  return messages
    .filter((message) => !message.pending)
    .map((message) => ({
      role: message.role,
      text:
        message.role === "user"
          ? message.text
          : message.segments.map((segment) => segment.text).join("\n"),
    }));
}

export function ChatScreen() {
  const { store, dispatch } = useSessions();
  const session = store.sessions.find((item) => item.id === store.activeId) ?? store.sessions[0];
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");
  const [books, setBooks] = useState<Book[]>([]);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"passage" | "verification">("passage");
  const [chunkId, setChunkId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [verifyId, setVerifyId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [chatsOpen, setChatsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [inspectOpen, setInspectOpen] = useState(false);
  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    void listBooks()
      .then(setBooks)
      .catch(() => setBooks([]));
  }, []);

  const sessionRef = useRef(session);
  sessionRef.current = session;

  useEffect(() => {
    const current = sessionRef.current;
    if (!current) return;
    const last = [...current.messages]
      .reverse()
      .find((message) => message.role === "assistant" && !message.pending);
    if (!last) return;
    if (last.attempts.length > 0) {
      setVerifyId(last.id);
      setTab("verification");
      return;
    }
    const citation = last.segments.flatMap((segment) => segment.citations)[0];
    if (citation) {
      setChunkId(citation.chunkId);
      setTab("passage");
    }
  }, [session?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: "end" });
  }, [session?.messages]);

  if (!session) return null;

  const pending = session.messages.some((message) => message.pending);
  const verified = session.messages.find((message) => message.id === verifyId);

  function openMobileInspect() {
    if (window.matchMedia("(max-width: 1023px)").matches) setInspectOpen(true);
  }

  function chooseCitation(citation: Citation) {
    setChunkId(citation.chunkId);
    setTab("passage");
    openMobileInspect();
  }

  async function ask(question: string) {
    const trimmed = question.trim();
    if (!trimmed || pending) return;
    const sessionId = session.id;
    const user: ChatMessage = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      role: "user",
      text: trimmed,
      segments: [],
      attempts: [],
    };
    const assistant: ChatMessage = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      role: "assistant",
      text: "",
      segments: [],
      attempts: [],
      pending: true,
    };
    dispatch({ type: "add-exchange", sessionId, user, assistant });
    setDraft("");
    abortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortRef.current = controller;

    try {
      await streamChat(
        {
          question: trimmed,
          subject: session.subject,
          bookId: session.bookId,
          history: historyOf(session.messages),
        },
        (event) => {
          switch (event.event) {
            case "meta":
              dispatch({
                type: "patch",
                sessionId,
                messageId: assistant.id,
                update: (message) => ({
                  ...message,
                  mode: event.data.mode,
                  retrievalNote: event.data.retrievalNote,
                }),
              });
              break;
            case "segment":
              dispatch({
                type: "patch",
                sessionId,
                messageId: assistant.id,
                update: (message) => ({
                  ...message,
                  segments: [...message.segments, event.data],
                }),
              });
              break;
            case "attempt":
              dispatch({
                type: "patch",
                sessionId,
                messageId: assistant.id,
                update: (message) => ({
                  ...message,
                  attempts: [...message.attempts, event.data],
                }),
              });
              setVerifyId(assistant.id);
              setTab("verification");
              openMobileInspect();
              break;
            case "done":
              dispatch({
                type: "patch",
                sessionId,
                messageId: assistant.id,
                update: (message) => ({
                  ...message,
                  note: event.data.note,
                  pending: false,
                }),
              });
              break;
            default: {
              const neverEvent: never = event;
              return neverEvent;
            }
          }
        },
        controller.signal,
      );
    } catch (reason: unknown) {
      if (reason instanceof DOMException && reason.name === "AbortError") return;
      dispatch({
        type: "patch",
        sessionId,
        messageId: assistant.id,
        update: (message) => ({
          ...message,
          pending: false,
          error: reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : "The preview server could not answer.",
        }),
      });
    }
  }

  function setSubject(subject: Subject | null) {
    const bookStillVisible =
      session.bookId === null ||
      books.some((book) => book.id === session.bookId && (subject === null || book.subject === subject));
    dispatch({
      type: "set-filter",
      subject,
      bookId: bookStillVisible ? session.bookId : null,
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid h-full min-h-0 lg:grid-cols-[16.5rem_minmax(0,1fr)_22rem]">
      <aside className="hidden min-h-0 border-r bg-sidebar lg:block">
        <SessionList
          sessions={store.sessions}
          activeId={session.id}
          onSelect={(id) => dispatch({ type: "select", id })}
          onNew={() => dispatch({ type: "new-chat" })}
          onRemove={(id) => dispatch({ type: "remove", id })}
        />
      </aside>

      <section className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-col">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b px-3 py-2 lg:hidden">
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => setChatsOpen(true)}>
            <MenuIcon />
            Chats
          </Button>
          <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setInspectOpen(true)}>
            Sources
          </Button>
        </div>
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[46rem] flex-col gap-6 px-4 py-6">
            {session.messages.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState onAsk={(question) => void ask(question)} />
            ) : (
              session.messages.map((message) => (
                <AnswerMessage
                  key={message.id}
                  message={message}
                  onCite={chooseCitation}
                  onFeedback={(value) => {
                    const next = message.feedback === value ? undefined : value;
                    dispatch({
                      type: "patch",
                      sessionId: session.id,
                      messageId: message.id,
                      update: (current) => ({ ...current, feedback: next }),
                    });
                    if (next) void sendFeedback({ messageId: message.id, value: next });
                  }}
                  onFollowUp={(text) => void ask(text)}
                />
              ))
            )}
            <div ref={bottomRef} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Composer
          value={draft}
          onChange={setDraft}
          onSubmit={() => void ask(draft)}
          disabled={pending}
          subject={session.subject}
          bookId={session.bookId}
          books={books}
          onSubject={setSubject}
          onBook={(bookId) => dispatch({ type: "set-filter", subject: session.subject, bookId })}
        />
      </section>

      <aside className="hidden min-h-0 border-l bg-sidebar lg:block">
        <InspectPanel
          tab={tab}
          onTab={setTab}
          chunkId={chunkId}
          attempts={verified?.attempts ?? []}
          note={verified?.note}
        />
      </aside>

      <Sheet open={chatsOpen} onOpenChange={setChatsOpen}>
        <SheetContent side="left" className="w-80 data-[side=left]:w-80">
          <SheetHeader>
            <SheetTitle>Chats</SheetTitle>
          </SheetHeader>
          <SessionList
            sessions={store.sessions}
            activeId={session.id}
            onSelect={(id) => {
              dispatch({ type: "select", id });
              setChatsOpen(false);
            }}
            onNew={() => {
              dispatch({ type: "new-chat" });
              setChatsOpen(false);
            }}
            onRemove={(id) => dispatch({ type: "remove", id })}
          />
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>

      <Sheet open={inspectOpen} onOpenChange={setInspectOpen}>
        <SheetContent side="right" className="w-full sm:max-w-md data-[side=right]:w-full">
          <SheetHeader className="sr-only">
            <SheetTitle>Sources and verification</SheetTitle>
          </SheetHeader>
          <InspectPanel
            tab={tab}
            onTab={setTab}
            chunkId={chunkId}
            attempts={verified?.attempts ?? []}
            note={verified?.note}
          />
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}

function EmptyState({ onAsk }: { onAsk: (question: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[40rem] flex-col gap-8 pt-8">
      <div>
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
          Textbook, solver, or model
        </p>
        <h2 className="font-heading mt-2 text-4xl leading-tight text-foreground">
          What do you want to work through?
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-3 max-w-xl text-base leading-7 text-muted-foreground">
          Concept questions come back with a book, chapter, and page. Numerical problems come back with a solver check. If a sentence has no source, it is marked Model.
        </p>
      </div>
      <ul className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
        {SUGGESTIONS.map((suggestion) => (
          <li key={suggestion}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onAsk(suggestion)}
              className="h-full w-full rounded-xl bg-card px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm leading-6 ring-1 ring-border hover:bg-muted"
            >
              {suggestion}
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">
        This preview uses scripted answers so the interface can be reviewed before the FastAPI model server is connected. Two sample chats are already in the list.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/flashcards/flashcard-screen.tsx ---

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { MathText } from "@/components/math-text";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { listFlashcards, getSource } from "@/lib/api";
import { shortBook } from "@/lib/format";
import type { Flashcard, SourcePassage, Subject } from "@/lib/types";
import { SUBJECTS } from "@/lib/types";
import { cn } from "cn";

export function FlashcardScreen() {
  const [cards, setCards] = useState<Flashcard[]>([]);
  const [subject, setSubject] = useState<Subject | "all">("all");
  const [order, setOrder] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState(false);
  const [known, setKnown] = useState(0);
  const [loadedPassage, setLoadedPassage] = useState<{
    chunkId: string;
    passage: SourcePassage;
  } | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    void listFlashcards()
      .then((next) => {
        setCards(next);
        setOrder(next.map((card) => card.id));
      })
      .catch(() => setError("Could not load flashcards."));
  }, []);

  const visible = order
    .map((id) => cards.find((card) => card.id === id))
    .filter((card): card is Flashcard => {
      if (!card) return false;
      return subject === "all" || card.subject === subject;
    });
  const card = visible[index] ?? null;
  const passage =
    flipped && card && loadedPassage?.chunkId === card.citation.chunkId ? loadedPassage.passage : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!card || !flipped) return;
    const chunkId = card.citation.chunkId;
    let cancelled = false;
    void getSource(chunkId)
      .then((next) => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoadedPassage({ chunkId, passage: next });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoadedPassage(null);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [card, flipped]);

  function chooseSubject(next: Subject | "all") {
    setSubject(next);
    setIndex(0);
    setFlipped(false);
    setKnown(0);
    setLoadedPassage(null);
  }

  function advance(remembered: boolean) {
    if (!card) return;
    if (remembered) setKnown((current) => current + 1);
    setFlipped(false);
    setLoadedPassage(null);
    setOrder((current) => {
      if (remembered) return current.filter((id) => id !== card.id);
      const without = current.filter((id) => id !== card.id);
      return [...without, card.id];
    });
    setIndex((current) => {
      const nextLength = remembered ? visible.length - 1 : visible.length;
      if (nextLength <= 0) return 0;
      return current >= nextLength ? 0 : current;
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-full min-h-0 w-full max-w-3xl flex-col px-4 py-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">Flashcards</p>
          <h2 className="font-heading text-3xl">Formulas and definitions</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <FilterButton active={subject === "all"} onClick={() => chooseSubject("all")}>
            All
          </FilterButton>
          {SUBJECTS.map((item) => (
            <FilterButton key={item} active={subject === item} onClick={() => chooseSubject(item)}>
              {item === "Engineering mathematics" ? "Mathematics" : item}
            </FilterButton>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {error ? <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}

      {card ? (
        <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col justify-center">
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {index + 1} of {visible.length}
            {known > 0 ? ` · ${known} known` : ""}
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFlipped((current) => !current)}
            className="mt-3 min-h-72 rounded-3xl bg-card px-8 py-10 text-left ring-1 ring-border"
          >
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
              {flipped ? "Meaning" : card.chapter}
            </p>
            {flipped ? (
              <MathText text={card.back} className="font-reading mt-4 text-2xl leading-9" />
            ) : (
              <>
                <p className="font-heading mt-4 text-3xl">{card.front}</p>
                {card.frontMath ? <MathText text={`$$${card.frontMath}$$`} /> : null}
              </>
            )}
            <p className="mt-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{flipped ? "Click to show the formula" : "Click to flip"}</p>
          </button>
          {flipped && passage ? (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-textbook">
              {shortBook(passage.book)} · {passage.section} · p. {passage.page}
            </p>
          ) : null}
          <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2">
            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => advance(false)}>
              Again
            </Button>
            <Button type="button" onClick={() => advance(true)}>
              Know
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col justify-center">
          <h3 className="font-heading text-3xl">{cards.length === 0 ? "Loading cards" : "Deck clear"}</h3>
          <p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">
            {cards.length === 0
              ? "Fetching formulas from the preview corpus."
              : "Every card in this filter is marked known."}
          </p>
          {cards.length > 0 ? (
            <Button
              type="button"
              className="mt-4 w-fit"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={() => {
                setOrder(cards.map((item) => item.id));
                setKnown(0);
                setIndex(0);
              }}
            >
              Reset deck
            </Button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function FilterButton({
  active,
  onClick,
  children,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
  children: string;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        "rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm",
        active ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "bg-muted text-foreground",
      )}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}


--- STEM-Matrix-main/frontend/components/landing/home-page.tsx ---

import Link from "next/link";
import { MathText } from "@/components/math-text";
import { SourceBadge, sourceBorder } from "@/components/source-badge";
import { buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { cn } from "cn";

const labels = [
  {
    source: "textbook" as const,
    title: "Tied to a page",
    text: "A Textbook sentence is backed by a retrieved passage. Open it and you get the book, chapter, section, and page.",
  },
  {
    source: "solver" as const,
    title: "Computed, then checked",
    text: "A Solver value comes from SymPy, NumPy, or SciPy. If the model disagrees, the mismatch is shown and sent back.",
  },
  {
    source: "model" as const,
    title: "Said out loud",
    text: "A Model sentence has no passage and no solver behind it. The label stays on the line instead of a made-up citation.",
  },
];

export function HomePage() {
  return (
    <div className="h-full overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-5xl flex-col px-5 py-12 sm:px-8 sm:py-16">
        <section className="grid items-start gap-12 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.05fr)_minmax(0,0.95fr)]">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
              IUST · Department of CSE · 2026
            </p>
            <h1 className="font-heading mt-4 max-w-xl text-5xl leading-[1.05] text-foreground sm:text-6xl">
              A STEM answer you can check.
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-5 max-w-xl text-lg leading-8 text-muted-foreground">
              Ask a concept and the reply cites a textbook page. Ask for a number and a solver computes it. Every part of the answer is labelled Textbook, Solver, or Model.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              <Link href="/ask" className={cn(buttonVariants({ size: "lg" }))}>
                Ask a question
              </Link>
              <Link href="/practice" className={cn(buttonVariants({ size: "lg", variant: "outline" }))}>
                Practice a chapter
              </Link>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-4 max-w-md text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">
              This build uses preview answers. The on-device model is not connected yet.
            </p>
          </div>

          <article className="rounded-2xl bg-card p-5 ring-1 ring-border sm:p-6">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
              Problem solving
            </p>
            <p className="font-heading mt-2 text-2xl">Integrate x^2 from 0 to 3</p>
            <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-4">
              <SampleSegment source="textbook" cite="Calculus Volume 1 · p. 342">
                The power rule says the antiderivative of $x^2$ is $x^3/3$.
              </SampleSegment>
              <SampleSegment source="solver" math="\int_0^3 x^2\,dx = 9">
                Evaluated from 0 to 3, the definite integral is 9.
              </SampleSegment>
              <SampleSegment source="model">
                The first draft said 27. The solver sent that error back, and the second draft matched.
              </SampleSegment>
            </div>
          </article>
        </section>

        <section className="mt-16 border-t border-border pt-10">
          <h2 className="font-heading text-3xl">The label is the point</h2>
          <ul className="mt-6 divide-y divide-border">
            {labels.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.source} className="grid gap-3 py-5 sm:grid-cols-[9rem_minmax(0,1fr)] sm:items-baseline">
                <SourceBadge source={item.source} />
                <div>
                  <p className="font-medium">{item.title}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">{item.text}</p>
                </div>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>

        <section className="mt-4 border-t border-border">
          <StudyLink
            href="/ask"
            kicker="Textbook Q&A"
            title="What is Lenz's law?"
            text="Retrieval pulls a passage from University Physics. The answer names the book, the section, and the page."
          />
          <StudyLink
            href="/practice"
            kicker="Practice"
            title="A set from one chapter"
            text="Multiple choice, multiple select, a typed number, or a short answer. Numbers are graded against the solver."
          />
          <StudyLink
            href="/flashcards"
            kicker="Flashcards"
            title="Formulas, with the page on the back"
            text="Flip a card for the meaning and the citation it was taken from."
          />
        </section>

        <footer className="mt-12 text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">
          StemMatrix is a B.Tech major project at the Islamic University of Science and Technology, Awantipora. The preview corpus is original summary text filed under openly licensed books, including OpenStax Calculus and University Physics.
        </footer>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SampleSegment({
  source,
  cite,
  math,
  children,
}: {
  source: "textbook" | "solver" | "model";
  cite?: string;
  math?: string;
  children: string;
}) {
  return (
    <section className={cn("border-l-2 pl-4", sourceBorder(source))}>
      <SourceBadge source={source} />
      <MathText text={children} className="font-reading mt-2 text-base leading-7" />
      {math ? <MathText text={`$$${math}$$`} /> : null}
      {cite ? <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-medium text-textbook">{cite}</p> : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function StudyLink({
  href,
  kicker,
  title,
  text,
}: {
  href: string;
  kicker: string;
  title: string;
  text: string;
}) {
  return (
    <Link href={href} className="group grid gap-2 border-b border-border py-6 sm:grid-cols-[9rem_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] sm:items-baseline">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">{kicker}</p>
      <div>
        <p className="font-heading text-2xl group-hover:underline">{title}</p>
        <p className="mt-1 max-w-xl text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">{text}</p>
      </div>
      <p className="text-sm font-medium">Open</p>
    </Link>
  );
}

"use client"

import { Tabs as TabsPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/tabs"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "cn"

function Tabs({
  className,
  orientation = "horizontal",
  ...props
}: TabsPrimitive.Root.Props) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.Root
      data-slot="tabs"
      data-orientation={orientation}
      className={cn(
        "group/tabs flex gap-2 data-horizontal:flex-col",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

const tabsListVariants = cva(
  "group/tabs-list inline-flex w-fit items-center justify-center rounded-lg p-[3px] text-muted-foreground group-data-horizontal/tabs:h-8 group-data-vertical/tabs:h-fit group-data-vertical/tabs:flex-col data-[variant=line]:rounded-none",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-muted",
        line: "gap-1 bg-transparent",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function TabsList({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  ...props
}: TabsPrimitive.List.Props & VariantProps<typeof tabsListVariants>) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.List
      data-slot="tabs-list"
      data-variant={variant}
      className={cn(tabsListVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function TabsTrigger({ className, ...props }: TabsPrimitive.Tab.Props) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.Tab
      data-slot="tabs-trigger"
      className={cn(
        "relative inline-flex h-[calc(100%-1px)] flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-transparent px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-foreground/60 transition-all group-data-vertical/tabs:w-full group-data-vertical/tabs:justify-start hover:text-foreground focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-1 focus-visible:outline-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1 aria-disabled:pointer-events-none aria-disabled:opacity-50 dark:text-muted-foreground dark:hover:text-foreground group-data-[variant=default]/tabs-list:data-active:shadow-sm group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-active:shadow-none [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
        "group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:bg-transparent group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-active:bg-transparent dark:group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-active:border-transparent dark:group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-active:bg-transparent",
        "data-active:bg-background data-active:text-foreground dark:data-active:border-input dark:data-active:bg-input/30 dark:data-active:text-foreground",
        "after:absolute after:bg-foreground after:opacity-0 after:transition-opacity group-data-horizontal/tabs:after:inset-x-0 group-data-horizontal/tabs:after:bottom-[-5px] group-data-horizontal/tabs:after:h-0.5 group-data-vertical/tabs:after:inset-y-0 group-data-vertical/tabs:after:-right-1 group-data-vertical/tabs:after:w-0.5 group-data-[variant=line]/tabs-list:data-active:after:opacity-100",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function TabsContent({ className, ...props }: TabsPrimitive.Panel.Props) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.Panel
      data-slot="tabs-content"
